# I am building a multi-year Roth Conversion & Retirement Tax Modeling tool for Track 1 of the hackathon. 

Please write a core JavaScript logic module (`simulationEngine.js`) that takes the following inputs:
- Initial Traditional IRA Balance
- Initial Taxable Brokerage Balance (used to pay conversion taxes)
- Annual Roth Conversion Amount
- Number of conversion years (the gap period before RMDs)
- Expected annual investment growth rate
- Filing status (Single / Married Filing Jointly)

The engine must calculate and return a year-by-year array showing:
1. Ending balances for Traditional IRA, Roth IRA, and Taxable Brokerage accounts.
2. Estimated annual federal income tax generated by the conversion.
3. The resulting MAGI and whether it crosses the 2026 Medicare IRMAA thresholds ($109k single / $218k joint), including the resulting Part B/D surcharge tier.
4. The projected RMD amount once the user reaches age 73, compared against a "do-nothing" baseline.

Please provide the clean code and a sample test script to verify the calculations.

## Option A — Test the calculation engine immediately (no install needed)

The `simulationEngine.js` uses ES module `export` syntax. The fastest way to run it is with **Node.js 18+** directly.

**Step 1 — Create a quick test file:**

```js
// test.mjs  ← create this file in the project root
import { runSimulation, calcFederalTax, calcIRMAA, calcRMD } from "./simulationEngine.js";

const result = runSimulation({
  traditionalIRABalance:   500000,
  taxableBrokerageBalance: 100000,
  annualRothConversion:    50000,
  conversionYears:         10,
  growthRate:              0.07,
  filingStatus:            "mfj",
  currentAge:              63,
  otherOrdinaryIncome:     30000,
});

console.log("=== SUMMARY ===");
console.table(result.summary);

console.log("\n=== YEAR-BY-YEAR ===");
result.years.forEach(y => {
  console.log(
    `Year ${y.year} (Age ${y.age}): ` +
    `Converted $${y.conversionAmount.toLocaleString()} | ` +
    `Trad IRA $${y.endingTraditionalIRA.toLocaleString()} | ` +
    `Roth $${y.endingRothIRA.toLocaleString()} | ` +
    `Tax $${y.conversionTax.toLocaleString()} | ` +
    `MAGI $${y.magi.toLocaleString()} → IRMAA ${y.irmaa.label}`
  );
});
```

**Step 2 — Run it:**

```powershell
node test.mjs
```

## How to run — step by step

### 1. Install Node.js

Download and install from **[nodejs.org](https://nodejs.org)** — pick the **LTS** version (v20 or v22).

After installing, **close and reopen your terminal**, then confirm it works:

```powershell
node --version   # should print v20.x.x or similar
npm --version
```

---

### 2. Install project dependencies

Open a terminal in the project folder (`c:\cora\roth-optimizer-hackathon`) and run:

```powershell
npm install
```

This downloads React, Vite, and Recharts from `package.json`.

---

### 3. Run the engine test (Option A)

```powershell
node test.mjs
```

This runs [`test.mjs`](test.mjs) — 5 test suites covering:
- Full 10-year MFJ year-by-year simulation
- Federal tax spot checks
- IRMAA tier boundary checks
- RMD calculations at ages 72, 73, 75
- Single filer IRMAA breach scenario

---

### 4. Start the React dashboard (once UI files are written)

```powershell
npm run dev
```

Then open **http://localhost:5173** in your browser.

how to install node?

### 🤖 Assistant

Here are the two easiest ways to install Node.js on Windows:

---

## Option 1 — Official installer (simplest)

1. Go to **[https://nodejs.org](https://nodejs.org)**
2. Click the **"LTS"** download button (the left one — Long Term Support, most stable)
3. Run the downloaded `.msi` file and click through the installer (all defaults are fine)
4. **Restart your terminal** (or VS Code) after it finishes

Verify it worked:
```powershell
node --version
npm --version
```

---

## Option 2 — winget (Windows package manager, one command)

If you have Windows 10/11 with `winget` available, just run this in any terminal:

```powershell
winget install OpenJS.NodeJS.LTS
```

Then restart your terminal and verify:
```powershell
node --version
npm --version
```

---

## Which LTS version to pick?

| Version | Status | Recommended for |
|---------|--------|-----------------|
| **v22 LTS** | Current LTS | ✅ New projects (pick this) |
| v20 LTS | Maintenance LTS | Works fine too |

---

Once Node is installed and `node --version` prints a version number, come back here and run:

```powershell
node test.mjs
```

That will execute all 5 test suites against [`simulationEngine.js`](simulationEngine.js) immediately — no `npm install` needed for the test since it has zero dependencies.

> 🔧 **write_file** — import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  LineChart, Line, BarChart, Bar,
  XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, Legend, ResponsiveContainer,
} from "recharts";
import { runSimulation } from "../simulationEngine.js";

// ─── Formatting helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const fmt$ = (n) =>
  n == null ? "—" : "$" + Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
const fmtPct = (n) =>
  n == null ? "—" : (n * 100).toFixed(1) + "%";

// ─── Reusable UI primitives ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
function Card({ title, children, accent }) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      background: "#fff",
      border: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
      borderRadius: 10,
      padding: "20px 24px",
      borderTop: accent ? `3px solid ${accent}` : undefined,
    }}>
      {title && (
        <h3 style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.05em", marginBottom: 14 }}>
          {title}
        </h3>
      )}
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function StatBox({ label, value, sub, color }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 700, color: color ?? "#1f2328" }}>{value}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginTop: 2 }}>{label}</div>
      {sub && <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#8b949e", marginTop: 1 }}>{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function InputField({ label, id, value, onChange, type = "number", min, max, step, note, prefix, suffix }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
      <label htmlFor={id} style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1f2328" }}>
        {label}
      </label>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
        {prefix && <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "#57606a" }}>{prefix}</span>}
        <input
          id={id}
          type={type}
          value={value}
          min={min}
          max={max}
          step={step}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(type === "number" ? parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0 : e.target.value)}
          style={{
            flex: 1,
            padding: "7px 10px",
            border: "1px solid #d0d7de",
            borderRadius: 6,
            fontSize: 14,
            outline: "none",
            background: "#fff",
          }}
        />
        {suffix && <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "#57606a" }}>{suffix}</span>}
      </div>
      {note && <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#8b949e" }}>{note}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

// ─── IRMAA badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function IRMAAbadge({ label, crosses }) {
  const bg = crosses ? "#fff3cd" : "#d1fae5";
  const color = crosses ? "#92400e" : "#065f46";
  return (
    <span style={{ background: bg, color, borderRadius: 4, padding: "2px 7px", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600 }}>
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}

// ─── Main App ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export default function App() {
  // Inputs
  const [tradIRA,       setTradIRA]       = useState(500000);
  const [brokerage,     setBrokerage]     = useState(100000);
  const [conversion,    setConversion]    = useState(50000);
  const [years,         setYears]         = useState(10);
  const [growth,        setGrowth]        = useState(7);
  const [filing,        setFiling]        = useState("mfj");
  const [age,           setAge]           = useState(63);
  const [otherIncome,   setOtherIncome]   = useState(30000);

  // Run simulation
  const { result, error } = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      const r = runSimulation({
        traditionalIRABalance:   tradIRA,
        taxableBrokerageBalance: brokerage,
        annualRothConversion:    conversion,
        conversionYears:         Math.round(years),
        growthRate:              growth / 100,
        filingStatus:            filing,
        currentAge:              Math.round(age),
        otherOrdinaryIncome:     otherIncome,
      });
      return { result: r, error: null };
    } catch (e) {
      return { result: null, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [tradIRA, brokerage, conversion, years, growth, filing, age, otherIncome]);

  const s = result?.summary;

  // Chart data
  const chartData = result?.years.map((y) => ({
    name: `Yr ${y.year}\n(${y.age})`,
    age: y.age,
    "Trad IRA":    Math.round(y.endingTraditionalIRA),
    "Roth IRA":    Math.round(y.endingRothIRA),
    "Taxable":     Math.round(y.endingTaxableBrokerage),
    "Conv. Tax":   Math.round(y.conversionTax),
    "Baseline RMD": Math.round(y.baselineRMD),
    "Projected RMD": Math.round(y.projectedRMD),
    "MAGI":        Math.round(y.magi),
  })) ?? [];

  const irmaaLine = filing === "single" ? 109000 : 218000;

  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: "100vh", background: "#f0f2f5", padding: "24px 16px" }}>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1100, margin: "0 auto", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>

        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ background: "#1f2328", borderRadius: 10, padding: "20px 28px", color: "#fff" }}>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 700, margin: 0 }}>
            🏦 Roth Conversion Optimizer
          </h1>
          <p style={{ color: "#8b949e", marginTop: 4, fontSize: 14 }}>
            Multi-year Roth conversion &amp; retirement tax modeling · 2024 brackets · 2026 IRMAA thresholds
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Inputs + Summary row */}
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "340px 1fr", gap: 20 }}>

          {/* Inputs panel */}
          <Card title="Inputs">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>
              <InputField label="Traditional IRA Balance" id="tradIRA" value={tradIRA} onChange={setTradIRA} min={0} step={1000} prefix="$" />
              <InputField label="Taxable Brokerage (for taxes)" id="brokerage" value={brokerage} onChange={setBrokerage} min={0} step={1000} prefix="$" note="Used to pay conversion taxes each year" />
              <InputField label="Annual Roth Conversion" id="conversion" value={conversion} onChange={setConversion} min={0} step={1000} prefix="$" />
              <InputField label="Other Annual Income" id="otherIncome" value={otherIncome} onChange={setOtherIncome} min={0} step={1000} prefix="$" note="Wages, Social Security, pension, etc." />
              <InputField label="Conversion Years" id="years" value={years} onChange={setYears} min={1} max={30} step={1} suffix="yrs" />
              <InputField label="Annual Growth Rate" id="growth" value={growth} onChange={setGrowth} min={0} max={20} step={0.5} suffix="%" />
              <InputField label="Current Age" id="age" value={age} onChange={setAge} min={18} max={85} step={1} suffix="yrs" />

              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                <label htmlFor="filing" style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}>Filing Status</label>
                <select
                  id="filing"
                  value={filing}
                  onChange={(e) => setFiling(e.target.value)}
                  style={{ padding: "7px 10px", border: "1px solid #d0d7de", borderRadius: 6, fontSize: 14, background: "#fff" }}
                >
                  <option value="single">Single</option>
                  <option value="mfj">Married Filing Jointly</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>

          {/* Summary panel */}
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
            {error && (
              <div style={{ background: "#fee2e2", border: "1px solid #fca5a5", borderRadius: 8, padding: "12px 16px", color: "#991b1b", fontSize: 14 }}>
                ⚠ {error}
              </div>
            )}

            {s && (
              <>
                <Card title="Conversion Summary" accent="#3b82d4">
                  <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, 1fr)", gap: 16 }}>
                    <StatBox label="Total Converted" value={fmt$(s.totalAmountConverted)} color="#3b82d4" />
                    <StatBox label="Total Conv. Tax" value={fmt$(s.totalFederalTaxOnConversions)} color="#ef4444" />
                    <StatBox label="IRMAA Surcharges" value={fmt$(s.totalIRMAASurcharges)} color="#f59e0b"
                      sub={`${s.irmaaBreachYears} breach yr${s.irmaaBreachYears !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`} />
                  </div>
                </Card>

                <Card title="Final Balances" accent="#10b981">
                  <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, 1fr)", gap: 16 }}>
                    <StatBox label="Traditional IRA" value={fmt$(s.finalTraditionalIRA)} color="#1f2328" />
                    <StatBox label="Roth IRA (tax-free)" value={fmt$(s.finalRothIRA)} color="#10b981" />
                    <StatBox label="Taxable Brokerage" value={fmt$(s.finalTaxableBrokerage)} color="#57606a" />
                  </div>
                </Card>

                <Card title="RMD Projection at Age 73" accent="#7c5cd8">
                  <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, 1fr)", gap: 16 }}>
                    <StatBox label="Projected RMD" value={fmt$(s.projectedRMDAtAge73)} color="#7c5cd8" />
                    <StatBox label="Baseline RMD (no conv.)" value={fmt$(s.baselineRMDAtAge73)} color="#8b949e" />
                    <StatBox label="Annual RMD Saved"
                      value={fmt$(s.baselineRMDAtAge73 - s.projectedRMDAtAge73)}
                      color="#10b981"
                      sub="less taxable income at 73" />
                  </div>
                </Card>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Charts */}
        {chartData.length > 0 && (
          <>
            {/* Account Balances chart */}
            <Card title="Account Balances — End of Year">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
                <LineChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <YAxis tickFormatter={(v) => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <Tooltip formatter={(v) => fmt$(v)} />
                  <Legend />
                  <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Trad IRA"  stroke="#3b82d4" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
                  <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Roth IRA"  stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
                  <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Taxable"   stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="5 3" />
                </LineChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Card>

            {/* MAGI vs IRMAA threshold */}
            <Card title={`MAGI vs 2026 IRMAA Threshold (${filing === "single" ? "$109k single" : "$218k MFJ"})`}>
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
                <BarChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <YAxis tickFormatter={(v) => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <Tooltip formatter={(v) => fmt$(v)} />
                  <Legend />
                  <Bar dataKey="MAGI" fill="#3b82d4" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                  {/* Reference line drawn via a flat invisible bar + custom label */}
                  <Bar
                    dataKey={() => irmaaLine}
                    name={`IRMAA Threshold (${fmt$(irmaaLine)})`}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="#ef4444"
                    strokeWidth={2}
                    strokeDasharray="6 3"
                    legendType="line"
                  />
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Card>

            {/* Annual conversion tax */}
            <Card title="Annual Federal Tax on Conversion">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={200}>
                <BarChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <YAxis tickFormatter={(v) => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <Tooltip formatter={(v) => fmt$(v)} />
                  <Legend />
                  <Bar dataKey="Conv. Tax" fill="#ef4444" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Card>

            {/* RMD comparison */}
            <Card title="Projected RMD: Conversion Strategy vs Do-Nothing Baseline">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
                <LineChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <YAxis tickFormatter={(v) => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <Tooltip formatter={(v) => fmt$(v)} />
                  <Legend />
                  <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Baseline RMD"   stroke="#8b949e" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="5 3" />
                  <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Projected RMD"  stroke="#7c5cd8" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
                </LineChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Card>

            {/* Year-by-year table */}
            <Card title="Year-by-Year Breakdown">
              <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
                <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 13 }}>
                  <thead>
                    <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                      {["Yr", "Age", "Converted", "Trad IRA", "Roth IRA", "Taxable", "Conv. Tax", "Eff. Rate", "MAGI", "IRMAA", "RMD Saved"].map((h) => (
                        <th key={h} style={{ padding: "8px 10px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                          {h}
                        </th>
                      ))}
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {result.years.map((y, i) => (
                      <tr key={i} style={{ background: i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa" }}>
                        <td style={td}>{y.year}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{y.age}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.conversionAmount)}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.endingTraditionalIRA)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#10b981", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(y.endingRothIRA)}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.endingTaxableBrokerage)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#ef4444" }}>{fmt$(y.conversionTax)}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmtPct(y.effectiveRate)}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.magi)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "center" }}>
                          <IRMAAbadge label={y.irmaa.label} crosses={y.irmaa.crossesThreshold} />
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#7c5cd8", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(y.rmdReduction)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </Card>
          </>
        )}

        <div style={{ textAlign: "center", fontSize: 12, color: "#8b949e", padding: "8px 0 16px" }}>
          Uses 2024 federal tax brackets · 2026 projected Medicare IRMAA thresholds · IRS Uniform Lifetime Table for RMDs
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const td = {
  padding: "7px 10px",
  textAlign: "right",
  borderBottom: "1px solid #f0f0f0",
  whiteSpace: "nowrap",
};

### 🤖 Assistant

That confirms everything. Here's the full explanation:

---

### What "Taxable Brokerage" means

It's your **regular investment account** (not an IRA) — money you've already paid income tax on. In this model it serves one specific job:

> **It's the wallet that pays the conversion taxes each year.**

Every year you do a Roth conversion, the engine does this:
1. Calculates the federal tax owed on the conversion
2. Deducts that tax from the Taxable Brokerage balance
3. The remaining balance still grows at your growth rate

---

### Why it hits $0 in later years

With the default inputs — **$100,000 brokerage, $50,000/yr conversion, 7% growth** — here's what happens:

| Year | Tax paid out | Growth earned | Net change |
|------|-------------|---------------|------------|
| 1 | −$5,552 | +$6,633 | Grows slightly |
| 2 | −$5,552 | +$6,730 | Still growing |
| … | … | … | … |
| ~Yr 7–8 | −$5,552 | less than tax | Starts shrinking |
| Eventually | balance < tax owed | — | **Hits $0** |

Once the brokerage hits $0, the engine caps the tax payment at whatever is left (`Math.min(conversionTax, taxBrokerage)` on [line 242](simulationEngine.js:242)) — so it never goes negative. But it means **the remaining conversion tax is unfunded** in the model.

---

### What this means for you

In real life, if the brokerage runs dry you'd either:
- **Pay taxes from the conversion itself** (less money actually lands in the Roth)
- **Use a paycheck/other cash** to cover the tax bill

Want me to add a warning indicator in the UI when the brokerage balance hits zero, and optionally model the "pay tax from conversion" scenario?

// IRS Uniform Lifetime Table divisors for RMD calculation (ages 72–90+)
const ULT = {
  72: 27.4, 73: 26.5, 74: 25.5, 75: 24.6, 76: 23.7, 77: 22.9,
  78: 22.0, 79: 21.1, 80: 20.2, 81: 19.4, 82: 18.5, 83: 17.7,
  84: 16.8, 85: 16.0, 86: 15.2, 87: 14.4, 88: 13.7, 89: 12.9,
  90: 12.2,
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SECURE 2.0 Act — RMD start age rules (enacted Dec 2022)
//
//   Born 1950 or earlier  → age 72  (pre-SECURE / SECURE 1.0)
//   Born 1951 – 1959      → age 73  (SECURE 2.0, effective 2023)
//   Born 1960 or later    → age 75  (SECURE 2.0, effective 2033)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function rmdStartAge(birthYear) {
  if (birthYear <= 1950) return 72;
  if (birthYear <= 1959) return 73;
  return 75;
}

/**
 * Calculate the Required Minimum Distribution for a given balance and age.
 * Returns 0 if below the RMD start age of 73.
 * @param {number} balance  Starting balance for the RMD year
 * @param {number} age
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcRMD(balance, age) {
  if (age < 73) return 0;
  const divisor = ULT[Math.min(age, 90)] ?? 12.2;
  return balance / divisor;
}
=======
/**
 * Calculate the Required Minimum Distribution for a given balance, age,
 * and birth year (used to derive the correct SECURE 2.0 start age).
 * Returns 0 if the owner has not yet reached their RMD start age.
 * @param {number} balance    Starting balance for the RMD year
 * @param {number} age        Owner's age during the RMD year
 * @param {number} birthYear  Owner's birth year (determines start age)
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcRMD(balance, age, birthYear) {
  const startAge = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  if (age < startAge) return 0;
  const divisor = ULT[Math.min(age, 90)] ?? 12.2;
  return balance / divisor;
}

export function runSimulation(inputs) {
  const {

    taxableBrokerageBalance,

    payTaxFromConversion = false,
    // Birth year drives the SECURE 2.0 RMD start age.
    // Defaults to (current calendar year - currentAge) if not supplied.
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,

### What's new

**New "Birth Year" input field** — sits right below Current Age, auto-fills when you change age, and shows a live note:

| Birth Year | Note shown | RMD age used |
|---|---|---|
| ≤ 1950 | *RMD starts at age 72 (pre-SECURE)* | **72** |

| ≥ 1960 | *RMD starts at age 75 (SECURE 2.0, born 1960+)* | **75** |

**The engine logic** — [`rmdStartAge(birthYear)`](simulationEngine.js:69) is a standalone exported function so it can be unit-tested or reused. [`calcRMD()`](simulationEngine.js:163) now takes `birthYear` as a third argument and derives the correct trigger internally.

The exact SECURE 2.0 cutoffs per the statute:
- **Born ≤ 1950** → age 72 (original SECURE Act / pre-reform)
- **Born 1951–1959** → age 73 (in effect from Jan 1, 2023)
- **Born ≥ 1960** → age 75 (takes effect Jan 1, 2033)

### 👤 User

There is a bug in the UI where if there is a 0 as first digit, cannot backspace to erase the 0 and it will type as 010 for 10 years. This seems to happen for any field to change the number.  Can you fix this bug.

---

### 🤖 Assistant

> 🔧 called: read_file

The bug is in the `InputField` component — it stores values as numbers, so when you clear the field it becomes `0`, and then typing `1` gives `01`. The fix is to store input values as strings internally and only parse to a number on blur or when the value is valid.

> 🔧 **read_file** — src/App.jsx

> 🔧 called: read_file



> 🔧 **read_file** — src/App.jsx

### 🤖 Assistant

> 🔧 called: apply_diff

The fix: switch `InputField` to `type="text"`, manage a local string draft with `useState`, only call `onChange` with a parsed number when the value is actually valid, and clear the leading-zero problem on blur.

> 🔧 **apply_diff** — <<<<<<< SEARCH

function InputField({ label, id, value, onChange, type = "number", min, max, step, note, prefix, suffix }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
      <label htmlFor={id} style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1f2328" }}>
        {label}
      </label>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
        {prefix && <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "#57606a" }}>{prefix}</span>}
        <input
          id={id}
          type={type}
          value={value}
          min={min}
          max={max}
          step={step}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(type === "number" ? parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0 : e.target.value)}
          style={{
            flex: 1,
            padding: "7px 10px",
            border: "1px solid #d0d7de",
            borderRadius: 6,
            fontSize: 14,
            outline: "none",
            background: "#fff",
          }}
        />
        {suffix && <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "#57606a" }}>{suffix}</span>}

=======
function InputField({ label, id, value, onChange, type = "number", min, max, step, note, prefix, suffix }) {
  // Keep a local string draft so the user can freely edit (backspace, clear,
  // type mid-number) without the parent state fighting the input cursor.
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(value));

  // Sync draft when the parent value changes from outside (e.g. age → birthYear)
  useEffect(() => {
    setDraft(String(value));
  }, [value]);

  if (type !== "number") {
    // Non-numeric fields: pass through directly
    return (
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
        <label htmlFor={id} style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1f2328" }}>{label}</label>
        <input
          id={id}
          type={type}
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
          style={{ flex: 1, padding: "7px 10px", border: "1px solid #d0d7de", borderRadius: 6, fontSize: 14, outline: "none", background: "#fff" }}
        />
        {note && <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#8b949e" }}>{note}</span>}
      </div>
    );
  }

  function handleChange(e) {
    const raw = e.target.value;
    setDraft(raw);
    // Only propagate a parsed number when the field has a real value
    const parsed = parseFloat(raw);
    if (!isNaN(parsed)) onChange(parsed);
  }

  function handleBlur() {
    // On blur: commit a clean number string, defaulting to 0 if empty/invalid
    const parsed = parseFloat(draft);
    const clean = isNaN(parsed) ? 0 : parsed;
    setDraft(String(clean));
    onChange(clean);
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
      <label htmlFor={id} style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1f2328" }}>
        {label}
      </label>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
        {prefix && <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "#57606a" }}>{prefix}</span>}
        <input
          id={id}
          type="text"
          inputMode="numeric"
          value={draft}
          min={min}
          max={max}
          step={step}
          onChange={handleChange}
          onBlur={handleBlur}
          style={{
            flex: 1,
            padding: "7px 10px",
            border: "1px solid #d0d7de",
            borderRadius: 6,
            fontSize: 14,
            outline: "none",
            background: "#fff",
          }}
        />
        {suffix && <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "#57606a" }}>{suffix}</span>}
      </div>
      {note && <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#8b949e" }}>{note}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

### 🤖 Assistant

✅ Clean build. Refresh your browser and the bug is fixed. Here's exactly what changed and why:

---

### What was wrong

`type="number"` inputs are controlled by React — every keystroke forces the value back to the parent's number state. So when you press **Backspace** to clear `10`, React immediately re-renders with `0`, and your next keystroke produces `01`.

### What the fix does

| Before | After |
|---|---|
| `type="number"`, value bound directly to parent state | `type="text"` with `inputMode="numeric"` (mobile keyboard still shows numbers) |
| Every keystroke called `onChange` and re-rendered | Local `draft` string absorbs all typing freely |
| Backspace → `0` → next digit → `01` | Backspace works naturally; field can be fully empty mid-edit |
| — | **`onBlur`** commits the final clean number to parent state when you leave the field |
| — | **`useEffect`** syncs the draft if the parent changes the value from outside (e.g. changing Age auto-updates Birth Year) |

function OptimizerPanel({ optResult, onApply }) {

  const { recommended, alternatives, breakdown } = optResult;

    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>

        background: "linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#dcfce7)",
        border: "1px solid #86efac", borderRadius: 10, padding: "18px 22px",
      }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: "#166534", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          ✅ Recommended Strategy
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4,1fr)", gap: 14, marginBottom: 14 }}>
          <StatBox label="Annual Conversion"   value={"$" + recommended.annualConversion.toLocaleString()} color="#166534" />
          <StatBox label="Duration"            value={`${recommended.years} yr${recommended.years !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`} color="#1f2328" />
          <StatBox label="Total Tax"           value={"$" + recommended.totalTax.toLocaleString()} color="#ef4444" />
          <StatBox label="Total Cost (Tax+IRMAA)" value={"$" + recommended.totalCost.toLocaleString()} color="#3b82d4" />
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#166534", marginBottom: 12 }}>{recommended.strategy}</div>
        <button

          }}
        >
          Apply to Simulator →
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Alternatives table */}
      {alternatives.length > 0 && (
        <Card title="All Candidates — ranked by total cost">
          <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
            <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 13 }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                  {cols.map(h => (
                    <th key={h} style={{ padding: "7px 10px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap", textAlign: h === "Strategy" || h === "" ? "left" : "right" }}>

                    <td style={td}>{r.years}</td>

                    <td style={{ ...td, color: r.totalIRMAA > 0 ? "#f59e0b" : "#8b949e" }}>
                      {r.totalIRMAA > 0 ? "$" + r.totalIRMAA.toLocaleString() : "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, fontWeight: i === 0 ? 700 : 400, color: i === 0 ? "#166534" : undefined }}>
                      {"$" + r.totalCost.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#7c5cd8" }}>
                      {r.rmdReduction > 0 ? "$" + r.rmdReduction.toLocaleString() + "/yr" : "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left" }}>
                      {i > 0 && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => onApply(r.annualConversion, r.years)}

### 🤖 Assistant

✅ Zero errors or warnings. Refresh your browser — here's what's new:

---

### What was built

**New "⚡ Optimize for Me" tab** — click it from the top of the app.

#### How the optimizer works

It tests every mathematically meaningful conversion ceiling:
1. **Top of each tax bracket** — fill to the 12%, 22%, 24% ceiling without spilling into the next rate
2. **IRMAA threshold boundaries** — stay just at or below $109k (single) / $218k (MFJ)
3. **Equal-spread options** — divide the full IRA balance evenly over 1→N years

Every candidate is run through the full simulation, scored by **total tax + IRMAA surcharges**, and ranked.

#### What you see in the Optimize tab

| Element | Description |
|---|---|
| **✅ Recommended Strategy** green card | The lowest-cost plan — annual amount, years, total tax, total cost |
| **"Apply to Simulator →" button** | One click copies the amounts into the Manual tab so you can explore it in detail |
| **All Candidates table** | Up to 6 ranked alternatives with individual "Apply" buttons |
| **Account Balances chart** | Shows the recommended plan's projected trajectory |

#### The new input in Optimize mode

**Max IRMAA Tier Allowed** dropdown — set how much Medicare surcharge you're willing to accept. Set to "No IRMAA" and the optimizer will only show plans that never cross the threshold, even if a slightly cheaper option exists that breaches it.

---

### 👤 User

the "Optimize for Me" don't take into account the Traditional IRA Balance. I would like a Roth Conversion tool to allows you to model multi-year conversion strategies, view the projected impact on lifetime tax liability, and simulate how filling up specific tax brackets (e.g., the 12%, 22%, or 24% brackets) affects your overall estate and cash flow. Also, track cumulative income streams to help you visualize how conversions impact Medicare surcharges and the taxation of your Social Security benefits. The end goal is to get annual Roth IRA conversion amounts that minimize lifetime taxes, IRMAA surcharges, minimize social securitiy taxes whil preserving living standards. Would like to see the bracket-filling strategies to minimize taxes too.

-------
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Optimizer
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Determine the maximum Roth conversion amount for a single year that stays
 * within a given MAGI ceiling (tax bracket top or IRMAA threshold).
 *
 * @param {number} ceiling         Target MAGI ceiling (e.g. top of 22% bracket)
 * @param {number} otherIncome     Other ordinary income this year
 * @param {number} tradIRABalance  Current Traditional IRA balance (caps conversion)
 * @returns {number}
 */
function conversionForCeiling(ceiling, otherIncome, tradIRABalance) {
  const headroom = Math.max(0, ceiling - otherIncome);
  return Math.min(headroom, tradIRABalance);
}

/**
 * Score a candidate strategy: total tax + IRMAA surcharges paid over all years.
 * Lower is better.
 *
 * @param {object} baseInputs   All runSimulation inputs except conversion fields
 * @param {number} amount       Annual conversion amount to try
 * @param {number} numYears     Number of conversion years to try
 * @returns {number} total cost (tax + IRMAA)
 */
function scorStrategy(baseInputs, amount, numYears) {
  const r = runSimulation({ ...baseInputs, annualRothConversion: amount, conversionYears: numYears });
  return r.summary.totalFederalTaxOnConversions + r.summary.totalIRMAASurcharges;
}

/**
 * Find the optimal Roth conversion strategy — the combination of annual
 * conversion amount and number of years that minimises total federal tax +
 * Medicare IRMAA surcharges, subject to:
 *   - Converting 100% of the Traditional IRA before the RMD start age
 *   - Not crossing into a higher IRMAA tier than the user allows
 *   - Using the available taxable brokerage to fund taxes
 *
 * Strategy: for each candidate MAGI ceiling (top of each tax bracket and just
 * below the IRMAA threshold), compute the flat annual conversion that fills to
 * that ceiling and the number of years needed. Score every candidate and return
 * the winner alongside a ranked list of alternatives.
 *
 * @param {object} inputs  Same shape as runSimulation inputs (no annualRothConversion / conversionYears needed)
 * @param {object} [opts]
 * @param {number} [opts.maxIRMAATier=0]  Maximum acceptable IRMAA tier (0 = stay below threshold)
 * @returns {{
 *   recommended: { annualConversion, years, totalTax, totalIRMAA, totalCost, strategy },
 *   alternatives: Array,
 *   breakdown: object   Full runSimulation result for the recommended strategy
 * }}
 */
export function optimizeConversion(inputs, opts = {}) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
  } = inputs;

  const { maxIRMAATier = 0 } = opts;

  const rmdAge    = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const maxYears  = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge); // years until RMDs begin
  const stdDed    = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const brackets  = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  // Build candidate MAGI ceilings:
  //  1. Top of each tax bracket (converted back to MAGI = taxable + stdDeduction + otherIncome)
  //  2. Just below each IRMAA tier threshold
  const ceilings = new Set();

  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (b.max === Infinity) continue;
    // bracket.max is taxable income; convert to MAGI
    const magiAtBracketTop = b.max + stdDed + otherOrdinaryIncome;
    ceilings.add(Math.floor(magiAtBracketTop));
  }

  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (t.tier > maxIRMAATier && t.maxMAGI !== Infinity) {
      // Stay just below this tier's threshold
      ceilings.add(t.maxMAGI);        // at the threshold (no surcharge if ≤)
    }
  }

  // Also try: convert entire balance equally over every possible year count
  for (let y = 1; y <= maxYears; y++) {
    const flat = Math.ceil(traditionalIRABalance / y);
    ceilings.add(otherOrdinaryIncome + flat); // implied MAGI ceiling
  }

  const candidates = [];

  for (const ceiling of ceilings) {
    const annualConversion = conversionForCeiling(ceiling, otherOrdinaryIncome, traditionalIRABalance);
    if (annualConversion <= 0) continue;

    // How many years to convert the full balance at this rate?
    const yearsNeeded = Math.min(
      Math.ceil(traditionalIRABalance / annualConversion),
      maxYears
    );
    if (yearsNeeded < 1) continue;

    const r = runSimulation({
      ...inputs,
      annualRothConversion: annualConversion,
      conversionYears:      yearsNeeded,
    });

    const totalTax   = r.summary.totalFederalTaxOnConversions;
    const totalIRMAA = r.summary.totalIRMAASurcharges;
    const totalCost  = totalTax + totalIRMAA;
    const maxTierHit = Math.max(...r.years.map(y => y.irmaa.tier));

    // Skip if IRMAA tier exceeds user's tolerance
    if (maxTierHit > maxIRMAATier) continue;

    candidates.push({
      annualConversion: round2(annualConversion),
      years:            yearsNeeded,
      totalTax:         round2(totalTax),
      totalIRMAA:       round2(totalIRMAA),
      totalCost:        round2(totalCost),
      maxIRMAATier:     maxTierHit,
      irmaaBreachYears: r.summary.irmaaBreachYears,
      finalTraditionalIRA: r.summary.finalTraditionalIRA,
      finalRothIRA:        r.summary.finalRothIRA,
      rmdReduction:     round2(
        r.summary.baselineRMDAtRmdAge - r.summary.projectedRMDAtRmdAge
      ),
      strategy: describeStrategy(annualConversion, yearsNeeded, ceiling, filingStatus, otherOrdinaryIncome, stdDed),
    });
  }

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("No valid conversion strategy found for the given constraints.");
  }

  // Sort by total cost ascending; break ties by fewer years (faster conversion)
  candidates.sort((a, b) => a.totalCost - b.totalCost || a.years - b.years);

  // De-duplicate: keep only the cheapest entry per (years, annualConversion) pair
  const seen = new Set();
  const unique = candidates.filter(c => {
    const key = `${c.years}:${c.annualConversion}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false;
    seen.add(key);
    return true;
  });

  const recommended = unique[0];
  const alternatives = unique.slice(1, 6); // top 5 alternatives

  // Full simulation for the recommended plan
  const breakdown = runSimulation({
    ...inputs,
    annualRothConversion: recommended.annualConversion,
    conversionYears:      recommended.years,
  });

  return { recommended, alternatives, breakdown };
}

/**
 * Human-readable label for why a ceiling was chosen.
 */
function describeStrategy(amount, years, ceiling, filingStatus, otherIncome, stdDed) {
  const taxableIncome = ceiling - otherIncome - stdDed;
  const brackets = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (Math.abs(ceiling - t.maxMAGI) < 500) {
      return `Stay below IRMAA ${t.label === "No IRMAA" ? "threshold" : t.label + " threshold"} — ${years} yr${years !== 1 ? "s" : ""} × $${amount.toLocaleString()}/yr`;
    }
  }
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (taxableIncome >= b.min && taxableIncome <= b.max) {
      return `Fill to top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket — ${years} yr${years !== 1 ? "s" : ""} × $${amount.toLocaleString()}/yr`;
    }
  }
  return `${years} yr${years !== 1 ? "s" : ""} × $${amount.toLocaleString()}/yr`;
}
=======
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Social Security taxation helper (IRS "combined income" provisional income)
//
// Provisional income = AGI + non-taxable interest + 50% of SS benefit
// Up to 50% of SS taxable if PI > $25k (single) / $32k (MFJ)
// Up to 85% of SS taxable if PI > $34k (single) / $44k (MFJ)
// Thresholds are NOT inflation-adjusted (set in 1983/1993 law).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const SS_THRESHOLDS = {
  single: { tier1: 25000, tier2: 34000 },
  mfj:    { tier1: 32000, tier2: 44000 },
};

/**
 * Calculate the taxable portion of Social Security benefits.
 * @param {number} ssBenefit       Annual gross SS benefit ($)
 * @param {number} otherAGI        AGI excluding SS (wages, conversion, RMDs, etc.)
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {{ taxableSS: number, inclusionRate: number }}
 */
export function calcSSTax(ssBenefit, otherAGI, filingStatus) {
  if (!ssBenefit || ssBenefit <= 0) return { taxableSS: 0, inclusionRate: 0 };
  const provisionalIncome = otherAGI + ssBenefit * 0.5;
  const { tier1, tier2 } = SS_THRESHOLDS[filingStatus];
  let taxableSS = 0;
  if (provisionalIncome <= tier1) {
    taxableSS = 0;
  } else if (provisionalIncome <= tier2) {
    taxableSS = Math.min(0.5 * ssBenefit, 0.5 * (provisionalIncome - tier1));
  } else {
    const tier2Amount = Math.min(0.5 * ssBenefit, 0.5 * (tier2 - tier1));
    taxableSS = Math.min(
      0.85 * ssBenefit,
      tier2Amount + 0.85 * (provisionalIncome - tier2)
    );
  }
  taxableSS = Math.min(taxableSS, 0.85 * ssBenefit); // hard cap at 85%
  return {
    taxableSS:     round2(taxableSS),
    inclusionRate: round2(taxableSS / ssBenefit),
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Lifetime projection engine
//
// Extends beyond the conversion window to model the full retirement picture:
// RMD years, SS benefits, estate value. Returns one row per year from
// currentAge to endAge.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Run a full lifetime projection combining conversion years + RMD years.
 *
 * @param {object} inputs
 * @param {number}  inputs.traditionalIRABalance
 * @param {number}  inputs.taxableBrokerageBalance
 * @param {number}  inputs.annualRothConversion
 * @param {number}  inputs.conversionYears
 * @param {number}  inputs.growthRate
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} inputs.filingStatus
 * @param {number}  [inputs.currentAge=60]
 * @param {number}  [inputs.birthYear]
 * @param {number}  [inputs.otherOrdinaryIncome=0]   Non-SS, non-RMD income (wages, pension)
 * @param {number}  [inputs.socialSecurityBenefit=0] Annual gross SS benefit (starts at ssStartAge)
 * @param {number}  [inputs.ssStartAge=67]           Age SS benefits begin
 * @param {number}  [inputs.endAge=90]               Age to project through
 * @param {boolean} [inputs.payTaxFromConversion=false]
 * @returns {{ years: LifetimeYear[], summary: object }}
 */
export function runLifetimeProjection(inputs) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    annualRothConversion,
    conversionYears,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    endAge = 90,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
  } = inputs;

  const stdDed   = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const rmdAge   = rmdStartAge(birthYear);

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;

  const years = [];

  for (let age = currentAge; age <= endAge; age++) {
    const yearIndex       = age - currentAge;
    const isConversionYr  = yearIndex < conversionYears && tradIRA > 0;
    const ss              = age >= ssStartAge ? socialSecurityBenefit : 0;

    // RMD (uses prior year-end balance → we'll compute after growth below)
    // We approximate by working with current tradIRA as start-of-year balance.
    const rmd = calcRMD(tradIRA, age, birthYear);

    // Conversion (capped at remaining balance)
    const conversionAmount = isConversionYr
      ? Math.min(annualRothConversion, tradIRA)
      : 0;

    // SS taxation — provisional income uses non-SS income + 50% SS
    const nonSSIncome  = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversionAmount + rmd;
    const { taxableSS, inclusionRate } = calcSSTax(ss, nonSSIncome, filingStatus);

    // Total ordinary income & taxable income
    const grossIncome   = nonSSIncome + taxableSS;
    const taxableIncome = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDed);
    const totalTax      = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    // Marginal tax from conversion only
    const baseIncome    = otherOrdinaryIncome + rmd + taxableSS;
    const baseTax       = calcFederalTax(Math.max(0, baseIncome - stdDed), filingStatus);
    const conversionTax = totalTax - baseTax;

    const { effectiveRate, marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    // MAGI for IRMAA = grossIncome (before std deduction)
    const magi  = grossIncome;
    const irmaa = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus);

    // Pay taxes from brokerage first
    const taxPaidFromBrokerage = Math.min(totalTax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaidFromBrokerage;
    const taxShortfall     = totalTax - taxPaidFromBrokerage;
    const brokerageExhausted = taxShortfall > 0;

    // Apply conversion
    tradIRA -= conversionAmount + rmd; // RMD leaves the IRA too
    tradIRA  = Math.max(0, tradIRA);

    const rothDeposit = payTaxFromConversion
      ? Math.max(0, conversionAmount - taxShortfall)
      : conversionAmount;
    rothIRA += rothDeposit;

    // Apply living expenses drawn from taxable (modeled as 0 here — user controls via other income)
    // Apply growth
    tradIRA      = Math.max(0, tradIRA)      * (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage = Math.max(0, taxBrokerage) * (1 + growthRate);

    // Estate value = all accounts
    const estateValue = tradIRA + rothIRA + taxBrokerage;

    years.push({
      age,
      year:             yearIndex + 1,
      phase:            isConversionYr ? "conversion" : age < rmdAge ? "gap" : "rmd",
      conversionAmount: round2(conversionAmount),
      rmd:              round2(rmd),
      ssBenefit:        round2(ss),
      taxableSS:        round2(taxableSS),
      ssInclusionRate:  round2(inclusionRate),
      grossIncome:      round2(grossIncome),
      taxableIncome:    round2(taxableIncome),
      totalFederalTax:  round2(totalTax),
      conversionTax:    round2(conversionTax),
      taxShortfall:     round2(taxShortfall),
      brokerageExhausted,
      effectiveRate:    round2(effectiveRate),
      marginalRate,
      magi:             round2(magi),
      irmaa: {
        tier:             irmaa.tier,
        label:            irmaa.label,
        partBMonthly:     irmaa.partBMonthly,
        partDMonthly:     irmaa.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge:  irmaa.annualSurcharge,
        crossesThreshold: irmaa.crossesThreshold,
      },
      endingTraditionalIRA:   round2(tradIRA),
      endingRothIRA:          round2(rothIRA),
      endingTaxableBrokerage: round2(taxBrokerage),
      estateValue:            round2(estateValue),
    });
  }

  const totalTaxPaid      = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.totalFederalTax, 0);
  const totalIRMAA        = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.irmaa.annualSurcharge, 0);
  const totalSSTax        = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.taxableSS, 0);
  const totalConverted    = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.conversionAmount, 0);
  const last              = years[years.length - 1] ?? {};

  return {
    years,
    summary: {
      totalLifetimeTax:         round2(totalTaxPaid),
      totalLifetimeIRMAA:       round2(totalIRMAA),
      totalLifetimeCost:        round2(totalTaxPaid + totalIRMAA),
      totalTaxableSS:           round2(totalSSTax),
      totalAmountConverted:     round2(totalConverted),
      finalTraditionalIRA:      last.endingTraditionalIRA  ?? 0,
      finalRothIRA:             last.endingRothIRA         ?? 0,
      finalTaxableBrokerage:    last.endingTaxableBrokerage ?? 0,
      estateValueAtEndAge:      last.estateValue           ?? 0,
      rmdStartAge:              rmdAge,
    },
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bracket-filling optimizer
//
// For each named bracket target (12%, 22%, 24%, IRMAA boundary, etc.),
// compute the optimal per-year conversion using a forward simulation that
// tracks the actual IRA balance year by year (not a flat amount × years).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Given a MAGI ceiling target and a starting IRA balance, compute the
 * maximum conversion that fits under the ceiling each year, then simulate
 * forward until the IRA is drained or maxYears is reached.
 *
 * Returns the year-by-year conversion schedule + total cost.
 */
function bracketFillSchedule(baseInputs, magiCeiling, maxYears) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
  } = baseInputs;

  const stdDed = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;

  const schedule     = [];
  let totalTax       = 0;
  let totalIRMAA     = 0;
  let totalSSTax     = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxYears; i++) {
    if (tradIRA <= 0) break;
    const age = currentAge + i;
    const ss  = age >= ssStartAge ? socialSecurityBenefit : 0;

    // How much headroom do we have under the MAGI ceiling?
    // First estimate SS taxability at this ceiling (iterative for accuracy)
    // Simple approach: assume SS taxability at 85% for the headroom calc,
    // then correct after computing actual conversion.
    const headroom     = Math.max(0, magiCeiling - otherOrdinaryIncome);
    const conversion   = Math.min(headroom, tradIRA);
    if (conversion <= 0) break;

    // Recalculate SS tax with actual conversion
    const nonSSIncome  = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversion;
    const { taxableSS } = calcSSTax(ss, nonSSIncome, filingStatus);
    const grossIncome  = nonSSIncome + taxableSS;
    const taxableInc   = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDed);
    const tax          = calcFederalTax(taxableInc, filingStatus);
    const magi         = grossIncome;
    const irmaa        = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus);

    const taxPaid = Math.min(tax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaid;
    const shortfall = tax - taxPaid;

    tradIRA  -= conversion;
    tradIRA   = Math.max(0, tradIRA);
    const deposit = payTaxFromConversion ? Math.max(0, conversion - shortfall) : conversion;
    rothIRA  += deposit;

    tradIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage  = Math.max(0, taxBrokerage) * (1 + growthRate);

    totalTax   += tax;
    totalIRMAA += irmaa.annualSurcharge;
    totalSSTax += taxableSS;

    schedule.push({
      age, conversion: round2(conversion), tax: round2(tax),
      taxableSS: round2(taxableSS), magi: round2(magi),
      irmaaLabel: irmaa.label, irmaaTier: irmaa.tier,
      endingTradIRA: round2(tradIRA),
    });
  }

  return {
    schedule,
    yearsUsed:      schedule.length,
    totalTax:       round2(totalTax),
    totalIRMAA:     round2(totalIRMAA),
    totalSSTax:     round2(totalSSTax),
    totalCost:      round2(totalTax + totalIRMAA),
    remainingTradIRA: round2(tradIRA),
    finalRothIRA:   round2(rothIRA),
    finalTaxable:   round2(taxBrokerage),
  };
}

/**
 * Human-readable label for a MAGI ceiling.
 */
function ceilingLabel(ceiling, filingStatus, otherIncome, stdDed) {
  const taxableIncome = ceiling - otherIncome - stdDed;
  const brackets      = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers    = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (Math.abs(ceiling - t.maxMAGI) <= 1000)
      return `IRMAA threshold (${t.tier === 0 ? "No surcharge" : t.label}) — $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
  }
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (b.max !== Infinity && Math.abs(taxableIncome - b.max) <= 1000)
      return `Top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket — $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
    if (taxableIncome >= b.min && taxableIncome < b.max)
      return `Within ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket — $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
  }
  return `Custom: $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
}

/**
 * Run the full bracket-filling optimizer.
 *
 * Tests every named bracket ceiling + IRMAA boundary + equal-spread options,
 * using a year-by-year forward simulation that correctly tracks the shrinking
 * IRA balance and growing accounts. Scores by total (tax + IRMAA surcharges).
 *
 * @param {object} inputs  All base inputs (no annualRothConversion / conversionYears)
 * @param {object} [opts]
 * @param {number} [opts.maxIRMAATier=0]    Max acceptable IRMAA tier
 * @param {number} [opts.endAge=90]         Lifetime projection end age
 * @returns {{
 *   strategies: BracketStrategy[],        All candidates sorted by cost
 *   recommended: BracketStrategy,         Cheapest
 *   bracketComparison: BracketStrategy[], One entry per named bracket ceiling
 * }}
 */
export function optimizeConversion(inputs, opts = {}) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
  } = inputs;

  const { maxIRMAATier = 0, endAge = 90 } = opts;

  const rmdAge   = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const maxYears = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge);
  const stdDed   = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const brackets = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  // Named bracket ceilings (top of each bracket converted to MAGI)
  const namedCeilings = [];
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (b.max === Infinity) continue;
    const magiCeiling = b.max + stdDed + otherOrdinaryIncome;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: Math.floor(magiCeiling),
      label:   `Fill to top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket`,
      bracketRate: b.rate,
    });
  }
  // IRMAA tier boundaries
  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (t.maxMAGI === Infinity) continue;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: t.maxMAGI,
      label:   `Stay at/below IRMAA ${t.label} threshold ($${t.maxMAGI.toLocaleString()})`,
      bracketRate: null,
    });
  }

  // Equal-spread options: divide full IRA over 1…maxYears years
  const equalSpreadCeilings = [];
  for (let y = 1; y <= maxYears; y++) {
    const perYear = Math.ceil(traditionalIRABalance / y);
    equalSpreadCeilings.push({
      ceiling: Math.floor(otherOrdinaryIncome + perYear),
      label:   `Equal spread over ${y} yr${y !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`,
      bracketRate: null,
    });
  }

  const allCeilings = [...namedCeilings, ...equalSpreadCeilings];
  const strategies  = [];
  const seen        = new Set();

  for (const { ceiling, label, bracketRate } of allCeilings) {
    const key = String(ceiling);
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);

    const result = bracketFillSchedule(inputs, ceiling, maxYears);
    if (result.yearsUsed === 0) continue;

    const maxTierHit = Math.max(...result.schedule.map(s => s.irmaaTier), 0);
    if (maxTierHit > maxIRMAATier) continue;

    // Lifetime projection with this strategy
    const firstYearConversion = result.schedule[0]?.conversion ?? 0;
    const lifetimeResult = runLifetimeProjection({
      ...inputs,
      annualRothConversion: firstYearConversion,
      conversionYears:      result.yearsUsed,
      endAge,
    });

    strategies.push({
      label,
      bracketRate,
      ceiling,
      ceilingLabel: ceilingLabel(ceiling, filingStatus, otherOrdinaryIncome, stdDed),
      conversionYears:      result.yearsUsed,
      typicalAnnualAmount:  result.schedule[0]?.conversion ?? 0,
      schedule:             result.schedule,
      // Conversion-window costs
      conversionTax:        result.totalTax,
      conversionIRMAA:      result.totalIRMAA,
      conversionSSTax:      result.totalSSTax,
      conversionCost:       result.totalCost,
      remainingTradIRA:     result.remainingTradIRA,
      finalRothIRA:         result.finalRothIRA,
      // Lifetime costs
      lifetimeTax:          lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeTax,
      lifetimeIRMAA:        lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeIRMAA,
      lifetimeCost:         lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeCost,
      lifetimeSSTax:        lifetimeResult.summary.totalTaxableSS,
      estateValue:          lifetimeResult.summary.estateValueAtEndAge,
      lifetimeYears:        lifetimeResult.years,
      maxIRMAATier:         maxTierHit,
      irmaaBreachYears:     result.schedule.filter(s => s.irmaaTier > 0).length,
    });
  }

  if (strategies.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("No valid conversion strategy found. Try relaxing the IRMAA tier constraint.");
  }

  // Sort by lifetime cost (tax + IRMAA over full retirement)
  strategies.sort((a, b) => a.lifetimeCost - b.lifetimeCost);

  // bracketComparison: one entry per named bracket ceiling (for side-by-side view)
  const bracketComparison = namedCeilings
    .map(nc => strategies.find(s => s.ceiling === nc.ceiling))
    .filter(Boolean);

  return {
    strategies,
    recommended: strategies[0],
    bracketComparison,
  };
}

// ─── Optimizer panel ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function OptimizerPanel({ optResult, onApply }) {
  if (!optResult) return null;
  const { recommended, alternatives, breakdown } = optResult;

  const cols = ["Strategy", "Yrs", "$/yr", "Total Tax", "IRMAA", "Total Cost", "RMD Saved", ""];
  const rows = [recommended, ...alternatives];

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>

      {/* Recommended highlight */}
      <div style={{
        background: "linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#dcfce7)",
        border: "1px solid #86efac", borderRadius: 10, padding: "18px 22px",
      }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: "#166534", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          ✅ Recommended Strategy
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4,1fr)", gap: 14, marginBottom: 14 }}>
          <StatBox label="Annual Conversion"   value={"$" + recommended.annualConversion.toLocaleString()} color="#166534" />
          <StatBox label="Duration"            value={`${recommended.years} yr${recommended.years !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`} color="#1f2328" />
          <StatBox label="Total Tax"           value={"$" + recommended.totalTax.toLocaleString()} color="#ef4444" />
          <StatBox label="Total Cost (Tax+IRMAA)" value={"$" + recommended.totalCost.toLocaleString()} color="#3b82d4" />
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#166534", marginBottom: 12 }}>{recommended.strategy}</div>
        <button
          onClick={() => onApply(recommended.annualConversion, recommended.years)}
          style={{
            background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 6,
            padding: "8px 18px", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, cursor: "pointer",
          }}
        >
          Apply to Simulator →
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Alternatives table */}
      {alternatives.length > 0 && (
        <Card title="All Candidates — ranked by total cost">
          <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
            <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 13 }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                  {cols.map(h => (
                    <th key={h} style={{ padding: "7px 10px", textAlign: h === "Strategy" || h === "" ? "left" : "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                      {h}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {rows.map((r, i) => (
                  <tr key={i} style={{ background: i === 0 ? "#f0fdf4" : i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa" }}>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left", maxWidth: 280, whiteSpace: "normal" }}>
                      {i === 0 && <span style={{ background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 4, padding: "1px 6px", fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, marginRight: 6 }}>BEST</span>}
                      {r.strategy}
                    </td>
                    <td style={td}>{r.years}</td>
                    <td style={td}>{"$" + r.annualConversion.toLocaleString()}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#ef4444" }}>{"$" + r.totalTax.toLocaleString()}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: r.totalIRMAA > 0 ? "#f59e0b" : "#8b949e" }}>
                      {r.totalIRMAA > 0 ? "$" + r.totalIRMAA.toLocaleString() : "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, fontWeight: i === 0 ? 700 : 400, color: i === 0 ? "#166534" : undefined }}>
                      {"$" + r.totalCost.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#7c5cd8" }}>
                      {r.rmdReduction > 0 ? "$" + r.rmdReduction.toLocaleString() + "/yr" : "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left" }}>
                      {i > 0 && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => onApply(r.annualConversion, r.years)}
                          style={{ background: "none", border: "1px solid #d0d7de", borderRadius: 5, padding: "3px 10px", fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer", color: "#57606a" }}
                        >
                          Apply
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Breakdown charts for recommended plan */}
      <Card title="Recommended Plan — Account Balances">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
          <LineChart data={breakdown.years.map(y => ({
            name: `Yr${y.year}(${y.age})`,
            "Trad IRA": Math.round(y.endingTraditionalIRA),
            "Roth IRA": Math.round(y.endingRothIRA),
            "Taxable":  Math.round(y.endingTaxableBrokerage),
          }))} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => "$" + Number(v).toLocaleString()} />
            <Legend />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Trad IRA" stroke="#3b82d4" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Roth IRA" stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Taxable"  stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="5 3" />
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─── Optimizer panel ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function OptimizerPanel({ optResult, onApply }) {
  if (!optResult) return null;
  const { recommended, strategies, bracketComparison } = optResult;
  const [selectedIdx, setSelectedIdx] = useState(0);
  const selected = strategies[selectedIdx] ?? recommended;

  const fmtK = v => v == null ? "—" : "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k";

  // Lifetime chart data for selected strategy
  const lifetimeChartData = selected.lifetimeYears?.map(y => ({
    age: y.age,
    "Trad IRA":       Math.round(y.endingTraditionalIRA),
    "Roth IRA":       Math.round(y.endingRothIRA),
    "Taxable":        Math.round(y.endingTaxableBrokerage),
    "Fed Tax":        Math.round(y.totalFederalTax),
    "MAGI":           Math.round(y.magi),
    "Taxable SS":     Math.round(y.taxableSS),
    "IRMAA Surcharge":Math.round(y.irmaa?.annualSurcharge ?? 0),
    "Estate":         Math.round(y.estateValue),
  })) ?? [];

  // Bracket comparison bar data
  const bracketBarData = bracketComparison.filter(Boolean).map(s => ({
    name: s.bracketRate != null ? `${(s.bracketRate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket` : s.label.split(" ")[3] ?? s.label,
    "Lifetime Tax":   s.lifetimeTax,
    "Lifetime IRMAA": s.lifetimeIRMAA,
    "Taxable SS":     s.lifetimeSSTax,
    "Estate Value":   s.estateValue,
    yrs:              s.conversionYears,
    amt:              s.typicalAnnualAmount,
  }));

  const irmaaLine = selected.lifetimeYears?.[0]
    ? (selected.lifetimeYears[0].irmaa?.partBMonthly != null ? undefined : undefined)
    : undefined;

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>

      {/* Recommended banner */}
      <div style={{ background: "linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#dcfce7)", border: "1px solid #86efac", borderRadius: 10, padding: "18px 22px" }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: "#166534", marginBottom: 10 }}>✅ Optimal Strategy (lowest lifetime tax + IRMAA)</div>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(5,1fr)", gap: 12, marginBottom: 14 }}>
          <StatBox label="Typical Annual Conv." value={fmt$(recommended.typicalAnnualAmount)} color="#166534" />
          <StatBox label="Conversion Years"    value={`${recommended.conversionYears} yrs`}  color="#1f2328" />
          <StatBox label="Lifetime Tax"        value={fmt$(recommended.lifetimeTax)}          color="#ef4444" />
          <StatBox label="Lifetime IRMAA"      value={fmt$(recommended.lifetimeIRMAA)}        color="#f59e0b" />
          <StatBox label="Estate at 90"        value={fmt$(recommended.estateValue)}          color="#10b981" />
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#166534", marginBottom: 12 }}>{recommended.ceilingLabel}</div>
        <button onClick={() => onApply(recommended.typicalAnnualAmount, recommended.conversionYears)}
          style={{ background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 6, padding: "8px 18px", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, cursor: "pointer" }}>
          Apply to Simulator →
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Bracket comparison — side-by-side bar chart */}
      {bracketBarData.length > 0 && (
        <Card title="Bracket Strategy Comparison — Lifetime Cost & Estate Impact">
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 10, fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a" }}>Each bar = filling to the top of that tax bracket. Lower lifetime cost = better strategy for your situation.</div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
            <BarChart data={bracketBarData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
              <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
              <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
              <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} />
              <Legend />
              <Bar dataKey="Lifetime Tax"   fill="#ef4444" radius={[3,3,0,0]} stackId="cost" />
              <Bar dataKey="Lifetime IRMAA" fill="#f59e0b" radius={[3,3,0,0]} stackId="cost" />
              <Bar dataKey="Taxable SS"     fill="#a855f7" radius={[3,3,0,0]} stackId="cost" />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
          <div style={{ overflowX: "auto", marginTop: 12 }}>
            <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12 }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                  {["Strategy","Conv. Yrs","$/yr","Lifetime Tax","IRMAA","SS Taxed","Estate @90",""].map(h => (
                    <th key={h} style={{ padding: "6px 10px", textAlign: h === "Strategy" || h === "" ? "left" : "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{h}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {strategies.slice(0,8).map((s, i) => (
                  <tr key={i} onClick={() => setSelectedIdx(i)}
                    style={{ background: i === selectedIdx ? "#eff6ff" : i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left", maxWidth: 260, whiteSpace: "normal" }}>
                      {i === 0 && <span style={{ background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 3, padding: "1px 5px", fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, marginRight: 5 }}>BEST</span>}
                      {s.ceilingLabel}
                    </td>
                    <td style={td}>{s.conversionYears}</td>
                    <td style={td}>{fmt$(s.typicalAnnualAmount)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#ef4444" }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeTax)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: s.lifetimeIRMAA > 0 ? "#f59e0b" : "#8b949e" }}>{s.lifetimeIRMAA > 0 ? fmt$(s.lifetimeIRMAA) : "—"}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#a855f7" }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeSSTax)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#10b981", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(s.estateValue)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left" }}>
                      <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); onApply(s.typicalAnnualAmount, s.conversionYears); }}
                        style={{ background: "none", border: "1px solid #d0d7de", borderRadius: 4, padding: "2px 8px", fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", color: "#57606a" }}>Apply</button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#8b949e", marginTop: 6 }}>Click a row to see its lifetime charts below.</div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Lifetime income stream chart for selected strategy */}
      <Card title={`Lifetime Income Streams — ${selected.ceilingLabel}`}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          Shows how conversions, RMDs, Social Security, and taxable SS stack up year-by-year through retirement.
        </div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
          <BarChart data={lifetimeChartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} label={{ value: "Age", position: "insideBottom", offset: -2, fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <Legend />
            <Bar dataKey="Fed Tax"         fill="#ef4444" stackId="inc" radius={[0,0,0,0]} />
            <Bar dataKey="IRMAA Surcharge" fill="#f59e0b" stackId="inc" />
            <Bar dataKey="Taxable SS"      fill="#a855f7" stackId="inc" />
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* MAGI & SS inclusion rate */}
      <Card title={`MAGI & Social Security Taxation — ${selected.ceilingLabel}`}>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
          <LineChart data={lifetimeChartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis yAxisId="left"  tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis yAxisId="right" orientation="right" tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <Legend />
            <Line yAxisId="left"  type="monotone" dataKey="MAGI"       stroke="#3b82d4" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            <Line yAxisId="right" type="monotone" dataKey="Taxable SS"  stroke="#a855f7" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="4 3" />
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Estate / account balances */}
      <Card title={`Account Balances & Estate Value — ${selected.ceilingLabel}`}>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
          <LineChart data={lifetimeChartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <Legend />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Trad IRA"  stroke="#3b82d4" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Roth IRA"  stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Taxable"   stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="5 3" />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Estate"    stroke="#1f2328" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="3 2" />
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Year-by-year detail for selected strategy */}
      <Card title={`Year-by-Year Detail — ${selected.ceilingLabel}`}>
        <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12 }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                {["Age","Phase","Conversion","RMD","SS Benefit","Taxable SS","SS %","Fed Tax","MAGI","IRMAA","Trad IRA","Roth IRA","Estate"].map(h => (
                  <th key={h} style={{ padding: "6px 8px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {selected.lifetimeYears?.map((y, i) => {
                const phaseColor = y.phase === "conversion" ? "#dbeafe" : y.phase === "rmd" ? "#fef3c7" : "#fff";
                return (
                  <tr key={i} style={{ background: phaseColor }}>
                    <td style={td}>{y.age}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 10, padding: "1px 5px", borderRadius: 3, fontWeight: 600,
                        background: y.phase === "conversion" ? "#bfdbfe" : y.phase === "rmd" ? "#fde68a" : "#e5e7eb",
                        color: y.phase === "conversion" ? "#1e40af" : y.phase === "rmd" ? "#92400e" : "#57606a" }}>
                        {y.phase.toUpperCase()}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#3b82d4" }}>{y.conversionAmount > 0 ? fmt$(y.conversionAmount) : "—"}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#f59e0b" }}>{y.rmd > 0 ? fmt$(y.rmd) : "—"}</td>

                    <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.magi)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "center" }}>

        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
