### 🤖 Assistant

✅ Clean build — zero errors. Refresh your browser. Here's everything that's new:

---

### What the "⚡ Optimize for Me" tab now does

#### New inputs (Optimize mode only)
| Input | Purpose |
|---|---|
| **Annual SS Benefit** | Your gross Social Security benefit — used to compute how much becomes taxable income |
| **SS Start Age** | Age you start collecting (62–70) |
| **Project Through Age** | Lifetime end age (default 90) |
| **Max IRMAA Tier** | Hard ceiling on Medicare surcharge tier |

---

#### How the optimizer works now

1. **Tracks the actual IRA balance year-by-year** — each year it converts up to the bracket ceiling, then growth is applied to whatever remains. No more flat-amount × years approximation
2. **Social Security taxation** — uses the IRS provisional income formula (AGI + 50% SS) to calculate how much SS becomes taxable (0%, 50%, or 85%). A Roth conversion raises your MAGI which raises taxable SS — the optimizer accounts for this interaction
3. **Lifetime scoring** — scores every strategy by **total lifetime tax + IRMAA** from now through your projected end age, including RMD years and SS years — not just the conversion window
4. **Bracket-fill strategies** — tests filling to the top of every bracket (10%, 12%, 22%, 24%, 32%, 35%) plus every IRMAA threshold

---

#### What you see

| Section | What it shows |
|---|---|
| **✅ Optimal Strategy banner** | Best plan — annual amount, years, lifetime tax, IRMAA, estate at 90 |
| **Bracket Strategy Comparison chart** | Stacked bar — lifetime tax + IRMAA + taxable SS by bracket target |
| **Ranked table** | Click any row to load its detailed charts below |
| **Lifetime Income Streams** | Year-by-year stacked bar: federal tax, IRMAA surcharges, taxable SS |
| **MAGI & SS Taxation chart** | How MAGI and taxable SS move together over your lifetime |
| **Account Balances & Estate** | Trad IRA, Roth IRA, Taxable, Estate — from now to age 90 |
| **Year-by-year table** | Full detail: conversion, RMD, SS, taxable SS %, tax, IRMAA — color-coded by phase |

### 🤖 Assistant

✅ Clean build. Refresh your browser — the error is gone.

---

### What changed and why

**Root cause:** The default was `maxIRMAATier = 0` which filtered out every strategy where MAGI exceeded the IRMAA threshold. For a $500k IRA with other income, most meaningful conversion amounts push MAGI above $109k (single) or $218k (MFJ), so nothing survived the filter.

**Fixes:**

| Change | Why |
|---|---|
| Default changed from `Tier 0` → `No limit` | The optimizer already scores by lifetime cost — it won't recommend paying IRMAA unless doing so saves more in taxes over your lifetime |
| Dropdown reordered — "No limit" first | Makes it obvious this is the right starting point |
| Note updated | Explains the optimizer's actual logic: IRMAA is only accepted if it lowers your **total lifetime** bill |

**How the optimizer now thinks about IRMAA:**
- A strategy that pays $2k/year in IRMAA surcharges but saves $8k/year in income taxes during conversions → **recommended**
- A strategy that pays $3k/year in IRMAA for no tax benefit → **ranked lower**

If you want to hard-exclude IRMAA (e.g. you're already on Medicare and can't afford the surcharge), switch the dropdown to **"Tier 0 only"**.

> 🔧 called: read_file

Three call sites — all need an `age` parameter so the 2-year lookback and the under-65 rule can be applied. The cleanest fix is to add `age` to `calcIRMAA` itself, then a `magiForIrmaaYear` helper that maps income year → IRMAA year. But since the engine doesn't store a 2-year history of past MAGIs, the correct approach is:

- **Medicare starts at 65.** No IRMAA before age 65.
- **IRMAA in age Y is based on income from age Y-2.** So income at age 63 drives IRMAA at 65, income at 64 drives IRMAA at 66, etc.
- The engine needs to track a 2-year rolling MAGI history and apply it with the correct 2-year lag.

/**
 * Determine the 2026 Medicare IRMAA tier for a given MAGI.
 * @param {number} magi
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {{ tier, label, partBMonthly, partDMonthly, annualSurcharge, crossesThreshold }}
 */
export function calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;
  for (const t of tiers) {
    if (magi <= t.maxMAGI) {
      return {
        tier: t.tier,
        label: t.label,
        partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly,
        partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
        crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0,
      };
    }
  }
  // Fallback to highest tier
  const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
  return {
    tier: last.tier,
    label: last.label,
    partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly,
    partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
    annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
    crossesThreshold: true,
  };
}
=======
// Sentinel "no IRMAA" result — returned for any year where Medicare does not apply.
const NO_IRMAA = {
  tier: 0, label: "No IRMAA", partBMonthly: 0, partDMonthly: 0,
  annualSurcharge: 0, crossesThreshold: false, onMedicare: false,
};

/**
 * Determine the IRMAA tier that actually applies to a person in a given year.
 *
 * Medicare (and therefore IRMAA) only applies at age 65+.
 * IRMAA uses a 2-year lookback: the surcharge for year Y is based on the
 * MAGI reported on the tax return filed for year Y-2.
 *
 * Call this function with:
 *   magi         — the MAGI earned THIS year (will affect IRMAA two years later)
 *   filingStatus — filing status
 *   age          — the person's age THIS year (used to decide if Medicare applies)
 *   magiTwoYearsAgo — MAGI from two years prior (what drives THIS year's IRMAA)
 *                     Pass null/undefined if age < 65 or history not yet available.
 *
 * Returns two separate objects:
 *   currentIRMAA  — the IRMAA surcharge actually owed THIS year (based on 2-yr-ago MAGI)
 *   futureIRMAA   — the IRMAA that THIS year's MAGI will cause in two years (a preview)
 *
 * @param {number} magi
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @param {number} age
 * @param {number|null} magiTwoYearsAgo
 * @returns {{ currentIRMAA: object, futureIRMAA: object }}
 */
export function calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age = 65, magiTwoYearsAgo = null) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;

  function tierForMagi(m) {
    if (m == null || m <= 0) return { ...NO_IRMAA, onMedicare: true };
    for (const t of tiers) {
      if (m <= t.maxMAGI) {
        return {
          tier: t.tier, label: t.label,
          partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
          annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
          crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0, onMedicare: true,
        };
      }
    }
    const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
    return {
      tier: last.tier, label: last.label,
      partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
      annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
      crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,
    };
  }

  // Before Medicare eligibility — no IRMAA at all
  if (age < 65) {
    return {
      currentIRMAA: { ...NO_IRMAA },
      futureIRMAA:  tierForMagi(magi), // preview: this income will affect IRMAA at age 65+
    };
  }

  // On Medicare: current year's surcharge is driven by 2-years-ago MAGI
  const currentIRMAA = tierForMagi(magiTwoYearsAgo);
  // Future preview: this year's MAGI will drive IRMAA two years from now
  const futureIRMAA  = tierForMagi(magi);

  return { currentIRMAA, futureIRMAA };
}

/**
 * Backwards-compatible single-value wrapper used in tests and simple lookups.
 * Returns the IRMAA tier for a given MAGI regardless of age (no lookback).
 */
export function calcIRMAARaw(magi, filingStatus) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;
  for (const t of tiers) {
    if (magi <= t.maxMAGI) {
      return {
        tier: t.tier, label: t.label,
        partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
        crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0, onMedicare: true,
      };
    }
  }
  const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
  return {
    tier: last.tier, label: last.label,
    partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
    annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
    crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,
  };
}

### 🤖 Assistant

> 🔧 called: apply_diff

Found it. `runLifetimeProjection` is called with `firstYearConversion` — but `bracketFillSchedule` produces variable per-year amounts as the IRA shrinks. Using only year 1 understates conversions in later years and overstates the remaining Trad IRA, inflating lifetime taxes. The fix is to use the **average** conversion across the schedule (a better approximation) and also expose the actual schedule to `runLifetimeProjection` so it uses exact per-year amounts.

The cleanest fix: add a `conversionSchedule` override to `runLifetimeProjection` that uses exact per-year amounts from `bracketFillSchedule` instead of a flat amount:

### 🤖 Assistant

The engine is now correct. Here's the full explanation of what's happening with your numbers, and what the real optimum is:

---

## What's actually happening with your $2.7M IRA

### The core math problem with slow conversions

Your Traditional IRA **grows faster than you convert it**:
- $2.7M × 7% = **$189,000 growth per year**
- Converting $200k/yr removes $200k but adds $189k back in growth — net drain is only **$11k/yr**
- After 10 years of $200k conversions, the IRA is still **$2.35M+**
- Then **RMDs begin at age 75** on a still-massive balance — and you pay 37% tax on those for the rest of your life

### Correct numbers with your exact inputs (age 63, born 1961, MFJ)

| Strategy | Yrs | $/yr | Lifetime Tax | Lifetime IRMAA | **Total Cost** | Estate at 90 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **✅ Best: Fill 37% bracket** | **4** | **$900k** | $1,792k | $46k | **$1,838k** | **$17.95M** |
| Fill 35% bracket | 5 | $675k | $1,793k | $53k | $1,846k | $17.95M |
| IRMAA Tier 4 ceiling | 6 | $570k | $1,796k | $57k | $1,852k | $17.95M |
| Manual $200k × 10 yrs | 10 | $200k | $2,042k | $123k | **$2,165k** | $13.58M |
| No conversion | 0 | $0 | $2,722k | $90k | $2,812k | $7.89M |

### Why the optimizer recommends $900k/yr for 4 years

The numbers seem backwards — more tax per year but less total — because:

1. **You pay 37% now** on conversions, which is painful upfront
2. **But RMDs at age 75 would also be taxed at 37%** on an even larger balance (the IRA keeps growing)
3. **Fast conversion drains the Trad IRA completely** — zero RMDs forever
4. **The Roth IRA then grows tax-free for 20+ years** — that's worth ~$4.4M more in estate value vs the slow plan

### The UI numbers you saw were wrong — now fixed

The bug: the optimizer was using **only year 1's conversion amount** when projecting lifetime costs, instead of the exact year-by-year schedule (where conversions shrink as the IRA drains). This made aggressive short strategies appear more expensive than they really are. The fix passes the exact per-year schedule to the lifetime projection.

**Refresh your browser** — the Optimize tab will now show the correct numbers matching the table above.

The engines are **perfectly consistent** for the conversion years. The difference is by design — here's the exact breakdown:

---

## They ARE showing the same numbers — just different scopes

| | Manual Simulator | Optimize for Me |
|---|---|---|
| **What it shows** | Conversion window only (6 years, age 60–65) | Full lifetime (age 60–90) |
| **Total Tax** | $897,479 | $1,726,933 |
| **IRMAA** | $5,826 | $15,887 |
| **Covers** | Years 1–6 only | Years 1–31 (including RMDs, SS, all of retirement) |

The **conversion-phase numbers match exactly**:

| Year | Age | Conversion | Tax | IRMAA |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 60 | $570,000 | $169,748 | $0 (not on Medicare) |
| 2 | 61 | $570,000 | $169,748 | $0 |
| 3 | 62 | $570,000 | $169,748 | $0 |
| 4 | 63 | $570,000 | $169,748 | $0 |
| 5 | 64 | $570,000 | $169,748 | $0 |
| 6 | 65 | $207,277 | $48,742 | $5,826 |
| **Total** | | **$3,057,277** | **$897,479** | **$5,826** |

The **extra $829k in lifetime tax** shown in Optimize comes from **years 7–31** — taxes on RMDs from the remaining Trad IRA balance, taxes on Social Security once it starts, and IRMAA from those years. The Manual tab doesn't show those years because its scope is only the conversion window.

## The answer to your question

**Yes, they should show the same numbers for the conversion window — and they do.** Both engines produce identical results for years 1–6 with your inputs:

| Year | Age | Converted | Tax | IRMAA |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1–5 | 60–64 | $570,000 | $169,748 | $0 (not on Medicare) |
| 6 | 65 | $207,277 | $48,742 | $5,826 |
| **Window total** | | **$3,057,277** | **$897,479** | **$5,826** ✅ |

| | Manual Simulator | Optimize for Me |
|---|---|---|
| **Scope** | Conversion years only (6 yrs) | Full lifetime to age 90 (31 yrs) |
| **Tax shown** | $897,479 | $1,726,933 |
| **Extra $829k** | Not shown | RMDs + SS taxation at ages 66–90 |
| **IRMAA shown** | $5,826 | $15,887 |
| **Extra $10k** | Not shown | Medicare surcharges at ages 66–90 |

// ─── Main App ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// ─── Tax brackets reference (2024) ───────────────────────────────────────────
const BRACKETS = {
  single: [
    { rate: 0.10, max: 11600 },
    { rate: 0.12, max: 47150 },
    { rate: 0.22, max: 100525 },
    { rate: 0.24, max: 191950 },
    { rate: 0.32, max: 243725 },
    { rate: 0.35, max: 609350 },
  ],
  mfj: [
    { rate: 0.10, max: 23200 },
    { rate: 0.12, max: 94300 },
    { rate: 0.22, max: 201050 },
    { rate: 0.24, max: 383900 },
    { rate: 0.32, max: 487450 },
    { rate: 0.35, max: 731200 },
  ],
};
const STD_DED = { single: 14600, mfj: 29200 };
const BRACKET_COLORS = {
  "10%": "#6ee7b7", "12%": "#34d399", "22%": "#3b82d4",
  "24%": "#7c5cd8", "32%": "#f59e0b", "35%": "#ef4444", "37%": "#991b1b",
};

// ─── Bracket Comparison panel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
function BracketPanel({ baseInputs, ssIncome, ssStartAge, endAge, onApply }) {
  const [selectedBracket, setSelectedBracket] = useState(null);

  // Build one scenario per bracket ceiling
  const scenarios = useMemo(() => {
    const { filingStatus, otherOrdinaryIncome = 0 } = baseInputs;
    const brackets = BRACKETS[filingStatus];
    const stdDed   = STD_DED[filingStatus];
    const results  = [];

    for (const b of brackets) {
      const label     = `${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}%`;
      const magiTop   = b.max + stdDed + otherOrdinaryIncome; // MAGI at bracket top
      const annual    = Math.max(0, magiTop - otherOrdinaryIncome); // max conversion
      if (annual <= 0) continue;

      // How many years to drain the IRA at this rate (capped at years to RMD)
      const rmdAge    = baseInputs.birthYear
        ? (baseInputs.birthYear <= 1950 ? 72 : baseInputs.birthYear <= 1959 ? 73 : 75)
        : 75;
      const maxYrs    = Math.max(1, rmdAge - (baseInputs.currentAge ?? 60));
      const numYears  = Math.min(Math.ceil(baseInputs.traditionalIRABalance / annual), maxYrs);

      try {
        const lifetime = runLifetimeProjection({
          ...baseInputs,
          annualRothConversion:  annual,
          conversionYears:       numYears,
          socialSecurityBenefit: ssIncome,
          ssStartAge,
          endAge,
        });
        results.push({
          label,
          rate:         b.rate,
          magiTop:      Math.round(magiTop),
          annualAmount: Math.round(annual),
          numYears,
          lifetimeTax:  lifetime.summary.totalLifetimeTax,
          lifetimeIRMAA:lifetime.summary.totalLifetimeIRMAA,
          lifetimeSSTax:lifetime.summary.totalTaxableSS,
          lifetimeCost: lifetime.summary.totalLifetimeCost,
          estateValue:  lifetime.summary.estateValueAtEndAge,
          finalTradIRA: lifetime.summary.finalTraditionalIRA,
          finalRothIRA: lifetime.summary.finalRothIRA,
          years:        lifetime.years,
        });
      } catch (_) { /* skip invalid */ }
    }
    return results;
  }, [baseInputs, ssIncome, ssStartAge, endAge]);

  const best = scenarios.length > 0
    ? scenarios.reduce((a, b) => a.lifetimeCost < b.lifetimeCost ? a : b)
    : null;

  const active = selectedBracket
    ? scenarios.find(s => s.label === selectedBracket)
    : best;

  // Comparison bar chart data
  const barData = scenarios.map(s => ({
    name:            `${s.label} bracket`,
    "Lifetime Tax":  Math.round(s.lifetimeTax),
    "Lifetime IRMAA":Math.round(s.lifetimeIRMAA),
    "SS Taxed":      Math.round(s.lifetimeSSTax),
    estateValue:     Math.round(s.estateValue),
    label:           s.label,
  }));

  // Lifetime chart for active scenario
  const lifetimeData = active?.years.map(y => ({
    age:             y.age,
    "Trad IRA":      Math.round(y.endingTraditionalIRA),
    "Roth IRA":      Math.round(y.endingRothIRA),
    "Taxable":       Math.round(y.endingTaxableBrokerage),
    "Fed Tax":       Math.round(y.totalFederalTax),
    "IRMAA":         Math.round(y.irmaa?.annualSurcharge ?? 0),
    "Taxable SS":    Math.round(y.taxableSS ?? 0),
    "MAGI":          Math.round(y.magi),
  })) ?? [];

  if (scenarios.length === 0) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20, color: "#57606a" }}>Enter your inputs on the left to see bracket strategies.</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>

      {/* Strategy selector chips */}
      <Card title="Select a Tax Bracket Target">
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 14 }}>
          Each strategy converts the maximum amount that keeps your MAGI within the top of that bracket each year, repeating until the Traditional IRA is drained. Click a bracket to explore it.
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 10, marginBottom: 16 }}>
          {scenarios.map(s => {
            const isBest   = s.label === best?.label;
            const isActive = s.label === (active?.label);
            return (
              <button key={s.label} onClick={() => setSelectedBracket(s.label)}
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 18px", borderRadius: 8, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600,
                  background: isActive ? (BRACKET_COLORS[s.label] ?? "#3b82d4") : "#f7f8fa",
                  color:      isActive ? "#fff" : "#1f2328",
                  border:     isActive ? `2px solid ${BRACKET_COLORS[s.label] ?? "#3b82d4"}` : "2px solid #e5e7eb",
                  position:   "relative",
                }}>
                {isBest && <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: -8, right: -8, background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 10, padding: "1px 6px", fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700 }}>★ BEST</span>}
                {s.label} bracket
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 400, marginTop: 2, opacity: 0.85 }}>
                  {s.numYears} yr{s.numYears !== 1 ? "s" : ""} · {fmt$(s.annualAmount)}/yr
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Summary comparison table */}
        <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 13 }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                {["Bracket","Annual Conv.","Years","MAGI ceiling","Lifetime Tax","Lifetime IRMAA","SS Taxed","Total Cost","Estate @90","Apply"].map(h => (
                  <th key={h} style={{ padding: "7px 10px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap", textAlign: h === "Bracket" ? "left" : "right" }}>{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {scenarios.map((s, i) => {
                const isBest   = s.label === best?.label;
                const isActive = s.label === active?.label;
                return (
                  <tr key={i} onClick={() => setSelectedBracket(s.label)}
                    style={{ background: isActive ? "#eff6ff" : isBest ? "#f0fdf4" : i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left" }}>
                      <span style={{ display: "inline-block", width: 10, height: 10, borderRadius: 2, background: BRACKET_COLORS[s.label] ?? "#ccc", marginRight: 6 }} />
                      <strong>{s.label}</strong>
                      {isBest && <span style={{ marginLeft: 6, fontSize: 10, background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 3, padding: "1px 5px", fontWeight: 700 }}>BEST</span>}
                    </td>
                    <td style={td}>{fmt$(s.annualAmount)}</td>
                    <td style={td}>{s.numYears}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#57606a" }}>{fmt$(s.magiTop)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#ef4444" }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeTax)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: s.lifetimeIRMAA > 0 ? "#f59e0b" : "#8b949e" }}>{s.lifetimeIRMAA > 0 ? fmt$(s.lifetimeIRMAA) : "—"}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#a855f7" }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeSSTax)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, fontWeight: isActive ? 700 : 400, color: isBest ? "#166534" : undefined }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeCost)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#10b981", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(s.estateValue)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left" }}>
                      <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); onApply(s.annualAmount, s.numYears); }}
                        style={{ background: isBest ? "#16a34a" : "none", color: isBest ? "#fff" : "#57606a", border: isBest ? "none" : "1px solid #d0d7de", borderRadius: 5, padding: "3px 10px", fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                        Apply
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Side-by-side lifetime cost bar chart */}
      <Card title="Lifetime Cost by Bracket — Tax + IRMAA + Taxable SS">
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>Stacked bars show how much of your lifetime income goes to tax under each strategy. Lower = better.</div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
          <BarChart data={barData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} />
            <Legend />
            <Bar dataKey="Lifetime Tax"   fill="#ef4444" stackId="a" radius={[0,0,0,0]} />
            <Bar dataKey="Lifetime IRMAA" fill="#f59e0b" stackId="a" />
            <Bar dataKey="SS Taxed"       fill="#a855f7" stackId="a" radius={[3,3,0,0]} />
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Estate value comparison */}
      <Card title="Estate Value at Age 90 by Bracket">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
          <BarChart data={barData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000000).toFixed(1) + "M"} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} />
            <Bar dataKey="estateValue" name="Estate Value" fill="#10b981" radius={[4,4,0,0]} />
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Detailed lifetime view for selected bracket */}
      {active && (
        <>
          <Card title={`${active.label} Bracket — Lifetime Account Balances`}
            accent={BRACKET_COLORS[active.label] ?? "#3b82d4"}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>
              Converting <strong>{fmt$(active.annualAmount)}/yr</strong> for <strong>{active.numYears} years</strong> · MAGI ceiling <strong>{fmt$(active.magiTop)}</strong> · Lifetime cost <strong>{fmt$(active.lifetimeCost)}</strong>
            </div>
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
              <LineChart data={lifetimeData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
                <Legend />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Trad IRA" stroke="#3b82d4" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Roth IRA" stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Taxable"  stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="5 3" />
              </LineChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </Card>

          <Card title={`${active.label} Bracket — Annual Tax, IRMAA & Taxable SS`}>
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
              <BarChart data={lifetimeData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
                <Legend />
                <Bar dataKey="Fed Tax"    fill="#ef4444" stackId="b" />
                <Bar dataKey="IRMAA"      fill="#f59e0b" stackId="b" />
                <Bar dataKey="Taxable SS" fill="#a855f7" stackId="b" radius={[3,3,0,0]} />
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </Card>

          {/* Year-by-year table */}
          <Card title={`${active.label} Bracket — Year-by-Year Detail`}>
            <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
              <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12 }}>
                <thead>
                  <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                    {["Age","Phase","Conversion","MAGI","Fed Tax","Eff. Rate","IRMAA","Taxable SS","Trad IRA","Roth IRA","Estate"].map(h => (
                      <th key={h} style={{ padding: "6px 8px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{h}</th>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {active.years.map((y, i) => {
                    const bg = y.phase === "conversion" ? "#dbeafe" : y.phase === "rmd" ? "#fef3c7" : "#fff";
                    return (
                      <tr key={i} style={{ background: bg }}>
                        <td style={td}>{y.age}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "center" }}>
                          <span style={{ fontSize: 10, padding: "1px 5px", borderRadius: 3, fontWeight: 600,
                            background: y.phase === "conversion" ? "#bfdbfe" : y.phase === "rmd" ? "#fde68a" : "#e5e7eb",
                            color: y.phase === "conversion" ? "#1e40af" : y.phase === "rmd" ? "#92400e" : "#57606a" }}>
                            {y.phase.toUpperCase()}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#3b82d4" }}>{y.conversionAmount > 0 ? fmt$(y.conversionAmount) : "—"}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.magi)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#ef4444" }}>{fmt$(y.totalFederalTax)}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmtPct(y.effectiveRate)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: y.irmaa?.crossesThreshold ? "#f59e0b" : "#8b949e" }}>
                          {y.onMedicare ? (y.irmaa?.annualSurcharge > 0 ? fmt$(y.irmaa.annualSurcharge) : "—") : "N/A"}
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#a855f7" }}>{y.taxableSS > 0 ? fmt$(y.taxableSS) : "—"}</td>
                        <td style={td}>{fmt$(y.endingTraditionalIRA)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, color: "#10b981", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(y.endingRothIRA)}</td>
                        <td style={{ ...td, fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(y.estateValue)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 16, marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12, color: "#57606a" }}>
              <span style={{ background: "#bfdbfe", padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 3 }}>Blue = Conversion</span>
              <span style={{ background: "#fde68a", padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 3 }}>Yellow = RMD</span>
              <span style={{ background: "#e5e7eb", padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 3 }}>Gray = Gap</span>
            </div>
          </Card>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ─── Main App ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Previous conversation summary:

---

## Goal
Build a complete multi-year **Roth Conversion & Retirement Tax Modeling** web application for Track 1 of the "Building with IBM Bob" hackathon. The tool models year-by-year Roth conversion strategies, minimizes lifetime taxes + IRMAA surcharges + Social Security taxes, and helps users find the optimal conversion amount and duration.

## Instructions
- Use React + Vite frontend with Recharts for charts
- Core calculation engine in pure JavaScript (`simulationEngine.js`) — no dependencies
- All tax rules must be accurate: 2024 federal brackets, 2026 IRMAA thresholds, SECURE 2.0 RMD ages, IRS provisional income SS taxation formula, 2-year IRMAA lookback
- Manual Simulator shows conversion-window-only numbers; Optimize and Bracket tabs show **lifetime** numbers (age → endAge) — these are intentionally different scopes, not a bug
- "Apply to Simulator" buttons in Optimize and Bracket tabs copy values into the Manual tab
- Taxes are paid from the Taxable Brokerage account; optional toggle to pay from conversion itself
- Build must pass with zero errors (chunk size warning is acceptable)

## Discoveries
- **The optimizer was using only year-1 conversion amount** for lifetime projections instead of the exact per-year schedule from `bracketFillSchedule` — fixed by adding `conversionSchedule` parameter to `runLifetimeProjection`
- **IRMAA 2-year lookback**: `calcIRMAA` now takes `age` and `magiTwoYearsAgo` — ages < 65 get `NO_IRMAA` for current year; a `futureIRMAA` preview is returned for pre-65 rows. `calcIRMAARaw` is the backwards-compatible single-value wrapper used in tests
- **Slow conversions on large IRAs cost more lifetime tax**: A $2.7M IRA growing at 7% adds ~$189k/yr — converting $200k/yr barely drains it, leaving a massive RMD burden later. The optimizer correctly recommends aggressive short strategies (e.g. $900k/yr × 4 yrs) because the Trad IRA RMDs at 37% for 15+ years exceed the upfront conversion tax cost
- **Default `maxIRMAATier` must be 5** (no limit) — setting it to 0 caused "no valid strategy found" errors for large IRA balances where every bracket-fill pushes MAGI above IRMAA thresholds
- **`InputField` bug**: `type="number"` caused `010` on backspace — fixed by switching to `type="text"` with `inputMode="numeric"`, local `draft` state, and `onBlur` to commit clean numbers
- SECURE 2.0 RMD ages: born ≤1950 → 72, born 1951–1959 → 73, born ≥1960 → 75

## Accomplished

### ✅ Completed
- **`simulationEngine.js`** — full engine with:
  - `calcFederalTax`, `calcTaxRates` (2024 brackets)
  - `calcIRMAA` (age-aware, 2-year lookback, returns `{currentIRMAA, futureIRMAA}`), `calcIRMAARaw`
  - `calcRMD`, `rmdStartAge` (SECURE 2.0)
  - `calcSSTax` (IRS provisional income formula, 0%/50%/85% inclusion)
  - `runSimulation` — conversion window only, year-by-year, with brokerage exhaustion tracking
  - `runLifetimeProjection` — age→endAge full retirement model including RMDs, SS taxation, estate value; supports `conversionSchedule` for exact per-year amounts
  - `bracketFillSchedule` — internal helper that tracks actual IRA balance year-by-year per bracket ceiling
  - `optimizeConversion` — tests all bracket ceilings + IRMAA boundaries + equal-spread options; scores by **lifetime** cost; returns `{strategies, recommended, bracketComparison}` sorted by lifetime cost

- **`src/App.jsx`** — three-tab React dashboard:
  - **📊 Manual Simulator**: inputs panel + conversion-window summary cards + 4 charts + year-by-year table with IRMAA lookback badges
  - **🎯 Bracket Comparison** *(just added)*: `BracketPanel` component — bracket selector chips with ★ BEST badge, summary comparison table, stacked lifetime cost bar chart, estate value bar chart, per-bracket lifetime balance/tax/detail charts + year-by-year table; "Apply" button sends to Manual tab
  - **⚡ Optimize for Me**: `OptimizerPanel` — ranked strategy table (click row to load charts), lifetime income streams chart, MAGI/SS chart, estate chart, full year-by-year table with phase color coding

- **`test.mjs`** — 29-test validation suite (all passing):
  - Federal tax spot checks
  - IRMAA raw tier checks + age-aware lookback checks (age 63 = N/A, age 65 with/without lookback MAGI)
  - SECURE 2.0 `rmdStartAge` boundary tests
  - `calcRMD` with all 3 birth-year cohorts
  - Single filer IRMAA breach detection
  - `runSimulation` RMD start age integration

- **`package.json`** — React 18 + Vite 5 + Recharts 3
- **`index.html`**, **`vite.config.js`**, **`src/main.jsx`**, **`src/index.css`** — standard Vite/React scaffold

### 🟡 Just completed (needs browser refresh to verify)
- **Bracket Comparison tab** fully wired up — `BracketPanel` renders all 6 brackets (10%–35%) side by side with lifetime cost, estate value, and drill-down charts

### 🔲 Potential next steps (not started)

- Social Security benefit optimization (claiming age 62 vs 67 vs 70 comparison)

- Side-by-side scenario comparison (e.g. run two strategies simultaneously)

```
├── package.json                  # React 18, Vite 5, Recharts 3 — "type": "module"

    ├── main.jsx                  # React root render
    ├── index.css                 # Global reset + body styles
```

### Key exports from `simulationEngine.js`
| Export | Description |
|---|---|
| `runSimulation(inputs)` | Conversion window only — used by Manual tab |
| `runLifetimeProjection(inputs)` | Full age→endAge projection — used by Optimize + Bracket tabs |
| `optimizeConversion(inputs, opts)` | Bracket-fill optimizer — used by Optimize tab |
| `calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus)` | Federal tax calculation |
| `calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiTwoYearsAgo)` | Age-aware IRMAA with 2-yr lookback |
| `calcIRMAARaw(magi, filingStatus)` | Simple IRMAA tier lookup (no age/lookback) |
| `calcRMD(balance, age, birthYear)` | RMD with SECURE 2.0 start age |
| `rmdStartAge(birthYear)` | Returns 72/73/75 per SECURE 2.0 |
| `calcSSTax(ssBenefit, otherAGI, filingStatus)` | SS provisional income taxation |

```powershell
npm run dev
# open http://localhost:5173

# Terminal 2 — engine tests
cd c:\cora\roth-optimizer-hackathon
node test.mjs
```

    // --- Federal tax ---
    const grossIncome   = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversionAmount;
    const taxableIncome = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDeduction);
    const totalTax      = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    const taxWithoutConversion = calcFederalTax(
      Math.max(0, otherOrdinaryIncome - stdDeduction),
      filingStatus
    );
    const conversionTax = totalTax - taxWithoutConversion;

    const { effectiveRate, marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    // --- MAGI & IRMAA with 2-year lookback ---
    // currentIRMAA = what the person actually pays this year (based on income 2 yrs ago)
    // futureIRMAA  = what this year's income will cause in 2 years (a preview)
    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa, futureIRMAA } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistory[0]);
    // Advance the history window
    magiHistory[0] = magiHistory[1];
    magiHistory[1] = magi;

    // --- Apply conversion (deduct from Trad, add to Roth) ---
    tradIRA -= conversionAmount;

    // Taxes paid from taxable brokerage (capped at available balance)
    const taxPaidFromBrokerage = Math.min(conversionTax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaidFromBrokerage;

    // Shortfall: tax the brokerage couldn't cover
    const taxShortfall = conversionTax - taxPaidFromBrokerage;
    const brokerageExhausted = taxShortfall > 0;
=======
    // --- Federal + State tax ---
    const grossIncome    = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversionAmount;
    const taxableIncome  = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDeduction);
    const federalTax     = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);
    const stateTax       = grossIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const totalTax       = federalTax + stateTax;

    const baseGross     = otherOrdinaryIncome;
    const federalBase   = calcFederalTax(Math.max(0, baseGross - stdDeduction), filingStatus);
    const stateBase     = baseGross * stateTaxRate;
    const conversionTax = (federalTax + stateTax) - (federalBase + stateBase);

    const { effectiveRate, marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    // --- MAGI & IRMAA with 2-year lookback ---
    // currentIRMAA = what the person actually pays this year (based on income 2 yrs ago)
    // futureIRMAA  = what this year's income will cause in 2 years (a preview)
    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa, futureIRMAA } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistory[0]);
    // Advance the history window
    magiHistory[0] = magiHistory[1];
    magiHistory[1] = magi;

    tradIRA -= conversionAmount;

    const taxPaidFromBrokerage = Math.min(totalTax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaidFromBrokerage;

    const taxShortfall = totalTax - taxPaidFromBrokerage;

const td = {
  padding: "7px 10px",
  textAlign: "right",
  borderBottom: "1px solid #f0f0f0",
  whiteSpace: "nowrap",
};

// ─── CSV export utility ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function exportCSV(rows, headers, filename) {
  const esc = v => {
    if (v == null) return "";
    const s = String(v);
    return s.includes(",") || s.includes('"') || s.includes("\n")
      ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`
      : s;
  };
  const lines = [
    headers.map(esc).join(","),
    ...rows.map(row => headers.map(h => esc(row[h])).join(",")),
  ];
  const blob = new Blob([lines.join("\n")], { type: "text/csv" });
  const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a    = document.createElement("a");
  a.href     = url;
  a.download = filename;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

// ─── Break-even panel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function BreakEvenPanel({ baseInputs, ssIncome, ssStartAge, endAge, onApply }) {
  const breakEvenData = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return calcBreakEven({
        ...baseInputs,
        socialSecurityBenefit: ssIncome,
        ssStartAge,
        endAge,
      });
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }, [baseInputs, ssIncome, ssStartAge, endAge]);

  if (!breakEvenData) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20, color: "#57606a" }}>Enter inputs on the left to see break-even analysis.</div>;
  }

  const { breakEvenAge, years } = breakEvenData;
  const chartData = years.map(y => ({
    age:         y.age,
    "Roth Strategy":  y.rothEstate,
    "Do Nothing":     y.baselineEstate,
    "Advantage":      y.advantage,
  }));

  const rothFinal    = years[years.length - 1]?.rothEstate ?? 0;
  const baseFinal    = years[years.length - 1]?.baselineEstate ?? 0;
  const lastAdvantage = years[years.length - 1]?.advantage ?? 0;

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>

      {/* Hero banner */}
      <div style={{
        background: breakEvenAge != null ? "linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#dcfce7)" : "linear-gradient(135deg,#fef9f0,#fef3c7)",
        border: `1px solid ${breakEvenAge != null ? "#86efac" : "#fcd34d"}`,
        borderRadius: 10, padding: "20px 24px",
      }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: breakEvenAge != null ? "#166534" : "#92400e", marginBottom: 12 }}>
          {breakEvenAge != null
            ? `✅ Roth strategy surpasses do-nothing at age ${breakEvenAge}`
            : "⚠ Roth strategy does not overtake do-nothing within the projection window"}
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4,1fr)", gap: 14 }}>
          <StatBox label="Break-Even Age"      value={breakEvenAge ?? "N/A"} color={breakEvenAge ? "#166534" : "#92400e"} />
          <StatBox label={`Roth Estate (${endAge})`}     value={fmt$(rothFinal)}  color="#10b981" />
          <StatBox label={`Baseline Estate (${endAge})`} value={fmt$(baseFinal)}  color="#8b949e" />
          <StatBox label="Net Advantage"        value={fmt$(lastAdvantage)} color={lastAdvantage >= 0 ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444"} />
        </div>
        {breakEvenAge != null && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 12, fontSize: 13, color: "#166534" }}>
            Despite paying upfront conversion taxes, the Roth strategy's tax-free growth and lower RMD burden creates a net worth advantage that begins at age <strong>{breakEvenAge}</strong> and compounds through retirement.
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Net worth comparison chart */}
      <Card title="Total Net Worth: Roth Strategy vs Do-Nothing Baseline">
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          Shows cumulative total estate value (Traditional + Roth + Taxable) for each strategy. The cross-over is the break-even point.
        </div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
          <LineChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} label={{ value: "Age", position: "insideBottom", offset: -2, fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <Legend />
            {breakEvenAge != null && (
              <ReferenceLine x={breakEvenAge} stroke="#16a34a" strokeDasharray="6 3"
                label={{ value: `Break-even ${breakEvenAge}`, position: "top", fontSize: 11, fill: "#16a34a" }} />
            )}
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Roth Strategy" stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2.5} dot={false} />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="Do Nothing"    stroke="#8b949e" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="5 3" />
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Advantage / deficit area chart */}
      <Card title="Roth Advantage Over Do-Nothing (annual net worth delta)">
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          Green = Roth strategy is ahead. Red = do-nothing is still ahead. The transition from red to green is the break-even age.
        </div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
          <AreaChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#374151" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            {breakEvenAge != null && (
              <ReferenceLine x={breakEvenAge} stroke="#16a34a" strokeDasharray="5 3" />
            )}
            <Area type="monotone" dataKey="Advantage" stroke="#10b981" fill="#bbf7d0"
              strokeWidth={2} dot={false}
              baseValue={0} />
          </AreaChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Year-by-year table with CSV export */}
      <Card title="Year-by-Year Break-Even Detail">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginBottom: 8 }}>
          <button onClick={() => exportCSV(
            years.map(y => ({
              Age: y.age,
              "Roth Estate": y.rothEstate,
              "Baseline Estate": y.baselineEstate,
              "Net Advantage": y.advantage,
              "Cumulative Tax Delta": y.cumulativeTaxDelta,
            })),
            ["Age","Roth Estate","Baseline Estate","Net Advantage","Cumulative Tax Delta"],
            "break-even.csv"
          )}
            style={{ background: "#3b82d4", color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 6, padding: "6px 14px", fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 600 }}>
            ⬇ Export CSV
          </button>
        </div>
        <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12 }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                {["Age","Roth Estate","Do-Nothing Estate","Net Advantage","Cumulative Tax Delta"].map(h => (
                  <th key={h} style={{ padding: "6px 10px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {years.map((y, i) => (
                <tr key={i} style={{ background: y.advantage >= 0 ? "#f0fdf4" : i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa" }}>
                  <td style={td}>{y.age}{y.age === breakEvenAge ? " ⭐" : ""}</td>
                  <td style={{ ...td, color: "#10b981", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(y.rothEstate)}</td>
                  <td style={{ ...td, color: "#57606a" }}>{fmt$(y.baselineEstate)}</td>
                  <td style={{ ...td, color: y.advantage >= 0 ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444", fontWeight: 600 }}>
                    {y.advantage >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{fmt$(y.advantage)}
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ ...td, color: y.cumulativeTaxDelta < 0 ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444" }}>
                    {y.cumulativeTaxDelta >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{fmt$(y.cumulativeTaxDelta)}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─── Social Security claiming comparison panel ────────────────────────────────
function SSClaimingPanel({ baseInputs, fraAnnualBenefit, endAge }) {
  const result = useMemo(() => {
    if (!fraAnnualBenefit || fraAnnualBenefit <= 0) return null;
    try {
      return calcSSClaimingComparison({
        ...baseInputs,
        socialSecurityBenefit: fraAnnualBenefit,
        endAge,
      });
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }, [baseInputs, fraAnnualBenefit, endAge]);

  const [selectedAge, setSelectedAge] = useState(67);

  if (!fraAnnualBenefit || fraAnnualBenefit <= 0) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20, color: "#57606a" }}>Enter an annual SS benefit (FRA amount at 67) in the inputs panel to see the claiming age comparison.</div>;
  }
  if (!result) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20, color: "#57606a" }}>Computing…</div>;
  }

  const { scenarios } = result;
  const selected = scenarios.find(s => s.claimAge === selectedAge) ?? scenarios[2];
  const bestEstate = scenarios.reduce((a, b) => a.estateValue > b.estateValue ? a : b);
  const bestSS     = scenarios.reduce((a, b) => a.totalSS > b.totalSS ? a : b);
  const bestCost   = scenarios.reduce((a, b) => a.lifetimeCost < b.lifetimeCost ? a : b);

  const COLORS = { 62: "#f59e0b", 65: "#3b82d4", 67: "#7c5cd8", 70: "#10b981" };
  const LABELS = { 62: "Early (62)", 65: "Age 65", 67: "FRA (67)", 70: "Maximum (70)" };

  // Summary comparison bar data
  const barData = scenarios.map(s => ({
    name:    LABELS[s.claimAge] ?? `Age ${s.claimAge}`,
    "Total SS Received":  Math.round(s.totalSS),
    "Lifetime Tax":       Math.round(s.lifetimeTax),
    "Lifetime IRMAA":     Math.round(s.lifetimeIRMAA),
    "Estate Value":       Math.round(s.estateValue),
    claimAge: s.claimAge,
  }));

  // Lifetime chart for selected scenario
  const lifetimeData = selected.years.map(y => ({
    age:          y.age,
    "SS Benefit": Math.round(y.ssBenefit),
    "Taxable SS": Math.round(y.taxableSS ?? 0),
    "MAGI":       Math.round(y.magi),
    "Estate":     Math.round(y.estateValue),
    "Fed Tax":    Math.round(y.totalFederalTax),
  }));

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>

      {/* Claiming age chip selector */}
      <Card title="Select a Claiming Age to Explore">
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 14 }}>
          Based on an FRA (age 67) benefit of <strong>{fmt$(fraAnnualBenefit)}/yr</strong> ({fmt$(fraAnnualBenefit / 12)}/mo).
          The SSA adjusts your benefit based on when you claim relative to your Full Retirement Age.
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 10, marginBottom: 16 }}>
          {scenarios.map(s => {
            const isActive  = s.claimAge === selectedAge;
            const isBestEst = s.claimAge === bestEstate.claimAge;
            const color     = COLORS[s.claimAge] ?? "#57606a";
            return (
              <button key={s.claimAge} onClick={() => setSelectedAge(s.claimAge)}
                style={{
                  padding: "12px 18px", borderRadius: 8, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600,
                  background: isActive ? color : "#f7f8fa",
                  color:      isActive ? "#fff" : "#1f2328",
                  border:     isActive ? `2px solid ${color}` : "2px solid #e5e7eb",
                  position:   "relative",
                }}>
                {isBestEst && <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: -8, right: -8, background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 10, padding: "1px 6px", fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700 }}>★ BEST</span>}
                {LABELS[s.claimAge] ?? `Age ${s.claimAge}`}
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 400, marginTop: 3, opacity: 0.9 }}>
                  {fmt$(s.monthlyBenefit)}/mo · {fmt$(s.annualBenefit)}/yr
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Summary comparison table */}
        <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 13 }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: "#f7f8fa" }}>
                {["Claim Age","Monthly","Annual","Total SS Received","Lifetime Tax","IRMAA","SS Taxed","Total Cost","Estate @"+endAge].map(h => (
                  <th key={h} style={{ padding: "7px 10px", textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 600, color: "#57606a", borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {scenarios.map((s, i) => {
                const isBest   = s.claimAge === bestEstate.claimAge;
                const isActive = s.claimAge === selectedAge;
                return (
                  <tr key={i} onClick={() => setSelectedAge(s.claimAge)}
                    style={{ background: isActive ? "#eff6ff" : isBest ? "#f0fdf4" : i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "right", fontWeight: 700 }}>
                      <span style={{ display: "inline-block", width: 10, height: 10, borderRadius: 2, background: COLORS[s.claimAge] ?? "#ccc", marginRight: 6 }} />
                      Age {s.claimAge}
                      {isBest && <span style={{ marginLeft: 6, fontSize: 10, background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 3, padding: "1px 5px", fontWeight: 700 }}>BEST</span>}
                    </td>
                    <td style={td}>{fmt$(s.monthlyBenefit)}</td>
                    <td style={td}>{fmt$(s.annualBenefit)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: COLORS[s.claimAge] ?? "#57606a", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(s.totalSS)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#ef4444" }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeTax)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: s.lifetimeIRMAA > 0 ? "#f59e0b" : "#8b949e" }}>{s.lifetimeIRMAA > 0 ? fmt$(s.lifetimeIRMAA) : "—"}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#a855f7" }}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeSSTax)}</td>
                    <td style={td}>{fmt$(s.lifetimeCost)}</td>
                    <td style={{ ...td, color: "#10b981", fontWeight: 600 }}>{fmt$(s.estateValue)}</td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Estate comparison bar */}
      <Card title="Estate Value by Claiming Age">
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          Higher estate = more wealth transferred. This is driven by delaying SS to avoid taxable SS income, which reduces IRMAA and taxes during RMD years.
        </div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
          <BarChart data={barData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000000).toFixed(2) + "M"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} />
            <Bar dataKey="Estate Value" fill="#10b981" radius={[4,4,0,0]}
              label={{ position: "top", formatter: v => "$" + (v/1000).toFixed(0)+"k", fontSize: 10, fill: "#1f2328" }} />
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Total SS received vs lifetime cost */}
      <Card title="Total SS Received vs Lifetime Tax + IRMAA by Claiming Age">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
          <BarChart data={barData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} />
            <Legend />
            <Bar dataKey="Total SS Received" fill="#7c5cd8" radius={[3,3,0,0]} />
            <Bar dataKey="Lifetime Tax"      fill="#ef4444" stackId="cost" />
            <Bar dataKey="Lifetime IRMAA"    fill="#f59e0b" stackId="cost" radius={[3,3,0,0]} />
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      {/* Lifetime detail for selected scenario */}
      <Card title={`${LABELS[selected.claimAge] ?? `Age ${selected.claimAge}`} — Annual SS & MAGI Through Retirement`}
        accent={COLORS[selected.claimAge] ?? "#57606a"}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#57606a", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          Claiming at <strong>age {selected.claimAge}</strong> · <strong>{fmt$(selected.monthlyBenefit)}/mo</strong> ({(selected.annualBenefit / (fraAnnualBenefit||1) * 100).toFixed(0)}% of FRA benefit) · Total SS received: <strong>{fmt$(selected.totalSS)}</strong>
        </div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
          <LineChart data={lifetimeData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis yAxisId="left"  tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis yAxisId="right" orientation="right" tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <Legend />
            <Line yAxisId="left"  type="monotone" dataKey="SS Benefit" stroke={COLORS[selected.claimAge] ?? "#7c5cd8"} strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            <Line yAxisId="left"  type="monotone" dataKey="Taxable SS" stroke="#a855f7" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} strokeDasharray="4 3" />
            <Line yAxisId="right" type="monotone" dataKey="MAGI"       stroke="#3b82d4" strokeWidth={1.5} dot={false} strokeDasharray="3 2" />
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>

      <Card title={`${LABELS[selected.claimAge] ?? `Age ${selected.claimAge}`} — Estate Value`}>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
          <AreaChart data={lifetimeData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 20, left: 10, bottom: 5 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="age" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tickFormatter={v => "$" + (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k"} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <Tooltip formatter={v => fmt$(v)} labelFormatter={v => `Age ${v}`} />
            <Area type="monotone" dataKey="Estate" stroke="#10b981" fill="#d1fae5" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
          </AreaChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

## What was just completed

### 🔧 Engine additions — [`simulationEngine.js`](simulationEngine.js)

| Addition | Details |
|---|---|
| **`STATE_TAX_RATES`** (exported const) | 51 entries covering all 50 states + DC + "None (0%)". Approximate top marginal rates on ordinary income. |
| **`stateTaxRate` parameter** | Threaded through `runSimulation`, `runLifetimeProjection`, and `bracketFillSchedule`. Applied as a flat rate on gross ordinary income alongside federal tax. Paid from the taxable brokerage alongside federal tax each year. |
| **`calcBreakEven(inputs)`** | Runs two lifetime projections (with conversion vs. do-nothing baseline) and returns the age at which the Roth strategy's total estate first overtakes the baseline, plus a year-by-year advantage array. |
| **`calcSSClaimingComparison(inputs)`** | Runs four lifetime projections for SS claim ages 62, 65, 67, 70 using SSA's official bend-point factors (70% / 86% / 100% / 124% of FRA benefit), returning total SS received, lifetime cost, and estate value for each. |

### 🖥 UI additions — [`src/App.jsx`](src/App.jsx)

| Feature | Tab / Location |
|---|---|
| **State Income Tax dropdown** | Inputs panel (all tabs) — 51 states, dynamically updates all calculations |
| **State tax in summary cards** | Manual Simulator summary — shows separate state tax stat box when `stateTaxRate > 0` |
| **State + Fed columns in year-by-year table** | Manual Simulator — "Fed Tax" + "State Tax" (conditional) + "Total Tax" columns |
| **📈 Break-Even tab** | New tab — hero banner with break-even age, net worth comparison line chart, Roth-advantage area chart (green above zero / red below), year-by-year table with ⬇ Export CSV |
| **🔵 SS Claiming Age tab** | New tab — claiming age chip selector (62/65/67/70), comparison table, estate bar chart, SS received vs cost bar chart, drill-down lifetime SS & MAGI chart, estate area chart |
| **⬇ Export CSV buttons** | Manual Simulator year-by-year table + Break-Even detail table — downloads a clean `.csv` with all columns |
| **Shared `baseInputs` object** | `App()` now computes one `baseInputs` object used by all `useMemo` calls and panels, eliminating duplicate field lists |

### ✅ Validation
- **Build:** `vite build` — green (chunk-size warning only, acceptable)
- **Tests:** `node test.mjs` — **29/29 PASS**
- **New function spot-checks:** `calcBreakEven` returns age 77 with $1.77M advantage at 90; `calcSSClaimingComparison` returns correct benefit amounts (70%/86%/100%/124%); state tax rates correct for CA/TX/NY/MN; state tax threading confirmed with separate Fed/State/Total columns

Those 9 states are already set to `0` in the current [`STATE_TAX_RATES`](simulationEngine.js:46) map. The real gap is the **states that have income tax but exempt IRA/Roth conversions**. PA is the most prominent example — it has a 3.07% income tax but does NOT tax IRA distributions or Roth conversions.

Let me research and apply the full list of states with partial or full retirement income exemptions that cover Roth conversions:

The well-documented states that **have income tax but fully exempt traditional IRA distributions / Roth conversions** include:
- **PA** — 3.07% general rate, but IRA distributions are fully exempt
- **IL** — 4.95% general rate, retirement income (including IRA distributions) fully exempt
- **MS** — 5% general rate, retirement income fully exempt
- **AL** — 5% general rate, retirement income (IRA distributions) fully exempt  
- **HI** — 11% general rate, distributions from qualified pension plans exempt (but IRA-to-Roth conversions are generally **taxable** — so HI stays)

States with **partial** retirement income exemptions where Roth conversions remain partly or fully taxable are handled differently and should show the effective marginal rate.

## What was updated

### [`simulationEngine.js`](simulationEngine.js:39) — `STATE_TAX_RATES` rewritten

The rates are now **conversion-specific** (not generic income tax rates), organized into three categories:

| Category | States | Conversion tax |
|---|---|---|

### New export: [`STATE_TAX_NOTES`](simulationEngine.js)
