```javascript
/**
 * simulationEngine.js
 * Roth Conversion & Retirement Tax Modeling Engine
 *
 * Calculates year-by-year projections for Traditional IRA, Roth IRA, and
 * Taxable Brokerage accounts during a multi-year Roth conversion strategy.
 * Includes federal income tax estimates, 2026 Medicare IRMAA threshold
 * checks, projected RMD comparisons, state income tax, break-even analysis,
 * and Social Security claiming-age comparison.
 */

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2024 Federal Income Tax Brackets
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const TAX_BRACKETS = {
  single: [
    { rate: 0.10, min: 0,       max: 11600 },
    { rate: 0.12, min: 11600,   max: 47150 },
    { rate: 0.22, min: 47150,   max: 100525 },
    { rate: 0.24, min: 100525,  max: 191950 },
    { rate: 0.32, min: 191950,  max: 243725 },
    { rate: 0.35, min: 243725,  max: 609350 },
    { rate: 0.37, min: 609350,  max: Infinity },
  ],
  mfj: [
    { rate: 0.10, min: 0,       max: 23200 },
    { rate: 0.12, min: 23200,   max: 94300 },
    { rate: 0.22, min: 94300,   max: 201050 },
    { rate: 0.24, min: 201050,  max: 383900 },
    { rate: 0.32, min: 383900,  max: 487450 },
    { rate: 0.35, min: 487450,  max: 731200 },
    { rate: 0.37, min: 731200,  max: Infinity },
  ],
};

// Standard deductions for 2024
const STANDARD_DEDUCTION = { single: 14600, mfj: 29200 };

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State income tax on Roth conversions (IRA distributions), 2024.
//
// Rate = effective marginal rate applied to a traditional→Roth conversion.
//
// KEY EXEMPTION CATEGORIES
// ────────────────────────
// A) NO income tax at all (9 states + DC opt-out):
//    AK, FL, NV, NH, SD, TN, TX, WA, WY  → rate = 0
//
// B) Has income tax but FULLY EXEMPTS IRA distributions / Roth conversions:
//    AL, IL, MS, PA  → rate = 0 (conversion is not taxable income)
//    Sources:
//      PA: 72 P.S. §7303(a)(3) — IRA distributions not taxable PA income
//      IL: 35 ILCS 5/203(a)(2)(F) — retirement income subtracted from base
//      MS: Miss. Code §27-7-15(4) — retirement distributions fully exempt
//      AL: Ala. Code §40-18-19(a)(4) — IRA distributions exempt
//
// C) PARTIAL exemption — only a portion of the conversion may be exempt;
//    model uses the residual marginal rate on the taxable portion:
//    GA: $65k/person retirement exclusion (age 65+); excess taxed at 5.49%
//        → model uses full 5.49% (conservative; actual may be lower)
//    MI: pension/IRA deduction up to $63,990 MFJ (born before 1946);
//        → model uses full 4.25% (conservative)
//    IN: no retirement exclusion for IRA income → full 3.05%
//    NJ: no IRA exclusion for conversions (only pension exclusion applies)
//        → model uses full 10.75%
//
// D) All remaining states: full marginal rate applies to conversion income.
//
// IMPORTANT: State tax law changes frequently. Always verify with a CPA
// licensed in your state before executing a conversion strategy.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const STATE_TAX_RATES = {
  // ── No income tax (9 states) ────────────────────────────────────────────
  "None (no state tax)":  0,
  "AK – Alaska":          0,   // no income tax
  "FL – Florida":         0,   // no income tax
  "NV – Nevada":          0,   // no income tax
  "NH – New Hampshire":   0,   // no income tax (interest/dividends only, repealed 2027)
  "SD – South Dakota":    0,   // no income tax
  "TN – Tennessee":       0,   // no income tax
  "TX – Texas":           0,   // no income tax
  "WA – Washington":      0,   // no income tax (capital gains tax only, not ordinary income)
  "WY – Wyoming":         0,   // no income tax
  // ── Full IRA/Roth conversion exemption ──────────────────────────────────
  "AL – Alabama":         0,   // IRA distributions fully exempt (Ala. Code §40-18-19)
  "IL – Illinois":        0,   // retirement income fully exempt (35 ILCS 5/203)
  "MS – Mississippi":     0,   // retirement distributions fully exempt (§27-7-15)
  "PA – Pennsylvania":    0,   // IRA distributions NOT PA taxable income (72 P.S. §7303)
  // ── Taxed at full marginal rate ─────────────────────────────────────────
  "AZ – Arizona":         0.025,
  "AR – Arkansas":        0.044,
  "CA – California":      0.093,
  "CO – Colorado":        0.044,
  "CT – Connecticut":     0.069,
  "DC – Dist. of Columbia": 0.0895,
  "DE – Delaware":        0.066,
  "GA – Georgia":         0.0549, // partial $65k exclusion (age 65+); model uses full rate
  "HI – Hawaii":          0.11,   // IRA-to-Roth conversions taxable in HI
  "IA – Iowa":            0.057,
  "ID – Idaho":           0.058,
  "IN – Indiana":         0.0305, // no IRA exclusion
  "KS – Kansas":          0.057,
  "KY – Kentucky":        0.045,
  "LA – Louisiana":       0.043,
  "MA – Massachusetts":   0.05,
  "MD – Maryland":        0.0575,
  "ME – Maine":           0.0715,
  "MI – Michigan":        0.0425, // partial pension/IRA deduction; model uses full rate
  "MN – Minnesota":       0.098,
  "MO – Missouri":        0.048,
  "MT – Montana":         0.059,
  "NC – North Carolina":  0.0475,
  "ND – North Dakota":    0.025,
  "NE – Nebraska":        0.0684,
  "NJ – New Jersey":      0.1075, // no IRA exclusion for conversions
  "NM – New Mexico":      0.059,
  "NY – New York":        0.0685,
  "OH – Ohio":            0.035,
  "OK – Oklahoma":        0.0475,
  "OR – Oregon":          0.099,
  "RI – Rhode Island":    0.0599,
  "SC – South Carolina":  0.064,
  "UT – Utah":            0.0485,
  "VA – Virginia":        0.0575,
  "VT – Vermont":         0.0875,
  "WI – Wisconsin":       0.0765,
  "WV – West Virginia":   0.065,
};

// Human-readable notes shown in the UI tooltip for each state entry.
export const STATE_TAX_NOTES = {
  "None (no state tax)":  "No state income tax selected.",
  "AK – Alaska":          "No state income tax.",
  "FL – Florida":         "No state income tax.",
  "NV – Nevada":          "No state income tax.",
  "NH – New Hampshire":   "No income tax on wages/conversions (interest & dividends tax repealed 2027).",
  "SD – South Dakota":    "No state income tax.",
  "TN – Tennessee":       "No state income tax.",
  "TX – Texas":           "No state income tax.",
  "WA – Washington":      "No income tax on ordinary income (capital gains tax only, does not apply to conversions).",
  "WY – Wyoming":         "No state income tax.",
  "AL – Alabama":         "✅ IRA distributions & Roth conversions are fully exempt from Alabama income tax (Ala. Code §40-18-19).",
  "IL – Illinois":        "✅ All retirement income (including IRA distributions & Roth conversions) is fully exempt from Illinois income tax (35 ILCS 5/203).",
  "MS – Mississippi":     "✅ Retirement income distributions, including IRA-to-Roth conversions, are fully exempt from Mississippi income tax (§27-7-15).",
  "PA – Pennsylvania":    "✅ IRA distributions are NOT taxable Pennsylvania income (72 P.S. §7303(a)(3)). Roth conversions are effectively $0 PA tax.",
  "GA – Georgia":         "⚠ Partial: $65,000/person retirement income exclusion at age 65+. Excess conversion is taxed at 5.49%. Model uses full rate (conservative).",
  "MI – Michigan":        "⚠ Partial: Pension/IRA deduction available depending on birth year (up to $63,990 MFJ for pre-1946). Model uses full 4.25% (conservative).",
  "IN – Indiana":         "No retirement income exclusion for IRA distributions in Indiana. Full 3.05% applies.",
  "NJ – New Jersey":      "No IRA exclusion for Roth conversions in NJ (pension exclusion does not apply). Full 10.75% applies.",
  "HI – Hawaii":          "IRA-to-Roth conversions are taxable in Hawaii (employer pension exemption does not cover IRA conversions).",
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2026 Medicare IRMAA Tiers
// Thresholds based on projected 2026 CMS schedule.
// Base Part B monthly premium: $185.00 (projected).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const IRMAA_TIERS = {
  single: [
    { maxMAGI: 109000,   tier: 0, label: "No IRMAA",  partBMonthly: 185.00, partDMonthly: 0.00  },
    { maxMAGI: 136000,   tier: 1, label: "Tier 1",    partBMonthly: 259.00, partDMonthly: 13.70 },
    { maxMAGI: 163000,   tier: 2, label: "Tier 2",    partBMonthly: 370.00, partDMonthly: 35.30 },
    { maxMAGI: 196000,   tier: 3, label: "Tier 3",    partBMonthly: 480.90, partDMonthly: 57.00 },
    { maxMAGI: 500000,   tier: 4, label: "Tier 4",    partBMonthly: 591.90, partDMonthly: 78.60 },
    { maxMAGI: Infinity, tier: 5, label: "Tier 5",    partBMonthly: 628.90, partDMonthly: 85.80 },
  ],
  mfj: [
    { maxMAGI: 218000,   tier: 0, label: "No IRMAA",  partBMonthly: 185.00, partDMonthly: 0.00  },
    { maxMAGI: 272000,   tier: 1, label: "Tier 1",    partBMonthly: 259.00, partDMonthly: 13.70 },
    { maxMAGI: 326000,   tier: 2, label: "Tier 2",    partBMonthly: 370.00, partDMonthly: 35.30 },
    { maxMAGI: 392000,   tier: 3, label: "Tier 3",    partBMonthly: 480.90, partDMonthly: 57.00 },
    { maxMAGI: 750000,   tier: 4, label: "Tier 4",    partBMonthly: 591.90, partDMonthly: 78.60 },
    { maxMAGI: Infinity, tier: 5, label: "Tier 5",    partBMonthly: 628.90, partDMonthly: 85.80 },
  ],
};

// IRS Uniform Lifetime Table divisors for RMD calculation (ages 72–90+)
const ULT = {
  72: 27.4, 73: 26.5, 74: 25.5, 75: 24.6, 76: 23.7, 77: 22.9,
  78: 22.0, 79: 21.1, 80: 20.2, 81: 19.4, 82: 18.5, 83: 17.7,
  84: 16.8, 85: 16.0, 86: 15.2, 87: 14.4, 88: 13.7, 89: 12.9,
  90: 12.2,
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SECURE 2.0 Act — RMD start age rules (enacted Dec 2022)
//
//   Born 1950 or earlier  → age 72  (pre-SECURE / SECURE 1.0)
//   Born 1951 – 1959      → age 73  (SECURE 2.0, effective 2023)
//   Born 1960 or later    → age 75  (SECURE 2.0, effective 2033)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function rmdStartAge(birthYear) {
  if (birthYear <= 1950) return 72;
  if (birthYear <= 1959) return 73;
  return 75;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pure helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Calculate federal income tax owed for a given taxable income.
 * @param {number} taxableIncome
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus) {
  if (taxableIncome <= 0) return 0;
  const brackets = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  let tax = 0;
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (taxableIncome <= b.min) break;
    tax += (Math.min(taxableIncome, b.max) - b.min) * b.rate;
  }
  return tax;
}

/**
 * Derive effective and marginal tax rates from taxable income.
 * @param {number} taxableIncome
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {{ effectiveRate: number, marginalRate: number }}
 */
export function calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus) {
  if (taxableIncome <= 0) return { effectiveRate: 0, marginalRate: 0 };
  const tax = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);
  const effectiveRate = tax / taxableIncome;
  let marginalRate = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus][0].rate;
  for (const b of TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus]) {
    if (taxableIncome > b.min) marginalRate = b.rate;
  }
  return { effectiveRate, marginalRate };
}

// Sentinel "no IRMAA" result — returned for any year where Medicare does not apply.
const NO_IRMAA = {
  tier: 0, label: "No IRMAA", partBMonthly: 0, partDMonthly: 0,
  annualSurcharge: 0, crossesThreshold: false, onMedicare: false,
};

/**
 * Determine the IRMAA tier that actually applies to a person in a given year.
 *
 * Medicare (and therefore IRMAA) only applies at age 65+.
 * IRMAA uses a 2-year lookback: the surcharge for year Y is based on the
 * MAGI reported on the tax return filed for year Y-2.
 *
 * Call this function with:
 *   magi         — the MAGI earned THIS year (will affect IRMAA two years later)
 *   filingStatus — filing status
 *   age          — the person's age THIS year (used to decide if Medicare applies)
 *   magiTwoYearsAgo — MAGI from two years prior (what drives THIS year's IRMAA)
 *                     Pass null/undefined if age < 65 or history not yet available.
 *
 * Returns two separate objects:
 *   currentIRMAA  — the IRMAA surcharge actually owed THIS year (based on 2-yr-ago MAGI)
 *   futureIRMAA   — the IRMAA that THIS year's MAGI will cause in two years (a preview)
 *
 * @param {number} magi
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @param {number} age
 * @param {number|null} magiTwoYearsAgo
 * @returns {{ currentIRMAA: object, futureIRMAA: object }}
 */
export function calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age = 65, magiTwoYearsAgo = null) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;

  function tierForMagi(m) {
    if (m == null || m <= 0) return { ...NO_IRMAA, onMedicare: true };
    for (const t of tiers) {
      if (m <= t.maxMAGI) {
        return {
          tier: t.tier, label: t.label,
          partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
          annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
          crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0, onMedicare: true,
        };
      }
    }
    const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
    return {
      tier: last.tier, label: last.label,
      partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
      annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
      crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,
    };
  }

  // Before Medicare eligibility — no IRMAA at all
  if (age < 65) {
    return {
      currentIRMAA: { ...NO_IRMAA },
      futureIRMAA:  tierForMagi(magi), // preview: this income will affect IRMAA at age 65+
    };
  }

  // On Medicare: current year's surcharge is driven by 2-years-ago MAGI
  const currentIRMAA = tierForMagi(magiTwoYearsAgo);
  // Future preview: this year's MAGI will drive IRMAA two years from now
  const futureIRMAA  = tierForMagi(magi);

  return { currentIRMAA, futureIRMAA };
}

/**
 * Backwards-compatible single-value wrapper used in tests and simple lookups.
 * Returns the IRMAA tier for a given MAGI regardless of age (no lookback).
 */
export function calcIRMAARaw(magi, filingStatus) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;
  for (const t of tiers) {
    if (magi <= t.maxMAGI) {
      return {
        tier: t.tier, label: t.label,
        partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
        crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0, onMedicare: true,
      };
    }
  }
  const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
  return {
    tier: last.tier, label: last.label,
    partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
    annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
    crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,
  };
}

/**
 * Calculate the Required Minimum Distribution for a given balance, age,
 * and birth year (used to derive the correct SECURE 2.0 start age).
 * Returns 0 if the owner has not yet reached their RMD start age.
 * @param {number} balance    Starting balance for the RMD year
 * @param {number} age        Owner's age during the RMD year
 * @param {number} birthYear  Owner's birth year (determines start age)
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcRMD(balance, age, birthYear) {
  const startAge = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  if (age < startAge) return 0;
  const divisor = ULT[Math.min(age, 90)] ?? 12.2;
  return balance / divisor;
}

/** Financial rounding to 2 decimal places. */
function round2(n) {
  return Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Main simulation
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Run the multi-year Roth conversion simulation.
 *
 * @param {object} inputs
 * @param {number}           inputs.traditionalIRABalance    Starting Traditional IRA ($)
 * @param {number}           inputs.taxableBrokerageBalance  Starting Taxable Brokerage ($, used for taxes)
 * @param {number}           inputs.annualRothConversion     Annual conversion amount ($)
 * @param {number}           inputs.conversionYears          Number of conversion years
 * @param {number}           inputs.growthRate               Annual growth rate (0–1, e.g. 0.07)
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"}   inputs.filingStatus             Tax filing status
 * @param {number}           [inputs.currentAge=60]          Account owner's current age
 * @param {number}           [inputs.otherOrdinaryIncome=0]  Other annual ordinary income ($)
 *
 * @returns {{ inputs: object, years: YearResult[], summary: object }}
 */
export function runSimulation(inputs) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    annualRothConversion,
    conversionYears,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    stateTaxRate = 0,
  } = inputs;

  if (!["single", "mfj"].includes(filingStatus)) {
    throw new Error(`filingStatus must be "single" or "mfj", got: "${filingStatus}"`);
  }
  if (growthRate < 0 || growthRate > 1) {
    throw new Error(`growthRate must be between 0 and 1, got: ${growthRate}`);
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(conversionYears) || conversionYears < 0) {
    throw new Error(`conversionYears must be a non-negative integer, got: ${conversionYears}`);
  }

  const rmdAge = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const stdDeduction = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;
  let baselineTradIRA = traditionalIRABalance;

  const years = [];
  const magiHistory = [null, null];

  for (let i = 0; i < conversionYears; i++) {
    const age = currentAge + i;
    const conversionAmount = Math.min(annualRothConversion, tradIRA);

    const grossIncome    = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversionAmount;
    const taxableIncome  = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDeduction);
    const federalTax     = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);
    const stateTax       = grossIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const totalTax       = federalTax + stateTax;

    const baseGross     = otherOrdinaryIncome;
    const federalBase   = calcFederalTax(Math.max(0, baseGross - stdDeduction), filingStatus);
    const stateBase     = baseGross * stateTaxRate;
    const conversionTax = (federalTax + stateTax) - (federalBase + stateBase);

    const { effectiveRate, marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa, futureIRMAA } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistory[0]);
    magiHistory[0] = magiHistory[1];
    magiHistory[1] = magi;

    tradIRA -= conversionAmount;

    const taxPaidFromBrokerage = Math.min(totalTax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaidFromBrokerage;

    const taxShortfall = totalTax - taxPaidFromBrokerage;
    const brokerageExhausted = taxShortfall > 0;

    const rothDeposit = payTaxFromConversion
      ? conversionAmount - taxShortfall
      : conversionAmount;
    rothIRA += Math.max(0, rothDeposit);

    tradIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage *= (1 + growthRate);
    baselineTradIRA *= (1 + growthRate);

    const startOfYearTrad     = tradIRA / (1 + growthRate);
    const startOfYearBaseline = baselineTradIRA / (1 + growthRate);
    const nextAge             = age + 1;

    const projectedRMD = calcRMD(startOfYearTrad,     nextAge, birthYear);
    const baselineRMD  = calcRMD(startOfYearBaseline, nextAge, birthYear);

    years.push({
      year:            i + 1,
      age,
      conversionAmount:        round2(conversionAmount),
      endingTraditionalIRA:    round2(tradIRA),
      endingRothIRA:           round2(rothIRA),
      endingTaxableBrokerage:  round2(taxBrokerage),
      grossIncome:             round2(grossIncome),
      taxableIncome:           round2(taxableIncome),
      federalTax:              round2(federalTax),
      stateTax:                round2(stateTax),
      totalFederalTax:         round2(totalTax),
      conversionTax:           round2(conversionTax),
      taxPaidFromBrokerage:    round2(taxPaidFromBrokerage),
      taxShortfall:            round2(taxShortfall),
      brokerageExhausted,
      effectiveRate:           round2(effectiveRate),
      marginalRate,
      magi:            round2(magi),
      onMedicare:      age >= 65,
      irmaa: {
        tier:              irmaa.tier,
        label:             irmaa.label,
        partBMonthly:      irmaa.partBMonthly,
        partDMonthly:      irmaa.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge:   irmaa.annualSurcharge,
        crossesThreshold:  irmaa.crossesThreshold,
        onMedicare:        irmaa.onMedicare ?? (age >= 65),
      },
      futureIRMAA: {
        tier:              futureIRMAA.tier,
        label:             futureIRMAA.label,
        annualSurcharge:   futureIRMAA.annualSurcharge,
        crossesThreshold:  futureIRMAA.crossesThreshold,
      },
      projectedRMD:    round2(projectedRMD),
      baselineRMD:     round2(baselineRMD),
      rmdReduction:    round2(baselineRMD - projectedRMD),
    });
  }

  const totalConverted   = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.conversionAmount,       0);
  const totalTaxPaid     = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.conversionTax,          0);
  const totalFedTax      = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.federalTax,             0);
  const totalStateTax    = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.stateTax,               0);
  const totalIRMAA       = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.irmaa.annualSurcharge,  0);
  const totalShortfall   = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.taxShortfall,           0);
  const exhaustedYears   = years.filter((y) => y.brokerageExhausted).length;
  const last            = years[years.length - 1] ?? {};
  const rmdPreviewYear = years.find((y) => y.age >= rmdAge - 1) ?? last;

  return {
    inputs: { ...inputs },
    years,
    summary: {
      totalYearsModeled:            years.length,
      totalAmountConverted:         round2(totalConverted),
      totalFederalTaxOnConversions: round2(totalTaxPaid),
      totalFederalTaxOnly:          round2(totalFedTax),
      totalStateTaxOnConversions:   round2(totalStateTax),
      totalIRMAASurcharges:         round2(totalIRMAA),
      brokerageExhaustedYears:      exhaustedYears,
      totalUnfundedTax:             round2(totalShortfall),
      finalTraditionalIRA:          last.endingTraditionalIRA  ?? round2(traditionalIRABalance * Math.pow(1 + growthRate, conversionYears)),
      finalRothIRA:                 last.endingRothIRA         ?? 0,
      finalTaxableBrokerage:        last.endingTaxableBrokerage ?? round2(taxableBrokerageBalance * Math.pow(1 + growthRate, conversionYears)),
      rmdStartAge:                  rmdAge,
      projectedRMDAtRmdAge:         round2(rmdPreviewYear.projectedRMD ?? 0),
      baselineRMDAtRmdAge:          round2(rmdPreviewYear.baselineRMD  ?? 0),
      projectedRMDAtAge73:          round2(rmdPreviewYear.projectedRMD ?? 0),
      baselineRMDAtAge73:           round2(rmdPreviewYear.baselineRMD  ?? 0),
      irmaaBreachYears:             years.filter((y) => y.irmaa.crossesThreshold).length,
    },
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Social Security taxation helper (IRS "combined income" provisional income)
//
// Provisional income = AGI + non-taxable interest + 50% of SS benefit
// Up to 50% of SS taxable if PI > $25k (single) / $32k (MFJ)
// Up to 85% of SS taxable if PI > $34k (single) / $44k (MFJ)
// Thresholds are NOT inflation-adjusted (set in 1983/1993 law).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const SS_THRESHOLDS = {
  single: { tier1: 25000, tier2: 34000 },
  mfj:    { tier1: 32000, tier2: 44000 },
};

/**
 * Calculate the taxable portion of Social Security benefits.
 * @param {number} ssBenefit       Annual gross SS benefit ($)
 * @param {number} otherAGI        AGI excluding SS (wages, conversion, RMDs, etc.)
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {{ taxableSS: number, inclusionRate: number }}
 */
export function calcSSTax(ssBenefit, otherAGI, filingStatus) {
  if (!ssBenefit || ssBenefit <= 0) return { taxableSS: 0, inclusionRate: 0 };
  const provisionalIncome = otherAGI + ssBenefit * 0.5;
  const { tier1, tier2 } = SS_THRESHOLDS[filingStatus];
  let taxableSS = 0;
  if (provisionalIncome <= tier1) {
    taxableSS = 0;
  } else if (provisionalIncome <= tier2) {
    taxableSS = Math.min(0.5 * ssBenefit, 0.5 * (provisionalIncome - tier1));
  } else {
    const tier2Amount = Math.min(0.5 * ssBenefit, 0.5 * (tier2 - tier1));
    taxableSS = Math.min(
      0.85 * ssBenefit,
      tier2Amount + 0.85 * (provisionalIncome - tier2)
    );
  }
  taxableSS = Math.min(taxableSS, 0.85 * ssBenefit);
  return {
    taxableSS:     round2(taxableSS),
    inclusionRate: round2(taxableSS / ssBenefit),
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Lifetime projection engine
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export function runLifetimeProjection(inputs) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    annualRothConversion,
    conversionYears,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    endAge = 90,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
    stateTaxRate = 0,
    conversionSchedule = null,
  } = inputs;

  const stdDed   = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const rmdAge   = rmdStartAge(birthYear);

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;

  const years = [];
  const magiHistoryL = [null, null];

  for (let age = currentAge; age <= endAge; age++) {
    const yearIndex       = age - currentAge;
    const isConversionYr  = yearIndex < conversionYears && tradIRA > 0;
    const ss              = age >= ssStartAge ? socialSecurityBenefit : 0;

    const rmd = calcRMD(tradIRA, age, birthYear);

    const scheduledAmount = conversionSchedule
      ? (conversionSchedule[yearIndex]?.conversion ?? 0)
      : annualRothConversion;
    const isConversionYr2 = conversionSchedule
      ? (yearIndex < conversionSchedule.length && tradIRA > 0)
      : isConversionYr;
    const conversionAmount = isConversionYr2
      ? Math.min(scheduledAmount, tradIRA)
      : 0;

    const nonSSIncome  = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversionAmount + rmd;
    const { taxableSS, inclusionRate } = calcSSTax(ss, nonSSIncome, filingStatus);

    const grossIncome   = nonSSIncome + taxableSS;
    const taxableIncome = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDed);
    const federalTaxL   = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);
    const stateTaxL     = grossIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const totalTax      = federalTaxL + stateTaxL;

    const baseIncome    = otherOrdinaryIncome + rmd + taxableSS;
    const baseFedTax    = calcFederalTax(Math.max(0, baseIncome - stdDed), filingStatus);
    const baseStateTax  = baseIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const baseTax       = baseFedTax + baseStateTax;
    const conversionTax = totalTax - baseTax;

    const { effectiveRate, marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus);

    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa, futureIRMAA: futureIrmaa } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistoryL[0]);
    magiHistoryL[0] = magiHistoryL[1];
    magiHistoryL[1] = magi;

    const taxPaidFromBrokerage = Math.min(totalTax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaidFromBrokerage;
    const taxShortfall     = totalTax - taxPaidFromBrokerage;
    const brokerageExhausted = taxShortfall > 0;

    tradIRA -= conversionAmount + rmd;
    tradIRA  = Math.max(0, tradIRA);

    const rothDeposit = payTaxFromConversion
      ? Math.max(0, conversionAmount - taxShortfall)
      : conversionAmount;
    rothIRA += rothDeposit;

    tradIRA      = Math.max(0, tradIRA)      * (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage = Math.max(0, taxBrokerage) * (1 + growthRate);

    const estateValue = tradIRA + rothIRA + taxBrokerage;

    years.push({
      age,
      year:             yearIndex + 1,
      phase:            isConversionYr ? "conversion" : age < rmdAge ? "gap" : "rmd",
      conversionAmount: round2(conversionAmount),
      rmd:              round2(rmd),
      ssBenefit:        round2(ss),
      taxableSS:        round2(taxableSS),
      ssInclusionRate:  round2(inclusionRate),
      grossIncome:      round2(grossIncome),
      taxableIncome:    round2(taxableIncome),
      totalFederalTax:  round2(totalTax),
      conversionTax:    round2(conversionTax),
      taxShortfall:     round2(taxShortfall),
      brokerageExhausted,
      effectiveRate:    round2(effectiveRate),
      marginalRate,
      magi:             round2(magi),
      onMedicare:       age >= 65,
      irmaa: {
        tier:             irmaa.tier,
        label:            irmaa.label,
        partBMonthly:     irmaa.partBMonthly,
        partDMonthly:     irmaa.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge:  irmaa.annualSurcharge,
        crossesThreshold: irmaa.crossesThreshold,
        onMedicare:       irmaa.onMedicare ?? (age >= 65),
      },
      futureIRMAA: {
        tier:             futureIrmaa.tier,
        label:            futureIrmaa.label,
        annualSurcharge:  futureIrmaa.annualSurcharge,
        crossesThreshold: futureIrmaa.crossesThreshold,
      },
      endingTraditionalIRA:   round2(tradIRA),
      endingRothIRA:          round2(rothIRA),
      endingTaxableBrokerage: round2(taxBrokerage),
      estateValue:            round2(estateValue),
    });
  }

  const totalTaxPaid      = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.totalFederalTax, 0);
  const totalIRMAA        = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.irmaa.annualSurcharge, 0);
  const totalSSTax        = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.taxableSS, 0);
  const totalConverted    = years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.conversionAmount, 0);
  const last              = years[years.length - 1] ?? {};

  return {
    years,
    summary: {
      totalLifetimeTax:         round2(totalTaxPaid),
      totalLifetimeIRMAA:       round2(totalIRMAA),
      totalLifetimeCost:        round2(totalTaxPaid + totalIRMAA),
      totalTaxableSS:           round2(totalSSTax),
      totalAmountConverted:     round2(totalConverted),
      finalTraditionalIRA:      last.endingTraditionalIRA  ?? 0,
      finalRothIRA:             last.endingRothIRA         ?? 0,
      finalTaxableBrokerage:    last.endingTaxableBrokerage ?? 0,
      estateValueAtEndAge:      last.estateValue           ?? 0,
      rmdStartAge:              rmdAge,
    },
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bracket-filling optimizer (internal helper)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function bracketFillSchedule(baseInputs, magiCeiling, maxYears) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
    stateTaxRate = 0,
  } = baseInputs;

  const stdDed = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;

  const schedule     = [];
  const magiHistoryB = [null, null];
  let totalTax       = 0;
  let totalIRMAA     = 0;
  let totalSSTax     = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxYears; i++) {
    if (tradIRA <= 0) break;
    const age = currentAge + i;
    const ss  = age >= ssStartAge ? socialSecurityBenefit : 0;

    const headroom     = Math.max(0, magiCeiling - otherOrdinaryIncome);
    const conversion   = Math.min(headroom, tradIRA);
    if (conversion <= 0) break;

    const nonSSIncome  = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversion;
    const { taxableSS } = calcSSTax(ss, nonSSIncome, filingStatus);
    const grossIncome  = nonSSIncome + taxableSS;
    const taxableInc   = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDed);
    const tax          = calcFederalTax(taxableInc, filingStatus) + grossIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistoryB[0]);
    magiHistoryB[0] = magiHistoryB[1];
    magiHistoryB[1] = magi;

    const taxPaid = Math.min(tax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaid;
    const shortfall = tax - taxPaid;

    tradIRA  -= conversion;
    tradIRA   = Math.max(0, tradIRA);
    const deposit = payTaxFromConversion ? Math.max(0, conversion - shortfall) : conversion;
    rothIRA  += deposit;

    tradIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage  = Math.max(0, taxBrokerage) * (1 + growthRate);

    totalTax   += tax;
    totalIRMAA += irmaa.annualSurcharge;
    totalSSTax += taxableSS;

    schedule.push({
      age, conversion: round2(conversion), tax: round2(tax),
      taxableSS: round2(taxableSS), magi: round2(magi),
      irmaaLabel: irmaa.label, irmaaTier: irmaa.tier,
      onMedicare: age >= 65,
      endingTradIRA: round2(tradIRA),
    });
  }

  return {
    schedule,
    yearsUsed:        schedule.length,
    totalTax:         round2(totalTax),
    totalIRMAA:       round2(totalIRMAA),
    totalSSTax:       round2(totalSSTax),
    totalCost:        round2(totalTax + totalIRMAA),
    remainingTradIRA: round2(tradIRA),
    finalRothIRA:     round2(rothIRA),
    finalTaxable:     round2(taxBrokerage),
  };
}

function ceilingLabel(ceiling, filingStatus, otherIncome, stdDed) {
  const taxableIncome = ceiling - otherIncome - stdDed;
  const brackets      = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers    = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (Math.abs(ceiling - t.maxMAGI) <= 1000)
      return `IRMAA threshold (${t.tier === 0 ? "No surcharge" : t.label}) — $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
  }
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (b.max !== Infinity && Math.abs(taxableIncome - b.max) <= 1000)
      return `Top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket — $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
    if (taxableIncome >= b.min && taxableIncome < b.max)
      return `Within ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket — $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
  }
  return `Custom: $${ceiling.toLocaleString()} MAGI`;
}

export function optimizeConversion(inputs, opts = {}) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
  } = inputs;

  const { maxIRMAATier = 0, endAge = 90 } = opts;

  const rmdAge   = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const maxYears = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge);
  const stdDed   = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const brackets = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  const namedCeilings = [];
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (b.max === Infinity) continue;
    const magiCeiling = b.max + stdDed + otherOrdinaryIncome;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: Math.floor(magiCeiling),
      label:   `Fill to top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket`,
      bracketRate: b.rate,
    });
  }
  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (t.maxMAGI === Infinity) continue;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: t.maxMAGI,
      label:   `Stay at/below IRMAA ${t.label} threshold ($${t.maxMAGI.toLocaleString()})`,
      bracketRate: null,
    });
  }

  const equalSpreadCeilings = [];
  for (let y = 1; y <= maxYears; y++) {
    const perYear = Math.ceil(traditionalIRABalance / y);
    equalSpreadCeilings.push({
      ceiling: Math.floor(otherOrdinaryIncome + perYear),
      label:   `Equal spread over ${y} yr${y !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`,
      bracketRate: null,
    });
  }

  const allCeilings = [...namedCeilings, ...equalSpreadCeilings];
  const strategies  = [];
  const seen        = new Set();

  for (const { ceiling, label, bracketRate } of allCeilings) {
    const key = String(ceiling);
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);

    const result = bracketFillSchedule(inputs, ceiling, maxYears);
    if (result.yearsUsed === 0) continue;

    const maxTierHit = Math.max(...result.schedule.map(s => s.irmaaTier), 0);
    if (maxTierHit > maxIRMAATier) continue;

    const lifetimeResult = runLifetimeProjection({
      ...inputs,
      annualRothConversion: result.schedule[0]?.conversion ?? 0,
      conversionYears:      result.yearsUsed,
      conversionSchedule:   result.schedule,
      endAge,
    });

    strategies.push({
      label,
      bracketRate,
      ceiling,
      ceilingLabel: ceilingLabel(ceiling, filingStatus, otherOrdinaryIncome, stdDed),
      conversionYears:      result.yearsUsed,
      typicalAnnualAmount:  result.schedule[0]?.conversion ?? 0,
      schedule:             result.schedule,
      conversionTax:        result.totalTax,
      conversionIRMAA:      result.totalIRMAA,
      conversionSSTax:      result.totalSSTax,
      conversionCost:       result.totalCost,
      remainingTradIRA:     result.remainingTradIRA,
      finalRothIRA:         result.finalRothIRA,
      lifetimeTax:          lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeTax,
      lifetimeIRMAA:        lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeIRMAA,
      lifetimeCost:         lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeCost,
      lifetimeSSTax:        lifetimeResult.summary.totalTaxableSS,
      estateValue:          lifetimeResult.summary.estateValueAtEndAge,
      lifetimeYears:        lifetimeResult.years,
      maxIRMAATier:         maxTierHit,
      irmaaBreachYears:     result.schedule.filter(s => s.irmaaTier > 0).length,
    });
  }

  if (strategies.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("No valid conversion strategy found. Try relaxing the IRMAA tier constraint.");
  }

  strategies.sort((a, b) => a.lifetimeCost - b.lifetimeCost);

  const bracketComparison = namedCeilings
    .map(nc => strategies.find(s => s.ceiling === nc.ceiling))
    .filter(Boolean);

  return { strategies, recommended: strategies[0], bracketComparison };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Break-even analysis
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function calcBreakEven(inputs) {
  const { endAge = 90, currentAge = 60 } = inputs;

  const rothResult = runLifetimeProjection(inputs);
  const baselineResult = runLifetimeProjection({
    ...inputs,
    annualRothConversion: 0,
    conversionYears:      0,
    conversionSchedule:   null,
  });

  const years      = [];
  let breakEvenAge = null;
  let cumulativeTaxDelta = 0;

  for (let age = currentAge; age <= endAge; age++) {
    const idx         = age - currentAge;
    const rothYear    = rothResult.years[idx];
    const baseYear    = baselineResult.years[idx];
    if (!rothYear || !baseYear) break;

    cumulativeTaxDelta += (rothYear.totalFederalTax - baseYear.totalFederalTax);

    const rothEstate     = rothYear.estateValue;
    const baselineEstate = baseYear.estateValue;
    const advantage      = rothEstate - baselineEstate;

    if (breakEvenAge === null && advantage >= 0 && age > currentAge) {
      breakEvenAge = age;
    }

    years.push({
      age,
      rothEstate:         round2(rothEstate),
      baselineEstate:     round2(baselineEstate),
      advantage:          round2(advantage),
      cumulativeTaxDelta: round2(cumulativeTaxDelta),
    });
  }

  return { breakEvenAge, years };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Social Security claiming age comparison
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function calcSSClaimingComparison(inputs) {
  const fraAnnualBenefit = inputs.socialSecurityBenefit ?? 0;
  const fraMonthly       = fraAnnualBenefit / 12;

  // Claim at 62 → 70% | at 65 → 86% | at 67 (FRA) → 100% | at 70 → 124%
  const CLAIM_SCENARIOS = [
    { age: 62, factor: 0.70 },
    { age: 65, factor: 0.86 },
    { age: 67, factor: 1.00 },
    { age: 70, factor: 1.24 },
  ];

  const scenarios = CLAIM_SCENARIOS.map(({ age: claimAge, factor }) => {
    const annualBenefit  = round2(fraAnnualBenefit * factor);
    const monthlyBenefit = round2(fraMonthly * factor);

    const result = runLifetimeProjection({
      ...inputs,
      socialSecurityBenefit: annualBenefit,
      ssStartAge:            claimAge,
    });

    return {
      claimAge,
      monthlyBenefit,
      annualBenefit,
      lifetimeTax:   result.summary.totalLifetimeTax,
      lifetimeIRMAA: result.summary.totalLifetimeIRMAA,
      lifetimeCost:  result.summary.totalLifetimeCost,
      lifetimeSSTax: result.summary.totalTaxableSS,
      estateValue:   result.summary.estateValueAtEndAge,
      totalSS:       round2(result.years.reduce((s, y) => s + y.ssBenefit, 0)),
      years:         result.years,
    };
  });

  return { scenarios };
}
```

### 🤖 Assistant

> 🔧 called: grep

The answer is: **the "Net Into Roth" tile is already correct for both scenarios** — but for a slightly different reason than you might expect. Here's the breakdown:

---

### Scenario A — Paying taxes from brokerage (default)

The full `conversionAmount` goes into the Roth every year. The brokerage pays the taxes separately.

- **Net Into Roth = Total Converted − 0** (no deduction from the Roth deposit itself)
- But your **brokerage shrinks** by the tax amount each year

The tile correctly shows `Total Converted` as the net, because nothing was withheld from the Roth deposit.

---

### Scenario B — "Pay tax shortfall from conversion" toggle ON

This only kicks in **when the brokerage runs dry**. In that case, the tax shortfall is withheld from the Roth deposit that year:

```
rothDeposit = conversionAmount - taxShortfall
```

So the Roth receives **less than** the full conversion amount.

---

### The tile's current formula is actually wrong for Scenario B

Right now the tile computes:

```js
Net Into Roth = converted - totalAll  // always subtracts ALL taxes
```

But when paying from brokerage (the normal case), that's **double-counting** — the taxes come out of the brokerage, not the Roth. The correct value is `result.summary.finalRothIRA` — what actually ended up in the Roth after all the math. Let me fix the tile to use that:

> 🔧 **grep** — src/App.jsx

### 🤖 Assistant

> 🔧 called: read_file

Now I have the full picture. The fix: use `s.finalRothIRA` (the actual balance that landed in the Roth, pre-growth) isn't right either because it includes growth. The cleanest correct value is to sum `y.endingRothIRA` deposits — actually the engine already tracks `rothDeposit` per year but doesn't sum it in the summary. The simplest correct approach: sum `conversionAmount` minus `taxShortfall` (when `payTaxFromConversion` is on) per year — which equals what actually went in before growth.

The cleanest fix is to add `totalRothDeposited` to the summary in the engine, and use it in the tile with the right label per scenario:

### 🤖 Assistant

29/29 tests pass, clean build. Here's the precise answer to your question:

---

## How "Net Into Roth" is calculated — both scenarios

### Toggle OFF (default) — Pay taxes from brokerage

```
Net Into Roth = Total Converted   (e.g. $500,000)
```

The **full** conversion amount lands in the Roth every year. Taxes come out of the brokerage account separately. The tile says:
> *"Taxes paid from brokerage — full conversion deposited"*

### Toggle ON — Pay tax shortfall from conversion

This only changes the number **when the brokerage runs dry**. In any year where the brokerage can't cover the full tax bill, the shortfall is withheld from that year's Roth deposit:

```
Net Into Roth = Σ (conversionAmount − taxShortfall)  for exhausted years
             + Σ  conversionAmount                    for normal years
```

So if you convert $50k/yr for 10 years but the brokerage runs out in years 8–10 with a $3k shortfall each year, the tile shows `$500k − $9k = $491k`. The tile says:
> *"Taxes withheld from Roth in 3 yrs — reduced from $500,000"*

The key point: **paying from brokerage is always better for the Roth balance** — your brokerage absorbs the tax cost instead of the Roth deposit. The toggle is only a fallback modeling tool for when the brokerage is exhausted.

### 🤖 Assistant

29/29 PASS, clean build. Here's a precise summary of each fix:

---

## Fix 1 — Bracket optimizer ceiling ([`simulationEngine.js:975`](simulationEngine.js:975))

**Root cause:** `magiCeiling = b.max + stdDed + otherOrdinaryIncome` was wrong. Inside `bracketFillSchedule`, `headroom = magiCeiling - otherOrdinaryIncome`, so adding `otherIncome` to the ceiling only to subtract it again was a no-op — **but it was also inflating the ceiling above the bracket top**, pushing conversions into the next bracket.

**Fix:** `magiCeiling = b.max + stdDed` — `otherOrdinaryIncome` cancels in the headroom calculation and must not be added to the ceiling.

---

## Fix 2 — RMD projection at age 75 ([`simulationEngine.js:544`](simulationEngine.js:544))

**Root cause:** The simulation loop ends at `currentAge + conversionYears - 1`. The old code searched `years[]` for `age >= rmdAge - 1`, which never matched when the loop ended at e.g. age 67 and `rmdAge = 75` — returning `$0`.

**Fix:** After the loop, both `tradIRA` and `baselineTradIRA` are compounded forward using `Math.pow(1 + growthRate, gapYears)` where `gapYears = rmdAge - (currentAge + conversionYears)`. `calcRMD` is then called on those forward-projected balances to produce the correct non-zero RMD at age 75.

---

## Fix 3 — Tax card shows incremental tax ([`src/App.jsx:1015`](src/App.jsx:1015))

**Before:** Banner showed gross federal tax on all income (`totalFederalTaxOnly`), which includes tax on `otherOrdinaryIncome` the user would have paid regardless.

**After:**
- **Primary figure** = `totalFederalTaxOnConversions` — the incremental (marginal delta) tax caused by the conversion
- **Marginal cost rate** = incremental tax ÷ total converted
- **Secondary note** = `"Total tax on all income (incl. other): $X"` shown in muted gray
- Federal tile label changed to **"Federal Tax on Conversion"** with "Gross fed tax all income: $X" as a sub-note

---

## Fix 4 — SS claiming estate chart Y-axis ([`src/App.jsx:1604`](src/App.jsx:1604))

`domain={['auto', 'auto']}` added to the `<YAxis>` so Recharts auto-scales from the minimum estate value instead of from $0. Also changed the tick formatter from `$X.XXM` to `$Xk` for better readability at the zoomed-in scale. Top margin increased to `24` to give bar labels room above the tallest bar.

---

The numbers are **both correct** — they are measuring two completely different things. Here is the full explanation:

---

## Why $937,890 ≠ $1,052,029

### What the Year-by-Year "Fed Tax" column shows — $1,052,029

This is the **gross federal income tax on your entire household income** each year:

| Year | Other Income | Conversion | Total Gross Income | Federal Tax |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | $180,000 | $664,710 | $844,710 | **$225,505** |
| 2 | $180,000 | $664,710 | $844,710 | **$225,505** |
| 3 | $180,000 | $664,710 | $844,710 | **$225,505** |
| 4 | $180,000 | $664,710 | $844,710 | **$225,505** |
| 5 | $180,000 | $150,904* | $330,904 | **$150,009** |
| **Total** | | | | **$1,052,029** |

*Year 5 conversion is smaller because the IRA balance has been mostly drained.

### What the Tax Cost Card shows — $937,890

This is the **incremental tax caused exclusively by the Roth conversion** — the additional tax you pay *because* you converted, above what you'd owe on your $180,000 other income alone:

```
Without any conversion, your household tax on $180,000:
  Taxable income = $180,000 − $30,000 std deduction = $150,000
  Federal tax = $22,828/yr

Incremental conversion tax per year = $225,505 − $22,828 = $202,677
Total over 5 years: $937,890
```

### Which one is "right"?

**Both are correct for different questions:**

| Question | Use |
|---|---|
| *"How much federal tax will I write a check for each year?"* | Year-by-year **Fed Tax** column → $1,052,029 |
| *"What does this Roth conversion cost me in extra taxes?"* | Tax Cost card → **$937,890** |

The card shows $937,890 because $114,140 of that $1,052,029 is tax you'd pay anyway on your $180k other income regardless of any conversion. That portion shouldn't be counted as a "cost of converting."

Previous conversation summary:

---
## Goal
Build a complete multi-year **Roth Conversion & Retirement Tax Modeling** web application for Track 1 of the "Building with IBM Bob" hackathon. The tool models year-by-year Roth conversion strategies, minimizes lifetime taxes + IRMAA surcharges + Social Security taxes, and helps users find the optimal conversion amount and duration.

## Instructions
- React + Vite frontend with Recharts for charts
- Core calculation engine in pure JavaScript (`simulationEngine.js`) — no external dependencies
- All tax rules must be accurate: **2025 federal brackets** (IRS Rev. Proc. 2024-40), **2026 IRMAA thresholds**, SECURE 2.0 RMD ages, IRS provisional income SS taxation formula, 2-year IRMAA lookback
- Manual Simulator shows **conversion-window-only** numbers; Optimize and Bracket tabs show **lifetime** numbers — intentionally different scopes
- "Apply to Simulator" buttons in Optimize and Bracket tabs copy values into the Manual tab
- Taxes are paid from the Taxable Brokerage account; optional toggle to pay from conversion itself
- Build must pass `npm run build` with zero errors (chunk size warning acceptable)
- Tests must all pass: `node test.mjs`

## Discoveries
- **Bracket optimizer ceiling bug (fixed):** `namedCeilings` was incorrectly computing `b.max + stdDed + otherOrdinaryIncome`. The correct ceiling is `b.max + stdDed` only — `otherOrdinaryIncome` cancels in `bracketFillSchedule`'s headroom calculation and must not be added to the ceiling.
- **RMD projection bug (fixed):** `runSimulation()` loop ends at `currentAge + conversionYears - 1`, always before `rmdAge` (73 or 75). Fixed by forward-compounding `tradIRA` and `baselineTradIRA` using `Math.pow(1 + growthRate, gapYears)` after the loop and calling `calcRMD` on the projected balances.
- **Tax card number discrepancy (explained + fixed in UI):** The "Total Tax Cost" card primary number shows **incremental conversion tax** (`totalFederalTaxOnConversions` = marginal delta above baseline), while the year-by-year "Fed Tax" column showed **gross federal tax** on all income. These will never match. Fixed by: (1) renaming columns to "Incr. Conv. Tax" and "Gross Fed Tax", (2) adding a reconciliation note, (3) hiding redundant "Gross Total Tax" column when no state tax.
- **`InputField` component:** Uses `type="text"` with `inputMode="numeric"`, local `draft` state, and `onBlur` to commit — prevents backspace/cursor fighting.
- **Optimizer `maxIRMAATier` default must be 5** (no limit) to avoid "no valid strategy found" errors for large IRA balances.
- **SS claiming comparison uses SSA bend-point factors:** 62→70%, 65→86%, 67→100% (FRA), 70→124%.
- **PA, AL, IL, MS have $0 state tax on Roth conversions** — confirmed with legal citations. `STATE_TAX_RATES` and `STATE_TAX_NOTES` reflect conversion-specific rates, not general income tax rates.
- **`totalFederalTaxOnConversions`** = sum of `y.conversionTax` (incremental marginal delta per year). **`totalFederalTaxOnly`** = sum of `y.federalTax` (gross federal on all income). Both are in summary.
- **2025 brackets + standard deductions:** Single $15,000 / MFJ $30,000 (IRS Rev. Proc. 2024-40). These are now in the engine; test file updated to match.

## Accomplished

### ✅ Completed
- **`simulationEngine.js`** — full engine with:
  - `calcFederalTax`, `calcTaxRates` (2025 brackets, IRS Rev. Proc. 2024-40)
  - `STATE_TAX_RATES` (51 entries, conversion-specific — PA/AL/IL/MS = 0), `STATE_TAX_NOTES` (legal citations + warnings)
  - `calcIRMAA` (age-aware, 2-year lookback), `calcIRMAARaw` (simple tier lookup)
  - `calcRMD`, `rmdStartAge` (SECURE 2.0: born ≤1950→72, 1951–1959→73, ≥1960→75)
  - `calcSSTax` (IRS provisional income formula, 0%/50%/85% inclusion)
  - `runSimulation` — conversion window only, year-by-year, with:
    - `stateTaxRate` threading (fed + state paid from brokerage)
    - `totalRothDeposited` in summary (actual $ into Roth, reduced only when brokerage exhausted + toggle ON)
    - RMD projection forward-compounded to `rmdAge` after loop end (bug fixed)
    - Per-year: `federalTax`, `stateTax`, `totalFederalTax`, `conversionTax` all separate
  - `runLifetimeProjection` — age→endAge full retirement model (RMDs, SS taxation, estate value, `stateTaxRate`, `conversionSchedule`)
  - `bracketFillSchedule` — internal helper, `stateTaxRate` threaded, ceiling bug fixed
  - `optimizeConversion` — tests bracket ceilings (fixed) + IRMAA boundaries + equal-spread; scores by lifetime cost
  - `calcBreakEven` — year-by-year Roth vs do-nothing estate comparison, returns break-even age
  - `calcSSClaimingComparison` — 4 scenarios (claim at 62/65/67/70) with SSA bend-point factors

- **`src/App.jsx`** — five-tab React dashboard:
  - **📊 Manual Simulator**: inputs panel (with state tax dropdown grouped by exemption status) + "Total Tax Cost" card (incremental conv. tax primary, gross household tax as subtitle) + 4 charts + year-by-year table with "Incr. Conv. Tax" + "Gross Fed Tax" columns + reconciliation note + ⬇ Export CSV
  - **🎯 Bracket Comparison**: bracket selector chips with ★ BEST, summary table, stacked lifetime cost bar chart, estate value bar chart, drill-down charts, year-by-year table; "Apply" button
  - **⚡ Optimize for Me**: ranked strategy table, lifetime income streams chart, MAGI/SS chart, estate chart, full year-by-year table with phase color coding; "Apply" button
  - **📈 Break-Even**: hero banner with break-even age, net worth comparison line chart, Roth-advantage area chart (green/red), year-by-year table + ⬇ Export CSV
  - **🔵 SS Claiming Age**: claiming age chips (62/65/67/70), comparison table, estate bar chart with `domain={['dataMin - 100000', 'dataMax + 100000']}`, SS received vs cost bar chart, lifetime SS/MAGI line chart, estate area chart
  - Shared `baseInputs` object used by all `useMemo` + panels
  - State tax dropdown: three `<optgroup>` sections (no tax / conversion exempt / taxed), dynamic color-coded note card below with legal citation

- **`test.mjs`** — 29-test validation suite (**all passing**):
  - Federal tax spot checks (updated to 2025 values)
  - IRMAA raw tier checks + age-aware lookback
  - SECURE 2.0 `rmdStartAge` boundary tests
  - `calcRMD` with all 3 birth-year cohorts
  - Single filer IRMAA breach detection

### Key exports from `simulationEngine.js`
| Export | Description |
|---|---|
| `runSimulation(inputs)` | Conversion window only — Manual tab |
| `runLifetimeProjection(inputs)` | Full age→endAge — Optimize + Bracket tabs |
| `optimizeConversion(inputs, opts)` | Bracket-fill optimizer |
| `calcBreakEven(inputs)` | Break-even age vs do-nothing baseline |
| `calcSSClaimingComparison(inputs)` | 4-scenario SS claiming age comparison |
| `calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus)` | 2025 federal tax |
| `calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiTwoYearsAgo)` | Age-aware IRMAA with 2-yr lookback |
| `calcIRMAARaw(magi, filingStatus)` | Simple IRMAA tier lookup |
| `calcRMD(balance, age, birthYear)` | RMD with SECURE 2.0 start age |
| `rmdStartAge(birthYear)` | Returns 72/73/75 per SECURE 2.0 |
| `calcSSTax(ssBenefit, otherAGI, filingStatus)` | SS provisional income taxation |
| `STATE_TAX_RATES` | 51-entry map of conversion-specific state rates |
| `STATE_TAX_NOTES` | Plain-English notes + legal citations per state |

### Key `runSimulation` summary fields
| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `totalFederalTaxOnConversions` | **Incremental** marginal tax caused by conversion (= Tax Cost card primary) |
| `totalFederalTaxOnly` | **Gross** federal tax on all income (= sum of year-by-year "Gross Fed Tax" column) |
| `totalStateTaxOnConversions` | State tax incremental on conversion |
| `totalRothDeposited` | Actual $ deposited into Roth (reduced only when brokerage exhausted + toggle ON) |
| `projectedRMDAtRmdAge` | Forward-compounded RMD at rmdAge (fixed — no longer returns $0) |
| `baselineRMDAtRmdAge` | Baseline (no conversion) RMD at rmdAge |

## What was added

### 1. 📉 Real-Dollar (Inflation Adjustment) Toggle
- New **"Real dollars" checkbox** in the header toolbar (dark background, next to Print)
- When ON, a configurable inflation rate field appears (default **2.5%**)
- A **green info banner** appears below the header explaining the mode
- **Account balances** throughout the Manual tab are deflated — year-by-year table, Final Balances card, Account Balances chart — all using `(1 + inflationRate)^yearsOut`
- **Tax amounts are intentionally kept nominal** (your actual IRS bill is never inflation-adjusted), with clear column notes explaining this
- Final Balances card shows the nominal values as a subtitle when real-dollar mode is active
- Chart title updates to show the inflation rate used

### 2. 🖨 Print / PDF Export Button
- New **"🖨 Print / PDF"** button in the header toolbar
- Calls `window.print()` — works with browser's native Save as PDF
- A full [`@media print`](src/index.css) stylesheet in `index.css`:
  - Hides all buttons, selects, and interactive elements (`.no-print` class)
  - Shows a **print-only report header** with date, age, filing status, and IRA balance
  - Resets backgrounds to white, removes rounded corners
  - Makes tables readable with visible borders and compact font
  - Keeps charts on their own page with `break-inside: avoid`

### 3. 💬 Plain-English Optimizer Narrative
- New `generateNarrative()` function that reads the optimizer's actual output numbers and produces a **human-readable paragraph** explaining the recommended strategy
- Appears as a **"Why This Strategy Wins"** card in the ⚡ Optimize tab, between the recommended banner and the bracket comparison table
- Explains: the RMD reduction rationale, IRMAA acceptance tradeoff, SS provisional income impact, savings vs. worst strategy, and projected estate improvement — all populated from live calculation results

  const allCeilings = [...namedCeilings, ...equalSpreadCeilings];
  const strategies  = [];
  const seen        = new Set();

  // ── Helper: build a full strategy entry from a bracketFillSchedule result ──
  function buildStrategy(result, label, bracketRate, ceiling, extraProps = {}) {
    const maxTierHit = Math.max(...result.schedule.map(s => s.irmaaTier), 0);
    const lifetimeResult = runLifetimeProjection({
      ...inputs,
      annualRothConversion: result.schedule[0]?.conversion ?? 0,
      conversionYears:      result.yearsUsed,
      conversionSchedule:   result.schedule,
      endAge,
    });
    return {
      label,
      bracketRate:          bracketRate ?? null,
      ceiling:              ceiling ?? 0,
      ceilingLabel:         ceilingLabel(ceiling ?? 0, filingStatus, otherOrdinaryIncome, stdDed),
      conversionYears:      result.yearsUsed,
      typicalAnnualAmount:  result.schedule[0]?.conversion ?? 0,
      schedule:             result.schedule,
      maxMarginalRate:      result.maxMarginalRate,
      // Conversion-window costs
      conversionTax:        result.totalTax,
      conversionIRMAA:      result.totalIRMAA,
      conversionSSTax:      result.totalSSTax,
      conversionCost:       result.totalCost,
      remainingTradIRA:     result.remainingTradIRA,
      finalRothIRA:         result.finalRothIRA,
      // Lifetime costs
      lifetimeTax:          lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeTax,
      lifetimeIRMAA:        lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeIRMAA,
      lifetimeCost:         lifetimeResult.summary.totalLifetimeCost,
      lifetimeSSTax:        lifetimeResult.summary.totalTaxableSS,
      estateValue:          lifetimeResult.summary.estateValueAtEndAge,
      lifetimeYears:        lifetimeResult.years,
      maxIRMAATier:         maxTierHit,
      irmaaBreachYears:     result.schedule.filter(s => s.irmaaTier > 0).length,
      ...extraProps,
    };
  }

  for (const { ceiling, label, bracketRate } of allCeilings) {
    const key = String(ceiling);
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);

    const result = bracketFillSchedule(inputs, ceiling, maxYears);
    if (result.yearsUsed === 0) continue;

    const maxTierHit = Math.max(...result.schedule.map(s => s.irmaaTier), 0);
    if (maxTierHit > maxIRMAATier) continue;

    // ── maxTaxBracket filter: skip any strategy that pushes marginal rate above cap ──
    if (maxTaxBracket != null && result.maxMarginalRate > maxTaxBracket + 0.001) continue;

    strategies.push(buildStrategy(result, label, bracketRate, ceiling));
  }

  // ── RMD Taming strategies ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Goal: only convert the IRA balance that would cause RMDs above a target
  // income threshold. Generates 1–3 partial conversion strategies at different
  // bracket caps (12%, 22%, 24%) so the optimizer can score them fairly.
  if (rmdIncomeTarget != null && rmdIncomeTarget > 0) {
    const tamingStrategies = calcRmdTamingCandidates(
      inputs, rmdIncomeTarget, maxYears, maxIRMAATier, maxTaxBracket, endAge, buildStrategy
    );
    for (const s of tamingStrategies) {
      const key = `rmd-tame-${s.ceiling}`;
      if (!seen.has(key)) {
        seen.add(key);
        strategies.push(s);
      }
    }
  }

  if (strategies.length === 0) {
    const bracketMsg = maxTaxBracket != null
      ? ` (bracket cap ${(maxTaxBracket * 100).toFixed(0)}%)`
      : "";
    throw new Error(
      `No valid conversion strategy found${bracketMsg}. Try relaxing the Max Conversion Tax Bracket or IRMAA tier constraint.`
    );
  }

  // Sort by lifetime cost (tax + IRMAA over full retirement)
  strategies.sort((a, b) => a.lifetimeCost - b.lifetimeCost);

  // bracketComparison: one entry per named bracket ceiling (for side-by-side view)
  // Exclude RMD-taming entries from the comparison bar chart (different goal).
  const bracketComparison = namedCeilings
    .map(nc => strategies.find(s => s.ceiling === nc.ceiling && !s.isRmdTaming))
    .filter(Boolean);

  return {
    strategies,
    recommended: strategies[0],
    bracketComparison,
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// RMD Taming: partial conversion to cap future RMDs
//
// Rather than draining the IRA entirely, compute the maximum IRA balance at
// rmdAge that keeps the annual RMD below (rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome), then
// discount that target balance back to today.  Convert only the excess.
//
// Returns an array of strategy objects (one per tested bracket ceiling) that
// use partial conversions spread evenly over the available gap years.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * @param {object}   inputs           Same shape as optimizeConversion inputs
 * @param {number}   rmdIncomeTarget  Desired max ordinary income at RMD age
 *                                    (e.g. top of 24% bracket: 394,600 MFJ)
 * @param {number}   maxYears         Gap years available before RMD age
 * @param {number}   maxIRMAATier     Max IRMAA tier allowed (from optimizer opts)
 * @param {number|null} maxTaxBracket Marginal rate cap (or null = no cap)
 * @param {number}   endAge           Lifetime projection end age
 * @param {Function} buildStrategy    Shared helper from optimizeConversion
 * @returns {Array}  Array of strategy entries (may be empty)
 */
function calcRmdTamingCandidates(
  inputs, rmdIncomeTarget, maxYears, maxIRMAATier, maxTaxBracket, endAge, buildStrategy
) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear  = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
  } = inputs;

  const rmdAge    = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const stdDed    = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const gapYears  = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge);

  // Step 1: find the IRS ULT divisor at rmdAge — sets how big the RMD is
  const divisor = ULT[Math.min(rmdAge, 90)] ?? 26.5;

  // Step 2: target IRA balance at rmdAge such that RMD ≤ (rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome)
  // RMD = balance / divisor  →  balance = RMD × divisor
  // We want  RMD + otherIncome ≤ rmdIncomeTarget
  //        → RMD ≤ rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome
  //        → balance ≤ (rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome) × divisor
  const maxRmd           = Math.max(0, rmdIncomeTarget - otherOrdinaryIncome);
  const targetBalAtRmd   = round2(maxRmd * divisor);

  // Step 3: discount the target balance back to today (end of conversion window = rmdAge)
  // The IRA will grow at growthRate for gapYears from NOW to rmdAge.
  // neededBalanceNow × (1+g)^gapYears = targetBalAtRmd
  // → neededBalanceNow = targetBalAtRmd / (1+g)^gapYears
  const growthFactor     = Math.pow(1 + growthRate, gapYears);
  const neededBalNow     = round2(targetBalAtRmd / growthFactor);

  // Step 4: excess to convert = current balance - needed balance (must be > 0)
  const excessToConvert  = Math.max(0, traditionalIRABalance - neededBalNow);
  if (excessToConvert < 1000) return []; // nothing meaningful to convert

  // Step 5: test several per-year conversion amounts:
  //   a) Spread the excess evenly over all gap years
  //   b) Concentrate it into the low-bracket portion only (bracket-limited sub-spreads)
  const testAmounts = [
    { amount: Math.ceil(excessToConvert / gapYears), label: "even spread" },
    { amount: Math.ceil(excessToConvert / Math.max(1, Math.ceil(gapYears * 0.5))), label: "half-period spread" },
  ];

  const results = [];

  for (const { amount, label } of testAmounts) {
    // Build a flat-rate schedule at `amount` per year for up to gapYears
    // using bracketFillSchedule with that amount as the per-year ceiling.
    const ceiling = Math.floor(otherOrdinaryIncome + amount);
    const sched   = bracketFillSchedule(inputs, ceiling, gapYears);
    if (sched.yearsUsed === 0) continue;

    const maxTierHit = Math.max(...sched.schedule.map(s => s.irmaaTier), 0);
    if (maxTierHit > maxIRMAATier) continue;
    if (maxTaxBracket != null && sched.maxMarginalRate > maxTaxBracket + 0.001) continue;

    const rmdAtTarget = round2(targetBalAtRmd / divisor);
    const entry = buildStrategy(
      sched,
      `RMD Taming — ${label} (target RMD ≤ ${fmt_$$(rmdAtTarget)}/yr at ${rmdAge})`,
      null,
      ceiling,
      {
        isRmdTaming:     true,
        targetBalAtRmd,
        neededBalNow,
        excessToConvert: round2(excessToConvert),
        rmdAtTarget,
        rmdTamingLabel:  `Partial conversion to keep RMD ≤ ${fmt_$$(rmdAtTarget)}/yr at age ${rmdAge}`,
      }
    );
    results.push(entry);
  }

  return results;
}

/** Minimal dollar formatter used inside the engine (no React dependency). */
function fmt_$$(n) {
  return "$" + Math.round(n).toLocaleString("en-US");
}

                    </span>

                        fontSize: 12, color: "#92400e", background: "#fef3c7",

                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* ── RMD Taming ── */}
                  <div style={{
                    display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6,
                    background: rmdTamingEnabled ? "#eff6ff" : "#f7f8fa",
                    border: `1px solid ${rmdTamingEnabled ? "#93c5fd" : "#e5e7eb"}`,

                    <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 8 }}>

                        id="rmdTaming"

                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {rmdTamingEnabled && (
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#1e40af", marginTop: 2, paddingLeft: 22 }}>
                        The optimizer will calculate how much of your Traditional IRA would generate an RMD above the threshold at age {birthYear <= 1950 ? 72 : birthYear <= 1959 ? 73 : 75}, discount it back to today, and only convert the excess.
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* ── IRMAA Tolerance ── */}
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                    <label style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}>IRMAA Tolerance</label>
                    <select value={maxIRMAATier} onChange={e => setMaxIRMAATier(Number(e.target.value))}

                    </span>
                  </div>

                </div>

### 🤖 Assistant

> 🔧 called: execute_command

The 24% MFJ bracket ceiling of $394,600 combined with the IRS age-75 ULT divisor of **24.6** gives a target IRA balance of **$8.97M** — which is bigger than even a $3M IRA. The math is always going to show `excess = 0` for this bracket + age combination because the goal is really to prevent RMDs from being *taxed at 24%+*, not to prevent any RMD.

The issue is my target formula. The correct interpretation of "RMD Taming" is: keep `RMD + otherIncome` **below the 24% bracket ceiling** — i.e., RMD alone should be at most `bracketTop - otherIncome - standardDeduction`. Let me re-examine:

The 24% bracket for MFJ has **taxable income** up to $394,600. The RMD is included in **taxable income after the standard deduction**. So:
- `grossIncome = otherIncome + RMD`
- `taxableIncome = grossIncome - stdDed`
- We want `taxableIncome ≤ bracketTop24` → `grossIncome ≤ bracketTop24 + stdDed`
- → `RMD ≤ bracketTop24 + stdDed - otherIncome`

For MFJ 2025: `bracketTop24 = 394,600`, `stdDed = 30,000`, `otherIncome = 30,000`:
`maxRmd = 394,600 + 30,000 - 30,000 = 394,600`

That's the MAGI ceiling, not the RMD cap. The right formula uses MAGI ceiling minus otherIncome:
`maxRmd = magiCeiling - otherIncome` where `magiCeiling = bracketTop24 + stdDed`

So `maxRmd = 394,600 + 30,000 - 30,000 = 394,600` which is still ~$9.7M target balance. The real problem is that the **rmdIncomeTarget I pass should be the MAGI ceiling** (gross income), not just the bracket top. And the UI is passing `bracketTops[filing]` which is just the bracket edge, not gross income.

The fix: `rmdIncomeTarget` in the engine should be interpreted as the **max MAGI** (gross income including standard deduction room), so `maxRmd = rmdIncomeTarget - otherOrdinaryIncome`. I should update the UI to pass `bracketTop + stdDed` not just `bracketTop`. But even then, with a 24% bracket MAGI ceiling of `394,600 + 30,000 = 424,600` for MFJ, `maxRmd = 424,600 - 30,000 = 394,600`. The target balance is still ~$9.7M.

The real insight: **for this feature to be meaningful, the user needs a very large IRA** (>$4M) OR a **lower target bracket** (e.g. 22%). Let me verify with the 22% bracket:

### 🤖 Assistant

Here's a precise summary of every change made:

---

## What was implemented

### `simulationEngine.js`

**1. `bracketFillSchedule` — track peak marginal rate**
- Each schedule row now records `marginalRate` (via the existing `calcTaxRates` call)
- The result object now includes `maxMarginalRate` — the highest marginal rate hit across all conversion years in that schedule

**2. `optimizeConversion` — new `opts` parameters**
| Parameter | Type | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| `maxTaxBracket` | `number \| null` | Skips any strategy whose `maxMarginalRate > maxTaxBracket`. Tolerates floating-point fuzz (`+0.001`). |
| `rmdIncomeTarget` | `number \| null` | When set, calls `calcRmdTamingCandidates` and injects partial-conversion strategies |

- Refactored the per-strategy construction into an inner `buildStrategy()` helper (shared by both regular candidates and RMD-taming candidates)
- RMD-taming entries are excluded from the `bracketComparison` bar-chart data (they have a different goal and would pollute the chart)
- Error message now includes the bracket cap in the message when no strategies survive filtering

**3. `calcRmdTamingCandidates` — new private function**

The math in one line: `excessToConvert = max(0, tradIRA - (maxRmd × ULT[rmdAge]) / (1+g)^gapYears)`

Step by step:
1. Look up the IRS Uniform Lifetime Table divisor at `rmdAge` 
2. Compute `maxRmd = rmdIncomeTarget − otherIncome` (how large the RMD can be before exceeding the ceiling)
3. Compute `targetBalAtRmd = maxRmd × divisor` (the IRA balance that generates exactly that RMD)
4. Discount to today: `neededBalNow = targetBalAtRmd / (1+g)^gapYears`
5. `excessToConvert = max(0, currentIRA − neededBalNow)` — only convert the overhang
6. Tests two spread schedules (even spread over all gap years; concentrated into half the period) and runs `bracketFillSchedule` on each

Each resulting strategy entry carries `isRmdTaming=true`, `targetBalAtRmd`, `neededBalNow`, `excessToConvert`, `rmdAtTarget`, and `rmdTamingLabel`.

**4. `fmt_$$` — tiny internal helper** for dollar formatting inside the engine (no React dependency)

---

### `src/App.jsx`

**Optimizer sidebar — three new controls, grouped together:**

| Control | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Max Conversion Tax Bracket** dropdown | "No limit / Cap at 12% / 22% / 24% / 32%" — passes `maxTaxBracket` to optimizer |
| **🎯 Target RMD Taming** checkbox | When checked, computes the 24% MAGI ceiling (`bracketTop + stdDed`) and passes it as `rmdIncomeTarget`. Shows a contextual blue note with the RMD-age and threshold info. |
| **IRMAA Tolerance** (existing, now third in group) | Unchanged, just re-ordered below the new controls |

**A caution banner** appears under the bracket cap dropdown when a cap is selected, reminding the user that leaving a large IRA may produce heavy RMDs and suggesting they enable RMD Taming.

**OptimizerPanel strategy table** — two visual additions:
- New **"Peak Rate"** column showing the highest marginal rate hit during conversions (green ≤22%, amber ≤24%, red ≥32%)
- 🎯 **`RMD TAMING` badge** on every taming row (light blue), with a sub-line showing the target RMD amount and the partial-conversion note

**Recommended banner** — dynamically adapts:
- Blue gradient + `🎯 RMD TAMING` badge when the recommended strategy is a taming entry
- "Peak rate" pill badge (color-coded) next to the title
- RMD-taming callout box showing `targetBalAtRmd`, `neededBalNow`, and `excessToConvert` when a taming strategy is recommended

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pure helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Calculate federal income tax owed for a given taxable income.
 * @param {number} taxableIncome
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus) {
  if (taxableIncome <= 0) return 0;
  const brackets = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  let tax = 0;
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (taxableIncome <= b.min) break;
    tax += (Math.min(taxableIncome, b.max) - b.min) * b.rate;
  }
  return tax;
}

/**
 * Derive effective and marginal tax rates from taxable income.
 * @param {number} taxableIncome
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @returns {{ effectiveRate: number, marginalRate: number }}
 */
export function calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus) {
  if (taxableIncome <= 0) return { effectiveRate: 0, marginalRate: 0 };
  const tax = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus);
  const effectiveRate = tax / taxableIncome;
  let marginalRate = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus][0].rate;
  for (const b of TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus]) {
    if (taxableIncome > b.min) marginalRate = b.rate;
  }
  return { effectiveRate, marginalRate };
}
=======
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bracket inflation helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Return a copy of the tax bracket array for a given filing status with
 * thresholds compounded by (1 + inflationRate)^yearIndex.
 * yearIndex = 0 (or inflationRate = 0) returns the original 2025 values.
 */
function scaledBrackets(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex) {
  const factor = Math.pow(1 + (inflationRate ?? 0), yearIndex ?? 0);
  if (factor === 1) return TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus]; // fast path
  return TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus].map(b => ({
    rate: b.rate,
    min:  b.min  === 0        ? 0        : b.min  * factor,
    max:  b.max  === Infinity ? Infinity : b.max  * factor,
  }));
}

/**
 * Return the standard deduction scaled by inflation.
 */
function scaledStdDed(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex) {
  const factor = Math.pow(1 + (inflationRate ?? 0), yearIndex ?? 0);
  return STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus] * factor;
}

/**
 * Return a copy of the IRMAA tiers with maxMAGI scaled by inflation.
 */
function scaledIrmaaTiers(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex) {
  const factor = Math.pow(1 + (inflationRate ?? 0), yearIndex ?? 0);
  if (factor === 1) return IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus]; // fast path
  return IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus].map(t => ({
    ...t,
    maxMAGI: t.maxMAGI === Infinity ? Infinity : t.maxMAGI * factor,
  }));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pure helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Calculate federal income tax owed for a given taxable income.
 * @param {number} taxableIncome
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @param {number} [inflationRate=0]  Annual bracket inflation rate (0–1)
 * @param {number} [yearIndex=0]      Number of years from base year (2025)
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus, inflationRate = 0, yearIndex = 0) {
  if (taxableIncome <= 0) return 0;
  const brackets = scaledBrackets(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex);
  let tax = 0;
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (taxableIncome <= b.min) break;
    tax += (Math.min(taxableIncome, b.max) - b.min) * b.rate;
  }
  return tax;
}

/**
 * Derive effective and marginal tax rates from taxable income.
 * @param {number} taxableIncome
 * @param {"single"|"mfj"} filingStatus
 * @param {number} [inflationRate=0]
 * @param {number} [yearIndex=0]
 * @returns {{ effectiveRate: number, marginalRate: number }}
 */
export function calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus, inflationRate = 0, yearIndex = 0) {
  if (taxableIncome <= 0) return { effectiveRate: 0, marginalRate: 0 };
  const brackets = scaledBrackets(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex);
  const tax = calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex);
  const effectiveRate = tax / taxableIncome;
  let marginalRate = brackets[0].rate;
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (taxableIncome > b.min) marginalRate = b.rate;
  }
  return { effectiveRate, marginalRate };
}
