export function calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age = 65, magiTwoYearsAgo = null) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;

  function tierForMagi(m) {
    if (m == null || m <= 0) return { ...NO_IRMAA, onMedicare: true };
    for (const t of tiers) {
      if (m <= t.maxMAGI) {
        return {
          tier: t.tier, label: t.label,
          partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
          annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
          crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0, onMedicare: true,
        };
      }
    }
    const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
    return {
      tier: last.tier, label: last.label,
      partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
      annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
      crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,
    };
  }

  // Before Medicare eligibility — no IRMAA at all
  if (age < 65) {
    return {
      currentIRMAA: { ...NO_IRMAA },
      futureIRMAA:  tierForMagi(magi), // preview: this income will affect IRMAA at age 65+
    };
  }

  // On Medicare: current year's surcharge is driven by 2-years-ago MAGI
  const currentIRMAA = tierForMagi(magiTwoYearsAgo);
  // Future preview: this year's MAGI will drive IRMAA two years from now
  const futureIRMAA  = tierForMagi(magi);

  return { currentIRMAA, futureIRMAA };
}

/**
 * Backwards-compatible single-value wrapper used in tests and simple lookups.
 * Returns the IRMAA tier for a given MAGI regardless of age (no lookback).
 */
export function calcIRMAARaw(magi, filingStatus) {
  const tiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];
  const basePremium = tiers[0].partBMonthly;
  for (const t of tiers) {
    if (magi <= t.maxMAGI) {
      return {
        tier: t.tier, label: t.label,
        partBMonthly: t.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: t.partDMonthly,
        annualSurcharge: round2((t.partBMonthly - basePremium + t.partDMonthly) * 12),
        crossesThreshold: t.tier > 0, onMedicare: true,
      };
    }
  }
  const last = tiers[tiers.length - 1];
  return {
    tier: last.tier, label: last.label,
    partBMonthly: last.partBMonthly, partDMonthly: last.partDMonthly,
    annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
    crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,
  };
}

      annualSurcharge: round2((last.partBMonthly - basePremium + last.partDMonthly) * 12),
      crossesThreshold: true, onMedicare: true,

function bracketFillSchedule(baseInputs, magiCeiling, maxYears) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
    stateTaxRate = 0,
  } = baseInputs;

  const stdDed = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;

  const schedule     = [];
  const magiHistoryB = [null, null]; // 2-year MAGI lookback
  let totalTax       = 0;
  let totalIRMAA     = 0;
  let totalSSTax     = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxYears; i++) {
    if (tradIRA <= 0) break;
    const age = currentAge + i;
    const ss  = age >= ssStartAge ? socialSecurityBenefit : 0;

    // How much headroom do we have under the MAGI ceiling?
    // First estimate SS taxability at this ceiling (iterative for accuracy)
    // Simple approach: assume SS taxability at 85% for the headroom calc,
    // then correct after computing actual conversion.
    const headroom     = Math.max(0, magiCeiling - otherOrdinaryIncome);
    const conversion   = Math.min(headroom, tradIRA);
    if (conversion <= 0) break;

    // Recalculate SS tax with actual conversion
    const nonSSIncome  = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversion;
    const { taxableSS } = calcSSTax(ss, nonSSIncome, filingStatus);
    const grossIncome  = nonSSIncome + taxableSS;
    const taxableInc   = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDed);
    const tax          = calcFederalTax(taxableInc, filingStatus) + grossIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistoryB[0]);
    magiHistoryB[0] = magiHistoryB[1];
    magiHistoryB[1] = magi;

    const taxPaid = Math.min(tax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaid;
    const shortfall = tax - taxPaid;

    tradIRA  -= conversion;
    tradIRA   = Math.max(0, tradIRA);
    const deposit = payTaxFromConversion ? Math.max(0, conversion - shortfall) : conversion;
    rothIRA  += deposit;

    tradIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage  = Math.max(0, taxBrokerage) * (1 + growthRate);

    totalTax   += tax;
    totalIRMAA += irmaa.annualSurcharge;
    totalSSTax += taxableSS;

    const { marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableInc, filingStatus);

function bracketFillSchedule(baseInputs, magiCeiling, maxYears) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    taxableBrokerageBalance,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    payTaxFromConversion = false,
    stateTaxRate = 0,
    inflationRate = 0,
  } = baseInputs;

  let tradIRA      = traditionalIRABalance;
  let rothIRA      = 0;
  let taxBrokerage = taxableBrokerageBalance;

  const schedule     = [];
  const magiHistoryB = [null, null]; // 2-year MAGI lookback
  let totalTax       = 0;
  let totalIRMAA     = 0;
  let totalSSTax     = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxYears; i++) {
    if (tradIRA <= 0) break;
    const age = currentAge + i;
    const ss  = age >= ssStartAge ? socialSecurityBenefit : 0;

    // Inflation-scaled deduction for this year
    const stdDed = scaledStdDed(filingStatus, inflationRate, i);

    // ── Headroom calculation with SS circularity fix ──────────────────────────
    // Problem: taxable SS increases as conversion increases (provisional income
    // rises), so a static headroom = ceiling - otherIncome overshoots the
    // ceiling once taxableSS is added back to grossIncome.
    //
    // Fix: start with the static estimate, then iteratively reduce the
    // conversion until grossIncome (incl. actual taxableSS) ≤ magiCeiling.
    // This converges in ≤ 5 iterations because taxableSS is monotone in conv.
    let conversion = Math.min(Math.max(0, magiCeiling - otherOrdinaryIncome), tradIRA);
    if (conversion <= 0) break;

    if (ss > 0) {
      for (let iter = 0; iter < 6; iter++) {
        const { taxableSS: tss } = calcSSTax(ss, otherOrdinaryIncome + conversion, filingStatus);
        const gross = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversion + tss;
        if (gross <= magiCeiling + 0.5) break; // within $0.50 rounding tolerance
        // Reduce conversion by the overshoot (tss added to gross)
        conversion = Math.max(0, conversion - (gross - magiCeiling));
      }
      conversion = Math.min(conversion, tradIRA);
    }
    if (conversion <= 0) break;

    // Final income figures with the solved conversion amount
    const nonSSIncome  = otherOrdinaryIncome + conversion;
    const { taxableSS } = calcSSTax(ss, nonSSIncome, filingStatus);
    const grossIncome  = nonSSIncome + taxableSS;
    const taxableInc   = Math.max(0, grossIncome - stdDed);
    const tax          = calcFederalTax(taxableInc, filingStatus, inflationRate, i) + grossIncome * stateTaxRate;
    const magi = grossIncome;
    const { currentIRMAA: irmaa } = calcIRMAA(magi, filingStatus, age, magiHistoryB[0], inflationRate, i);
    magiHistoryB[0] = magiHistoryB[1];
    magiHistoryB[1] = magi;

    const taxPaid = Math.min(tax, taxBrokerage);
    taxBrokerage -= taxPaid;
    const shortfall = tax - taxPaid;

    tradIRA  -= conversion;
    tradIRA   = Math.max(0, tradIRA);
    const deposit = payTaxFromConversion ? Math.max(0, conversion - shortfall) : conversion;
    rothIRA  += deposit;

    tradIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    rothIRA      *= (1 + growthRate);
    taxBrokerage  = Math.max(0, taxBrokerage) * (1 + growthRate);

    totalTax   += tax;
    totalIRMAA += irmaa.annualSurcharge;
    totalSSTax += taxableSS;

    const { marginalRate } = calcTaxRates(taxableInc, filingStatus, inflationRate, i);

export function optimizeConversion(inputs, opts = {}) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
  } = inputs;

  const {
    maxIRMAATier  = 0,
    endAge        = 90,
    // maxTaxBracket: optional marginal-rate ceiling (e.g. 0.22, 0.24).
    // Any candidate strategy whose peak marginal rate exceeds this is excluded.
    // null / undefined / "none" = no bracket cap (default behaviour).
    maxTaxBracket = null,
    // rmdIncomeTarget: if set, generates a "RMD Taming" strategy whose goal is
    // keeping the projected RMD at rmdAge below this income threshold rather
    // than draining the IRA completely.
    rmdIncomeTarget = null,
  } = opts;

  const rmdAge   = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const maxYears = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge);
  const stdDed   = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const brackets = TAX_BRACKETS[filingStatus];
  const irmaaTiers = IRMAA_TIERS[filingStatus];

  // Named bracket ceilings.
  // The MAGI ceiling that keeps taxable income at the top of bracket b is:

  const namedCeilings = [];

    namedCeilings.push({

      label:   `Fill to top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket`,
      bracketRate: b.rate,

    if (t.maxMAGI === Infinity) continue;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: t.maxMAGI,
      label:   `Stay at/below IRMAA ${t.label} threshold ($${t.maxMAGI.toLocaleString()})`,

export function optimizeConversion(inputs, opts = {}) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,
    socialSecurityBenefit = 0,
    ssStartAge = 67,
    inflationRate = 0,
  } = inputs;

  const {
    maxIRMAATier  = 0,
    endAge        = 90,
    maxTaxBracket = null,
    // rmdIncomeTarget: MAGI ceiling approach (legacy, still supported)
    rmdIncomeTarget = null,
    // targetMaxRMD: direct dollar amount of desired max annual RMD (new, preferred).
    // When set, generates a "RMD Taming" strategy whose goal is keeping the
    // projected RMD at rmdAge ≤ targetMaxRMD.
    targetMaxRMD = null,
  } = opts;

  const rmdAge   = rmdStartAge(birthYear);
  const maxYears = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge);

  // Use year-1 (yearIndex=0) brackets/thresholds as the static ceiling basis for
  // optimizer candidates.  The actual year-by-year simulation inside
  // bracketFillSchedule will use the correct per-year inflation factor.
  const stdDed     = scaledStdDed(filingStatus, inflationRate, 0);
  const brackets   = scaledBrackets(filingStatus, inflationRate, 0);
  const irmaaTiers = scaledIrmaaTiers(filingStatus, inflationRate, 0);

  // Named bracket ceilings (based on year-0 / current-year brackets).
  // magiCeiling = b.max + stdDed  (otherIncome cancels in bracketFillSchedule headroom calc)
  const namedCeilings = [];
  for (const b of brackets) {
    if (b.max === Infinity) continue;
    const magiCeiling = b.max + stdDed;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: Math.floor(magiCeiling),
      label:   `Fill to top of ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% bracket`,
      bracketRate: b.rate,
    });
  }
  // IRMAA tier boundaries (inflation-adjusted year-0 values)
  for (const t of irmaaTiers) {
    if (t.maxMAGI === Infinity) continue;
    namedCeilings.push({
      ceiling: Math.floor(t.maxMAGI),
      label:   `Stay at/below IRMAA ${t.label} threshold (${fmt_$$(t.maxMAGI)} MAGI)`,
      bracketRate: null,
    });
  }

function calcRmdTamingCandidates(
  inputs, rmdIncomeTarget, maxYears, maxIRMAATier, maxTaxBracket, endAge, buildStrategy
) {
  const {
    traditionalIRABalance,
    growthRate,
    filingStatus,
    currentAge = 60,
    birthYear  = new Date().getFullYear() - currentAge,
    otherOrdinaryIncome = 0,

  const stdDed    = STANDARD_DEDUCTION[filingStatus];
  const gapYears  = Math.max(1, rmdAge - currentAge);

  // Step 1: find the IRS ULT divisor at rmdAge — sets how big the RMD is
  const divisor = ULT[Math.min(rmdAge, 90)] ?? 26.5;

  // Step 2: target IRA balance at rmdAge such that RMD ≤ (rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome)
  // RMD = balance / divisor  →  balance = RMD × divisor
  // We want  RMD + otherIncome ≤ rmdIncomeTarget
  //        → RMD ≤ rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome
  //        → balance ≤ (rmdIncomeTarget - otherIncome) × divisor
  const maxRmd           = Math.max(0, rmdIncomeTarget - otherOrdinaryIncome);
  const targetBalAtRmd   = round2(maxRmd * divisor);

### 🤖 Assistant

Zero errors, only the expected chunk-size warning. Here's a precise summary of every change:

---

## What was changed

### `simulationEngine.js`

#### 1. New inflation scaling helpers (private)
Three new private functions between `rmdStartAge` and `calcFederalTax`:

| Function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| [`scaledBrackets(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex)`](simulationEngine.js) | Returns `TAX_BRACKETS[status]` with every `min`/`max` multiplied by `(1+r)^y`. Fast-path returns the original array when `factor === 1`. |
| [`scaledStdDed(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex)`](simulationEngine.js) | Returns `STANDARD_DEDUCTION[status] × (1+r)^y`. |
| [`scaledIrmaaTiers(filingStatus, inflationRate, yearIndex)`](simulationEngine.js) | Returns `IRMAA_TIERS[status]` with each `maxMAGI` scaled. Premium dollar amounts are deliberately **not** scaled (premiums are set by CMS annually, not tied to CPI). |

#### 2. Updated exported helpers
- **`calcFederalTax(taxableIncome, filingStatus, inflationRate=0, yearIndex=0)`** — uses `scaledBrackets`; backward-compatible (extra args default to 0)
- **`calcTaxRates(taxableIncome, filingStatus, inflationRate=0, yearIndex=0)`** — uses `scaledBrackets`; marginal-rate loop also uses scaled brackets
- **`calcIRMAA(..., inflationRate=0, yearIndex=0)`** — uses `scaledIrmaaTiers`; `basePremium` reads from the unscaled constant so surcharge arithmetic stays correct
- **`calcIRMAARaw(magi, filingStatus, inflationRate=0, yearIndex=0)`** — same inflation plumbing

#### 3. `runSimulation` — `inflationRate` threaded
- Accepts `inflationRate = 0` from inputs
- Per-year loop: `stdDeduction = scaledStdDed(…, i)`, then `calcFederalTax/calcTaxRates/calcIRMAA` all called with `(inflationRate, i)`

#### 4. `runLifetimeProjection` — `inflationRate` threaded
- Accepts `inflationRate = 0`; per-year `stdDed = scaledStdDed(…, yearIndex)`, all tax/IRMAA calls take `(inflationRate, yearIndex)`

#### 5. `bracketFillSchedule` — `inflationRate` threaded + **SS headroom circularity fix**
- Accepts `inflationRate = 0`; per-year `stdDed` is scaled
- **Bug fixed:** the old static `headroom = magiCeiling - otherOrdinaryIncome` over-converted when SS was non-zero because adding back `taxableSS` pushed `grossIncome` above the ceiling. New code:
  1. Start with the static estimate as an upper bound
  2. When `ss > 0`: iterate up to 6 times — compute actual `taxableSS`, measure overshot, subtract overshot from `conversion`, stop when `gross ≤ ceiling + $0.50`
  3. This always converges (taxableSS is monotone in conversion income)

#### 6. `optimizeConversion` — `inflationRate` + `targetMaxRMD`
- Accepts `inflationRate` from `inputs`

### 🤖 Assistant

Three ways to run it:

---

### 1. Development server (hot-reload, use this for active work)

```powershell
$env:PATH = "C:\Program Files\nodejs;$env:PATH"
npm run dev
```

Then open **http://localhost:5173** in your browser. The page live-reloads whenever you save a file.

---

### 2. Production preview (serves the optimized build)

```powershell
$env:PATH = "C:\Program Files\nodejs;$env:PATH"
npm run build       # compile once
npm run preview     # serve the dist/ folder
```

Opens at **http://localhost:4173**.

---

### 3. Run the engine tests (no browser needed)

```powershell
node test.mjs
```

Runs the 30-test validation suite directly in Node — no server required.

---

**If `npm` isn't found**, prepend Node to your PATH first (the `$env:PATH` line above). The dev server is the fastest way to see changes — just save [`src/App.jsx`](src/App.jsx) or [`simulationEngine.js`](simulationEngine.js) and the browser updates instantly.

              <tbody>
                {strategies.slice(0, 12).map((s, i) => {
                  // Re-crown BEST: the first strategy in the sorted list is always
                  // the cheapest overall. But when the user has applied a constraint
                  // (bracket cap or RMD taming), the constrained champion may not
                  // be at index 0 — it's the cheapest entry that satisfies the
                  // user's explicit constraint. We find that here so the badge
                  // reflects the user's actual intent rather than the global optimum.
                  const constraintActive = (baseInputs?.maxTaxBracket != null) || s.isRmdTaming;
                  const constrainedBestIdx = (() => {
                    // The constrained winner is the lowest-lifetimeCost strategy
                    // that matches the active constraint type.
                    // Since strategies are sorted by lifetimeCost, the first
                    // RMD-taming entry (if any taming entries exist) or the first
                    // bracket-capped entry is the constrained best.
                    const hasTaming = strategies.some(x => x.isRmdTaming);
                    if (hasTaming) {
                      return strategies.findIndex(x => x.isRmdTaming);
                    }
                    // No taming active — index 0 is always best
                    return 0;
                  })();
                  const isBest = i === constrainedBestIdx;
                  return (
                  <tr key={i} onClick={() => setSelectedIdx(i)}
                    style={{ background: i === selectedIdx ? "#eff6ff" : s.isRmdTaming ? "#f0fdf4" : i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                    <td style={{ ...td, textAlign: "left", maxWidth: 300, whiteSpace: "normal" }}>
                      {isBest && <span style={{ background: "#16a34a", color: "#fff", borderRadius: 3, padding: "1px 5px", fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, marginRight: 5 }}>BEST</span>}

### 🤖 Assistant

Your remote is `https://github.com/ccjung/roth-conversion`. Here are the exact commands to push everything:

```powershell
# 1. Stage the changed source files (skip node_modules)
git add Readme.md src/App.jsx simulationEngine.js test.mjs

# 2. Also stage the rebuilt dist/ output
git add dist/

# 3. Commit with a message
git commit -m "feat: UI polish items 1-5 + rewrite README"

# 4. Push to GitHub
git push origin main
```

**What each step does:**

| Step | What it does |
|---|---|
| `git add Readme.md src/App.jsx ...` | Stages only the files we changed — keeps `node_modules` out |
| `git add dist/` | Stages the new production build (old JS bundle deleted, new one added) |
| `git commit -m "..."` | Creates a snapshot with a descriptive message |
| `git push origin main` | Uploads the commit to `github.com/ccjung/roth-conversion` |

If GitHub prompts for credentials, use your GitHub username and a **Personal Access Token** (PAT) as the password — GitHub no longer accepts plain passwords over HTTPS. You can create one at [github.com → Settings → Developer settings → Personal access tokens](https://github.com/settings/tokens) with the `repo` scope checked.
